+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# Emulator helpers for checking the two-word search on a version 10 (FTS5) build.
+#
+#   search-check.sh <emulator-serial> <out-dir> seed
+#       Force-stops the app, pulls its database, applies seed-search-corpus.sql on the host
+#       (needs sqlite3 with FTS5 on PATH), runs the FTS5 integrity check and pushes the
+#       database back. Writes <out-dir>/seed.txt with the article and index row counts.
+#   search-check.sh <emulator-serial> <out-dir> expect [words...]
+#       Pulls the database and writes <out-dir>/expect.txt: the number of rows the app shows
+#       for the search (every live match, capped at 100) and the top 10 titles, best first by
+#       bm25, from the same query the app runs. The words default to "harbor lantern".
+#   search-check.sh <emulator-serial> <out-dir> pull
+#       Pulls the database and writes <out-dir>/pull.txt: the row count of every table and of
+#       the search index, after the drive.
+#
+# Every mode force-stops the app before it reads or writes the database. Only emulators are
+# accepted, and the app must be a debug build (run-as).
+set -euo pipefail
+export MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 # keep device paths intact under Git Bash on Windows
+
+usage() {
+  echo "usage: $0 <emulator-serial> <out-dir> seed|expect|pull [words...]" >&2
+  exit 2
+}
+
+[ $# -ge 3 ] || usage
+serial=$1 out=$2 mode=$3
+shift 3
+case "$serial" in
+  emulator-*) ;;
+  *) echo "refusing $serial: only an emulator serial (emulator-NNNN) is accepted" >&2; exit 2 ;;
+esac
+command -v sqlite3 >/dev/null || { echo "sqlite3 is not on PATH" >&2; exit 2; }
+
+pkg=com.jayteealao.trails
+here=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
+mkdir -p "$out"
+# Working copies live under <out-dir>: a native sqlite3 on Windows cannot open /tmp paths.
+work="$out/work"
+mkdir -p "$work"
+trap 'rm -rf "$work"' EXIT
+
+dev() { adb -s "$serial" "$@"; }
+db() { sqlite3 "$work/Pocket" "$@"; }
+
+pull_db() { # -> $work/Pocket, with the write-ahead log folded in
+  dev shell am force-stop "$pkg"
+  rm -f "$work"/Pocket*
+  for f in Pocket Pocket-wal Pocket-shm; do
+    if dev shell run-as "$pkg" ls "databases/$f" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
+      dev exec-out run-as "$pkg" cat "databases/$f" >"$work/$f"
+    fi
+  done
+  [ -s "$work/Pocket" ] || { echo "no database file on the device" >&2; exit 1; }
+  db "PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE);" >/dev/null
+  local version
+  version=$(db 'PRAGMA user_version;')
+  [ "$version" -ge 10 ] || { echo "schema version $version has no FTS5 search index; install a version 10 build" >&2; exit 1; }
+}
+
+push_db() {
+  dev push "$work/Pocket" /data/local/tmp/trails-search.db >/dev/null
+  dev shell chmod 644 /data/local/tmp/trails-search.db
+  dev shell run-as "$pkg" sh -c "'cat /data/local/tmp/trails-search.db > databases/Pocket && rm -f databases/Pocket-wal databases/Pocket-shm'"
+  dev shell rm -f /data/local/tmp/trails-search.db
+}
+
+counts() {
+  for t in article article_tags article_images article_authors article_videos local_archive DomainMetadata; do
+    echo "$t $(db "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t;")"
+  done
+  echo "live articles $(db 'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;')"
+  echo "search index rows $(db 'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article_fts_docsize;')"
+}
+
+case "$mode" in
+  seed)
+    pull_db
+    db <"$here/seed-search-corpus.sql"
+    # Raises an error (and stops the script) when the index does not match the articles.
+    db "INSERT INTO article_fts(article_fts, rank) VALUES ('integrity-check', 1);"
+    counts >"$out/seed.txt"
+    push_db
+    cat "$out/seed.txt"
+    ;;
+  expect)
+    [ $# -gt 0 ] || set -- harbor lantern
+    # The match string the app builds for plain words: each word quoted, joined with OR.
+    match=""
+    for w in "$@"; do
+      [ -z "$match" ] || match="$match OR "
+      match="$match\"${w//\"/\"\"}\""
+    done
+    sql_match=${match//\'/\'\'}
+    pull_db
+    from="FROM article_fts JOIN article ON article.rowid = article_fts.rowid WHERE article_fts MATCH '$sql_match' AND article.deleted_at IS NULL"
+    {
+      echo "match $match"
+      echo "shown $(db "SELECT MIN(COUNT(*), 100) $from;") of $(db "SELECT COUNT(*) $from;") live matches"
+      echo "deleted matches $(db "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article_fts JOIN article ON article.rowid = article_fts.rowid WHERE article_fts MATCH '$sql_match' AND article.deleted_at IS NOT NULL;") (never shown)"
+      echo "top 10 by bm25:"
+      db "SELECT article.title $from ORDER BY bm25(article_fts) LIMIT 10;"
+    } >"$out/expect.txt"
+    cat "$out/expect.txt"
+    ;;
+  pull)
+    pull_db
+    counts >"$out/pull.txt"
+    cat "$out/pull.txt"
+    ;;
+  *) usage ;;
+esac
diff --git a/android/tools/db-upgrade-check/seed-search-corpus.sql b/android/tools/db-upgrade-check/seed-search-corpus.sql
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..480fd94
--- /dev/null
+++ b/android/tools/db-upgrade-check/seed-search-corpus.sql
@@ -0,0 +1,37 @@
+-- A search corpus seeded into the database of a version 10 build on a device.
+-- 150 live articles with "lantern" in the text, 20 live articles with both "harbor" and
+-- "lantern" in the text, and 10 deleted articles with "harbor" in the title. A search for
+-- the two words matches 170 live articles, so the app shows its limit of 100.
+-- Every row is stamped as backed up (backed_up_at = timeUpdated), so no sync pushes it.
+-- The article_fts triggers that the app created index every row as it is inserted.
+BEGIN;
+WITH RECURSIVE n(i) AS (SELECT 1 UNION ALL SELECT i + 1 FROM n WHERE i < 180),
+rows AS (
+  SELECT
+    i,
+    CASE
+      WHEN i <= 150 THEN printf('search-lantern-%03d', i)
+      WHEN i <= 170 THEN printf('search-both-%03d', i - 150)
+      ELSE printf('search-deleted-%03d', i - 170)
+    END AS itemId,
+    CASE
+      WHEN i <= 150 THEN printf('Evening walk notes %d', i)
+      WHEN i <= 170 THEN printf('Quayside walk notes %d', i - 150)
+      ELSE printf('Harbor survey %d', i - 170)
+    END AS title,
+    CASE
+      -- A different number of filler words per row gives every row its own bm25 score,
+      -- so the best-first order has no ties.
+      WHEN i <= 150 THEN 'A lantern hung by the gate. ' || replace(hex(zeroblob(20 + i)), '00', 'hedge ')
+      WHEN i <= 170 THEN 'The harbor wall held a lantern for each boat. ' || replace(hex(zeroblob(i - 150)), '00', 'quay ')
+      ELSE 'Notes on the harbor and its tides.'
+    END AS body,
+    1770000000000 + i * 1000 AS t
+  FROM n
+)
+INSERT INTO article (itemId, title, givenTitle, url, givenUrl, wordCount, status, hasImage, hasVideo, hasAudio,
+  sortId, timeAdded, timeUpdated, timeFavorited, listenDurationEstimate, text, text_source, backed_up_at, deleted_at)
+SELECT itemId, title, title, 'https://example.com/' || itemId, 'https://example.com/' || itemId, 100, '0', 0, 0, 0,
+  i, t, t, 0, 0, body, 'seed', t, CASE WHEN i > 170 THEN t ELSE NULL END
+FROM rows;
+COMMIT;

    wip(android): turn typed words and quoted phrases into a search that matches any of them, with every typed symbol taken literally
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

diff --git a/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/search/FtsMatchQuery.kt b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/search/FtsMatchQuery.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..837c094
--- /dev/null
+++ b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/search/FtsMatchQuery.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,40 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.search
+
+/**
+ * Builds the FTS5 `MATCH` string from what the person typed.
+ *
+ * Every word and every quoted phrase becomes one quoted FTS5 string (an inner `"` is doubled),
+ * and the strings are joined with ` OR `, so an article matches any of them. A `"` at the start
+ * of a word opens a phrase when a later `"` closes it; any other `"` is a literal character.
+ * Because every term is a quoted string, no operator, column filter, prefix star or parenthesis
+ * from the input reaches the FTS5 parser, and no term is left unterminated.
+ */
+object FtsMatchQuery {
+
+    /** The match string, or null when the input holds no word or phrase. */
+    fun build(input: String): String? {
+        val terms = mutableListOf<String>()
+        var i = 0
+        while (i < input.length) {
+            val c = input[i]
+            if (c.isWhitespace()) {
+                i++
+                continue
+            }
+            val close = if (c == '"') input.indexOf('"', i + 1) else -1
+            if (close > 0) {
+                val phrase = input.substring(i + 1, close)
+                if (phrase.isNotBlank()) terms += quote(phrase)
+                i = close + 1
+            } else {
+                var end = i
+                while (end < input.length && !input[end].isWhitespace()) end++
+                terms += quote(input.substring(i, end))
+                i = end
+            }
+        }
+        return terms.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.joinToString(" OR ")
+    }
+
+    internal fun quote(term: String) = "\"" + term.replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\""
+}

diff --git a/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/FtsIndexCheckCallback.kt b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/FtsIndexCheckCallback.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..07a70e4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/FtsIndexCheckCallback.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,40 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database
+
+import androidx.room3.RoomDatabase
+import androidx.sqlite.SQLiteConnection
+import androidx.sqlite.execSQL
+import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
+import timber.log.Timber
+
+/**
+ * At every open, checks that the search index holds one row per article and rebuilds it
+ * from `article` when it does not (a missed backfill, or a rowid change the content
+ * triggers did not see).
+ *
+ * The indexed count comes from FTS5's `article_fts_docsize` shadow table: `COUNT(*)` on an
+ * external-content FTS5 table reads the content table, so it could never differ. A failure
+ * here is logged and swallowed, so a broken index costs search, not the whole database.
+ * A later change that runs `VACUUM` (which may renumber `article`'s rowids with equal
+ * counts) must also rebuild the index.
+ */
+class FtsIndexCheckCallback : RoomDatabase.Callback() {
+    override suspend fun onOpen(connection: SQLiteConnection) {
+        super.onOpen(connection)
+        try {
+            val (indexed, articles) = connection.prepare(
+                "SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article_fts_docsize), (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article)"
+            ).use {
+                it.step()
+                it.getLong(0) to it.getLong(1)
+            }
+            if (indexed != articles) {
+                connection.execSQL("INSERT INTO article_fts(article_fts) VALUES ('rebuild')")
+                Timber.w("Search index rebuilt: %d indexed rows, %d articles", indexed, articles)
+            }
+        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
+            throw e
+        } catch (e: Exception) {
+            Timber.e(e, "Search index check failed")
+        }
+    }
+}

     }

+val MIGRATION_9_10 = object : Migration(9, 10) {
+    override suspend fun migrate(connection: SQLiteConnection) {
+        // Replaces the FTS4 search index with FTS5 and rebuilds it from article; no article
+        // row changes. Dropping the FTS4 table also drops its shadow tables. The table and
+        // trigger statements are the ones Room exports for version 10.
+        for (op in listOf("BEFORE_UPDATE", "BEFORE_DELETE", "AFTER_UPDATE", "AFTER_INSERT")) {
+            connection.execSQL("DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS room_fts_content_sync_article_fts_$op")
+        }
+        connection.execSQL("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `article_fts`")
+        connection.execSQL("CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `article_fts` USING FTS5(`title`, `text`, tokenize=`porter unicode61 remove_diacritics 2`, content=`article`)")
+        connection.execSQL("CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS room_fts_content_sync_article_fts_BEFORE_UPDATE BEFORE UPDATE ON `article` BEGIN DELETE FROM `article_fts` WHERE `rowid`=OLD.`rowid`; END")
+        connection.execSQL("CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS room_fts_content_sync_article_fts_BEFORE_DELETE BEFORE DELETE ON `article` BEGIN DELETE FROM `article_fts` WHERE `rowid`=OLD.`rowid`; END")
+        connection.execSQL("CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS room_fts_content_sync_article_fts_AFTER_UPDATE AFTER UPDATE ON `article` BEGIN INSERT INTO `article_fts`(`rowid`, `title`, `text`) VALUES (NEW.`rowid`, NEW.`title`, NEW.`text`); END")
+        connection.execSQL("CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS room_fts_content_sync_article_fts_AFTER_INSERT AFTER INSERT ON `article` BEGIN INSERT INTO `article_fts`(`rowid`, `title`, `text`) VALUES (NEW.`rowid`, NEW.`title`, NEW.`text`); END")
+        // Backfill: index every existing article.
+        connection.execSQL("INSERT INTO `article_fts`(`article_fts`) VALUES ('rebuild')")
+    }
+}
+
 /** Every hand-written migration, in order. Production and the tests register this one list. */

     MIGRATION_1_2,

     const val BY_RESOLVED_ID = "SELECT * FROM article WHERE resolvedId = :resolvedId ORDER BY timeAdded DESC LIMIT 1"
 
+    /**
+     * Search: the live articles whose title or text matches [match] (an FTS5 match string from
+     * `FtsMatchQuery`), best first by bm25 (lower is better), at most [limit]. The index reaches
+     * `article` by rowid. The snippet marks matched words with `<b>` and `</b>`, 15 tokens from
+     * the best column, as the result row shows them.
+     */
+    const val SEARCH_RANKED = """
+        SELECT article.itemId, article.title,
+        COALESCE(article.url, article.givenUrl) AS url,
+        article.image,
+        CASE WHEN article.favorite = '1' OR article.timeFavorited > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS favorite,
+        CASE WHEN article.timeRead IS NOT NULL AND article.timeRead > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS isRead,
+        snippet(article_fts, -1, '<b>', '</b>', '<b>...</b>', 15) AS snippet
+        FROM article_fts
+        JOIN article ON article.rowid = article_fts.rowid
+        WHERE article_fts MATCH :match
+          AND article.deleted_at IS NULL
+        ORDER BY bm25(article_fts)
+        LIMIT :limit
+    """
+
     const val BY_NORMALIZED_URL = "SELECT * FROM article WHERE normalized_url = :normalizedUrl LIMIT 1"
 }

+/**
+ * The ranked search query on a real in-memory database on the bundled SQLite engine (the
+ * engine the app ships): any typed word matches, quoted phrases match as phrases, results
+ * come best first by bm25, at most the limit, never a deleted article, and the index
+ * follows inserts, updates and deletes of `article`.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class Fts5SearchQueryTest {
+
+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+    private lateinit var dao: ArticleDao
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        db = JvmRoomDatabase.create(RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication())
+        dao = db.articleDao()
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+    }
+
+    private fun article(itemId: String, title: String, text: String, deletedAt: Long? = null) = Article(
+        itemId = itemId,
+        title = title,
+        url = "https://example.com/$itemId",
+        text = text,
+        timeAdded = 1_600_000_000_000,
+        timeUpdated = 1_600_000_000_000,
+        deletedAt = deletedAt,
+    )
+
+    /** Filler words that none of the searches below match. */
+    private fun filler(n: Int, words: Int) =
+        (0 until words).joinToString(" ") { listOf("zeta", "quill", "fjord", "basalt", "tundra", "moss", "pebble")[(n + it) % 7] }
+
+    private suspend fun seed() {
+        val rows = mutableListOf<Article>()
+        (1..60).mapTo(rows) { article("f$it", "Filler ${filler(it, 3)}", filler(it, 40)) }
+        rows += article("best", "River mill", "The river turns the mill. River water, mill stones, river, mill. ${filler(1, 10)}")
+        rows += article("river1", "Notes one", "${filler(2, 80)} river ${filler(3, 80)}")
+        rows += article("mill1", "Notes two", "${filler(4, 60)} mill ${filler(5, 60)}")
+        rows += article("phrase", "Notes three", "We walked the old mill road at dawn")
+        rows += article("apart", "Notes four", "Old stones lay by the mill")
+        rows += article("gone", "River mill river mill", "river mill river mill river mill", deletedAt = 1_700_000_000_000)
+        rows += article("ops", "NEAR AND OR", filler(6, 5))
+        rows += article("ab", "a b", filler(7, 5))
+        rows += article("runner", "Notes five", "I run every morning")
+        rows += article("accent", "Café notes", filler(8, 5))
+        (1..150).mapTo(rows) { article("lantern$it", "Lantern $it", "A lantern ${filler(it, 5 + it % 20)}") }
+        dao.upsertArticles(rows)
+    }
+
+    private suspend fun search(input: String, limit: Int = 100): List<ArticleItem> =
+        dao.searchArticlesRanked(FtsMatchQuery.build(input)!!, limit)
+
+    private suspend fun ids(input: String) = search(input).map { it.itemId }
+
+    /** The live matches of [match] in ascending bm25 order, read by a separate raw query. */
+    private suspend fun rawBm25Order(match: String): List<String> = db.useReaderConnection { connection ->
+        connection.usePrepared(
+            "SELECT article.itemId FROM article_fts JOIN article ON article.rowid = article_fts.rowid " +
+                "WHERE article_fts MATCH ? AND article.deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY bm25(article_fts)"
+        ) { statement ->
+            statement.bindText(1, match)
+            buildList { while (statement.step()) add(statement.getText(0)) }
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun operatorWordsAndSymbols_raiseNoError_andMatchAsLiteralWords() = runTest {
+        seed()
+        for (input in listOf("\"", "*", "-", "NEAR", "AND", "OR", "a\"b")) search(input)
+        assertEquals(listOf("ops"), ids("NEAR"))
+        assertEquals(listOf("ops"), ids("AND"))
+        assertEquals(listOf("ops"), ids("OR"))
+        assertEquals(listOf("ab"), ids("a\"b"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun twoWords_returnEitherWord_bestFirstByBm25_withoutDeleted() = runTest {
+        seed()
+        val results = ids("river mill")
+        assertTrue(results.toString(), results.containsAll(listOf("best", "river1", "mill1", "phrase", "apart")))
+        assertTrue(results.all { it in setOf("best", "river1", "mill1", "phrase", "apart") })
+        assertEquals("best", results.first())
+        assertEquals(rawBm25Order(FtsMatchQuery.build("river mill")!!), results)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun manyMatches_returnAtMostTheLimit() = runTest {
+        seed()
+        assertEquals(100, search("lantern").size)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun quotedPhrase_returnsOnlyPhraseMatches() = runTest {
+        seed()
+        assertEquals(listOf("phrase"), ids("\"old mill\""))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun deletedArticle_isNeverReturned() = runTest {
+        seed()
+        for (input in listOf("river", "mill", "river mill", "\"river mill\"")) {
+            assertFalse("gone in <$input>", "gone" in ids(input))
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun index_followsUpdateAndDelete() = runTest {
+        seed()
+        assertTrue("river1" in ids("river"))
+
+        dao.upsertArticle(article("river1", "Notes one", "${filler(2, 80)} harbor ${filler(3, 80)}"))
+        assertFalse("river1" in ids("river"))
+        assertEquals(listOf("river1"), ids("harbor"))
+
+        db.useWriterConnection { it.executeSQL("DELETE FROM article WHERE itemId = 'mill1'") }
+        assertFalse("mill1" in ids("mill"))
+
+        // Raises an error when the index and the content table disagree.
+        db.useWriterConnection { it.executeSQL("INSERT INTO article_fts(article_fts, rank) VALUES('integrity-check', 1)") }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun snippet_boldsTheMatchedWord() = runTest {
+        seed()
+        val snippet = search("river mill").first { it.itemId == "best" }.snippet.orEmpty()
+        assertTrue(snippet, Regex("<b>(river|mill)</b>", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE).containsMatchIn(snippet))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun stemmingAndDiacritics_matchWordForms() = runTest {
+        seed()
+        assertEquals(listOf("runner"), ids("running"))
+        assertEquals(listOf("accent"), ids("cafe"))
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database
+
+import android.app.Application
+import android.content.Context
+import androidx.room3.Room
+import androidx.room3.useReaderConnection
+import androidx.sqlite.driver.bundled.BundledSQLiteDriver
+import androidx.sqlite.execSQL
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.search.FtsMatchQuery
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+import timber.log.Timber
+
+/**
+ * When the search index holds fewer rows than `article` (a missed backfill, rowid drift),
+ * opening the database rebuilds it, so every article is searchable again. When the counts
+ * match, opening rebuilds nothing.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class FtsIndexCheckCallbackTest {
+
+    private val dbName = "fts-index-check"
+    private lateinit var context: Context
+    private val tree = RecordingTree()
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        context = RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication()
+        context.deleteDatabase(dbName)
+        context.getSharedPreferences("db_migrations", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit().clear().commit()
+        Timber.plant(tree)
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        Timber.uproot(tree)
+        context.deleteDatabase(dbName)
+    }
+
+    private fun appDatabase(): AppDatabase =
+        Room.databaseBuilder(context, AppDatabase::class.java, dbName).configureAppDatabase(context).build()
+
+    /** The app's engine and migrations, but no open callback that checks the index. */
+    private fun databaseWithoutCheck(): AppDatabase =
+        Room.databaseBuilder(context, AppDatabase::class.java, dbName)
+            .setDriver(BundledSQLiteDriver())
+            .addMigrations(*ALL_MIGRATIONS)
+            .build()
+
+    /** 20 articles; article 7's title holds the only "saffron". */
+    private suspend fun seedAndClose() {
+        val db = appDatabase()
+        db.articleDao().upsertArticles(
+            (1..20).map { n ->
+                Article(
+                    itemId = "a$n",
+                    title = if (n == 7) "Saffron fields" else "Article $n",
+                    url = "https://example.com/a$n",
+                    normalizedUrl = "example.com/a$n",
+                    text = "Body of article $n",
+                    timeAdded = n.toLong(),
+                    timeUpdated = n.toLong(),
+                )
+            }
+        )
+        db.close()
+    }
+
+    /** Removes article 7 from the search index only, with the FTS5 delete command. */
+    private fun dropArticle7FromIndex() {
+        val connection = BundledSQLiteDriver().open(context.getDatabasePath(dbName).absolutePath)
+        try {
+            val (rowid, title, text) = connection.prepare("SELECT rowid, title, text FROM article WHERE itemId = 'a7'").use {
+                it.step()
+                Triple(it.getLong(0), it.getText(1), it.getText(2))
+            }
+            connection.prepare("INSERT INTO article_fts(article_fts, rowid, title, text) VALUES('delete', ?, ?, ?)").use {
+                it.bindLong(1, rowid)
+                it.bindText(2, title)
+                it.bindText(3, text)
+                it.step()
+            }
+            assertEquals(19L, connection.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article_fts_docsize").use { it.step(); it.getLong(0) })
+        } finally {
+            connection.close()
+        }
+    }
+
+    private suspend fun AppDatabase.indexedRows(): Long =
+        useReaderConnection { c -> c.usePrepared("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article_fts_docsize") { it.step(); it.getLong(0) } }
+
+    @Test
+    fun indexShortOfArticles_isRebuiltOnOpen() = runTest {
+        seedAndClose()
+        dropArticle7FromIndex()
+
+        val db = appDatabase()
+        try {
+            assertEquals(20, db.articleDao().countAllArticles())
+            assertEquals(20L, db.indexedRows())
+            assertEquals(listOf("a7"), db.articleDao().searchArticlesRanked(FtsMatchQuery.build("saffron")!!, 100).map { it.itemId })
+            assertTrue(tree.messages.toString(), tree.messages.any { it.startsWith(REBUILD_LOG) })
+        } finally {
+            db.close()
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun indexInStep_isNotRebuilt() = runTest {
+        seedAndClose()
+        tree.messages.clear()
+
+        val db = appDatabase()
+        try {
+            assertEquals(20, db.articleDao().countAllArticles())
+            assertEquals(20L, db.indexedRows())
+            assertTrue(tree.messages.toString(), tree.messages.none { it.startsWith(REBUILD_LOG) })
+        } finally {
+            db.close()
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun withoutTheCheck_theIndexStaysShort() = runTest {
+        seedAndClose()
+        dropArticle7FromIndex()
+
+        val db = databaseWithoutCheck()
+        try {
+            assertEquals(20, db.articleDao().countAllArticles())
+            assertEquals(19L, db.indexedRows())
+        } finally {
+            db.close()
+        }
+    }
+
+    private class RecordingTree : Timber.Tree() {
+        val messages = mutableListOf<String>()
+        override fun log(priority: Int, tag: String?, message: String, t: Throwable?) {
+            messages += message
+        }
+    }
+
+    private companion object {
+        /** The start of the line [FtsIndexCheckCallback] logs when it rebuilds. */
+        const val REBUILD_LOG = "Search index rebuilt"
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database
+
+import android.app.Application
+import android.content.Context
+import androidx.room3.Room
+import androidx.room3.useReaderConnection
+import androidx.sqlite.SQLiteConnection
+import androidx.sqlite.SQLiteStatement
+import androidx.sqlite.driver.bundled.BundledSQLiteDriver
+import androidx.sqlite.execSQL
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.search.FtsMatchQuery
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.json.JSONObject
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+import java.io.File
+
+/**
+ * A version 9 library opens in the current app with every row intact and a search index
+ * that holds every article again.
+ *
+ * The version 9 file is built on the host from the exported version 9 schema with the
+ * bundled engine (its FTS4 triggers fill the old index as the rows go in), then opened
+ * through [configureAppDatabase] exactly as the app opens it, so Room runs the 9 to 10
+ * migration and validates every table against the current schema. The instrumented
+ * [MigrationTest] case checks the same step on a device.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class Migration9To10JvmTest {
+
+    private val dbName = "migration-9-10-jvm"
+    private lateinit var context: Context
+    private lateinit var schema9: JSONObject
+
+    private val seededTables = listOf("article", "article_tags", "article_images", "article_authors", "local_archive")
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        context = RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication()
+        context.deleteDatabase(dbName)
+        context.getSharedPreferences("db_migrations", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit().clear().commit()
+        schema9 = JSONObject(schemaFile(9).readText()).getJSONObject("database")
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        context.deleteDatabase(dbName)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun version9Library_migratesTo10_keepsEveryRow_andRebuildsTheSearchIndex() = runTest {
+        val before = createVersion9Library()
+        assertEquals(40, before.getValue("article").size)
+        assertEquals(40, before.getValue("article_tags").size)
+        assertEquals(1, before.getValue("article_images").size)
+        assertEquals(1, before.getValue("article_authors").size)
+        assertEquals(1, before.getValue("local_archive").size)
+
+        val db = Room.databaseBuilder(context, AppDatabase::class.java, dbName)
+            .configureAppDatabase(context)
+            .build()
+        try {
+            // The first DAO call opens the database: Room migrates 9 to 10 and validates the schema.
+            // Every fifth article (5, 10, ..., 40) is deleted, so 32 are in the library.
+            assertEquals(32, db.articleDao().countAllArticles())
+
+            db.useReaderConnection { connection ->
+                assertEquals(listOf("10"), connection.usePrepared("PRAGMA user_version") { it.texts() })
+                // Every column of every row reads back unchanged.
+                for ((table, rows) in before) {
+                    val columns = columns(table)
+                    val after = connection.usePrepared(selectAll(table, columns)) { it.rows(columns.size) }
+                    assertEquals("rows of $table", rows, after)
+                }
+                assertEquals(
+                    listOf("40"),
+                    connection.usePrepared("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article_fts_docsize") { it.texts() },
+                )
+                val ftsSql = connection.usePrepared("SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name = 'article_fts'") { it.texts() }.single()
+                assertTrue(ftsSql, ftsSql.contains("fts5", ignoreCase = true))
+                assertEquals(
+                    emptyList<String>(),
+                    connection.usePrepared("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE name = 'article_fts_segments'") { it.texts() },
+                )
+            }
+            assertEquals(
+                listOf("a13"),
+                db.articleDao().searchArticlesRanked(FtsMatchQuery.build("roses")!!, 100).map { it.itemId },
+            )
+        } finally {
+            db.close()
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** Creates the version 9 file from `9.json` and fills it; returns every row of each seeded table. */
+    private fun createVersion9Library(): Map<String, List<List<String?>>> {
+        val path = context.getDatabasePath(dbName)
+        path.parentFile?.mkdirs()
+        val connection = BundledSQLiteDriver().open(path.absolutePath)
+        try {
+            val entities = schema9.getJSONArray("entities")
+            for (i in 0 until entities.length()) {
+                val entity = entities.getJSONObject(i)
+                val table = entity.getString("tableName")
+                connection.execSQL(entity.getString("createSql").replace("\${TABLE_NAME}", table))
+                entity.optJSONArray("indices")?.let { indices ->
+                    for (j in 0 until indices.length()) {
+                        connection.execSQL(indices.getJSONObject(j).getString("createSql").replace("\${TABLE_NAME}", table))
+                    }
+                }
+                entity.optJSONArray("contentSyncTriggers")?.let { triggers ->
+                    for (j in 0 until triggers.length()) connection.execSQL(triggers.getString(j))
+                }
+            }
+            val setup = schema9.getJSONArray("setupQueries")
+            for (i in 0 until setup.length()) connection.execSQL(setup.getString(i))
+            connection.execSQL("PRAGMA user_version = 9")
+
+            for (n in 1..40) connection.insertArticle(n)
+            for (n in 1..40) connection.execSQL("INSERT INTO article_tags (itemId, tag, sortId, type) VALUES ('a$n', 'tag${n % 4}', $n, 'user')")
+            connection.execSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO article_images (imageId, itemId, src, width, height, credit, caption) " +
+                    "VALUES ('img1', 'a1', 'https://example.com/1.png', 640, 480, 'Ann', 'Caption 1')"
+            )
+            connection.execSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO article_authors (authorId, itemId, name, url) VALUES ('au1', 'a1', 'Ann', 'https://example.com/ann')"
+            )
+            connection.execSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO local_archive (itemId, archiveKey, localPath, compressedSizeBytes, originalSizeBytes, downloadedAt, status) " +
+                    "VALUES ('a1', 'k1', '/archives/a1', 10, 20, 3000, 'complete')"
+            )
+
+            assertEquals(listOf("9"), connection.texts("PRAGMA user_version"))
+            // The FTS4 triggers indexed every article.
+            assertEquals(listOf("40"), connection.texts("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article_fts_docsize"))
+            return seededTables.associateWith { connection.dumpTable(it, columns(it)) }
+        } finally {
+            connection.close()
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Article [n]: every fifth deleted, every fourth archived, every third favourite, all with
+     * a normalized URL (so the open callback's URL backfill leaves them as they are). Article
+     * 13's title holds the only "roses".
+     */
+    private fun SQLiteConnection.insertArticle(n: Int) {
+        val deletedAt = if (n % 5 == 0) "${5_000 + n}" else "NULL"
+        val archivedAt = if (n % 4 == 0) "${4_000 + n}" else "NULL"
+        val favorite = if (n % 3 == 0) "'1'" else "NULL"
+        val timeFavorited = if (n % 3 == 0) 3_000 + n else 0
+        val title = if (n == 13) "Roses in winter" else "Title $n"
+        execSQL(
+            "INSERT INTO article (itemId, resolvedId, title, givenTitle, url, givenUrl, normalized_url, excerpt, " +
+                "wordCount, favorite, status, hasImage, hasVideo, hasAudio, sortId, timeAdded, timeUpdated, timeFavorited, " +
+                "listenDurationEstimate, text, articleId, resolved, deleted_at, archived_at, text_source, backed_up_at, " +
+                "sync_failures) " +
+                "VALUES ('a$n', 'r$n', '$title', '$title', 'https://example.com/a$n', " +
+                "'https://example.com/a$n', 'example.com/a$n', 'About $n', 120, $favorite, '0', 1, 0, 0, $n, " +
+                "${1_000 + n}, ${2_000 + n}, $timeFavorited, 60, 'Body of article $n', 'p$n', 1, $deletedAt, $archivedAt, 'warg', " +
+                "${6_000 + n}, 0)"
+        )
+    }
+
+    private fun columns(table: String): List<String> {
+        val entities = schema9.getJSONArray("entities")
+        for (i in 0 until entities.length()) {
+            val entity = entities.getJSONObject(i)
+            if (entity.getString("tableName") != table) continue
+            val fields = entity.getJSONArray("fields")
+            return (0 until fields.length()).map { fields.getJSONObject(it).getString("columnName") }
+        }
+        error("table $table is not in the version 9 schema")
+    }
+
+    private fun SQLiteConnection.dumpTable(table: String, columns: List<String>): List<List<String?>> =
+        prepare(selectAll(table, columns)).use { it.rows(columns.size) }
+
+    private fun selectAll(table: String, columns: List<String>): String {
+        val list = columns.joinToString(", ") { "`$it`" }
+        return "SELECT $list FROM `$table` ORDER BY $list"
+    }
+
+    private fun SQLiteStatement.rows(columnCount: Int): List<List<String?>> = buildList {
+        while (step()) add((0 until columnCount).map { if (isNull(it)) null else getText(it) })
+    }
+
+    private fun SQLiteStatement.texts(): List<String> = buildList { while (step()) add(getText(0)) }
+
+    private fun SQLiteConnection.texts(sql: String): List<String> = prepare(sql).use { it.texts() }
+
+    /** The exported schema of [version]; Gradle runs unit tests from the module directory. */
+    private fun schemaFile(version: Int): File {
+        val relative = "schemas/com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.AppDatabase/$version.json"
+        return listOf(File(relative), File("app/$relative")).firstOrNull { it.isFile }
+            ?: error("schema $relative not found from ${File(".").absolutePath}")
+    }
+}

new file mode 100644

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.search
+
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+import kotlin.random.Random
+
+/**
+ * The match string built from what the person types: every word and every quoted phrase is
+ * one quoted FTS5 string, joined by OR, so no typed character reaches the FTS5 query parser
+ * as an operator.
+ */
+class FtsMatchQueryTest {
+
+    @Test
+    fun twoWords_matchEitherWord() {
+        assertEquals("\"river\" OR \"mill\"", FtsMatchQuery.build("river mill"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun quotedPhrase_staysOnePhrase() {
+        assertEquals("\"old mill\"", FtsMatchQuery.build("\"old mill\""))
+        assertEquals("\"old mill\" OR \"river\"", FtsMatchQuery.build("\"old mill\" river"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun operatorWordsAndSymbols_areQuotedAsLiteralWords() {
+        val expected = mapOf(
+            "\"" to "\"\"\"\"",
+            "*" to "\"*\"",
+            "-" to "\"-\"",
+            "NEAR" to "\"NEAR\"",
+            "AND" to "\"AND\"",
+            "OR" to "\"OR\"",
+            "a\"b" to "\"a\"\"b\"",
+        )
+        for ((input, match) in expected) assertEquals("input $input", match, FtsMatchQuery.build(input))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun emptyOrBlankInput_buildsNoQuery() {
+        assertNull(FtsMatchQuery.build(""))
+        assertNull(FtsMatchQuery.build("   \t "))
+        assertNull(FtsMatchQuery.build("\"\""))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun unclosedQuote_isALiteralCharacter() {
+        assertEquals("\"\"\"old\" OR \"mill\"", FtsMatchQuery.build("\"old mill"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun tabsAndRepeatedSpaces_separateWords() {
+        assertEquals("\"river\" OR \"mill\" OR \"road\"", FtsMatchQuery.build("  river\t\tmill    road "))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun accentedWord_passesThroughUnchanged() {
+        assertEquals("\"café\"", FtsMatchQuery.build("café"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun randomInput_alwaysBuildsWellFormedStrings() {
+        val random = Random(2026)
+        val alphabet = "\"*-()^:+ ab"
+        val wellFormed = Regex("\"(?:[^\"]|\"\")*\"(?: OR \"(?:[^\"]|\"\")*\")*")
+        repeat(200) {
+            val input = String(CharArray(1 + random.nextInt(12)) { alphabet[random.nextInt(alphabet.length)] })
+            val match = FtsMatchQuery.build(input) ?: return@repeat
+            assertEquals("even quote count for <$input>: $match", 0, match.count { it == '"' } % 2)
+            assertTrue("well-formed for <$input>: $match", wellFormed.matches(match))
+        }
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.screens.articleSearch
+
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.ArticleRepository
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.models.ArticleItem
+import io.mockk.coEvery
+import io.mockk.every
+import io.mockk.mockk
+import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
+import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
+import kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable
+import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
+import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOf
+import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.StandardTestDispatcher
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.TestScope
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.UnconfinedTestDispatcher
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.advanceTimeBy
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.advanceUntilIdle
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.resetMain
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runCurrent
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.setMain
+import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/**
+ * Only the newest search reaches the screen: a search that is replaced before it finishes
+ * never shows its results or its error, even when its database call ignores cancellation.
+ */
+@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
+class ArticleSearchViewModelTest {
+
+    private val dispatcher = StandardTestDispatcher()
+    private val repository = mockk<ArticleRepository>()
+
+    private val old = ArticleItem(itemId = "old", title = "Old result", url = "https://example.com/old")
+    private val new = ArticleItem(itemId = "new", title = "New result", url = "https://example.com/new")
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        Dispatchers.setMain(dispatcher)
+        every { repository.allTags() } returns flowOf(emptyList())
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        Dispatchers.resetMain()
+    }
+
+    private class Collected(val states: List<ArticleSearchState>, val events: List<ArticleSearchEvent>)
+
+    /** Builds the ViewModel on the test dispatcher and collects its state and events for the whole test. */
+    private fun TestScope.viewModel(): Pair<ArticleSearchViewModel, Collected> {
+        val viewModel = ArticleSearchViewModel(repository, dispatcher)
+        val states = mutableListOf<ArticleSearchState>()
+        val events = mutableListOf<ArticleSearchEvent>()
+        backgroundScope.launch(UnconfinedTestDispatcher(testScheduler)) { viewModel.state.collect { states += it } }
+        backgroundScope.launch(UnconfinedTestDispatcher(testScheduler)) { viewModel.event.collect { events += it } }
+        return viewModel to Collected(states, events)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun slowSearchReplacedByNewerOne_onlyTheNewerResultIsShown() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        coEvery { repository.searchLocal("slow") } coAnswers { delay(1_000); listOf(old) }
+        coEvery { repository.searchLocal("new") } coAnswers { delay(10); listOf(new) }
+        val (viewModel, collected) = viewModel()
+
+        viewModel.search("slow")
+        advanceTimeBy(5)
+        viewModel.search("new")
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(new), viewModel.state.value.searchResults)
+        assertFalse(viewModel.state.value.isSearching)
+        assertTrue(collected.states.none { old in it.searchResults })
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun replacedSearchThatIgnoresCancellation_isStillNeverShown() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        coEvery { repository.searchLocal("slow") } coAnswers {
+            withContext(NonCancellable) { delay(1_000); listOf(old) }
+        }
+        coEvery { repository.searchLocal("new") } coAnswers { delay(10); listOf(new) }
+        val (viewModel, collected) = viewModel()
+
+        viewModel.search("slow")
+        advanceTimeBy(5)
+        viewModel.search("new")
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(new), viewModel.state.value.searchResults)
+        assertFalse(viewModel.state.value.isSearching)
+        assertTrue(collected.states.none { old in it.searchResults })
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun errorOfTheCurrentSearch_isShown_errorOfAReplacedSearchIsNot() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        val replacedError = IllegalStateException("replaced")
+        val currentError = IllegalStateException("current")
+        coEvery { repository.searchLocal("slow") } coAnswers {
+            withContext(NonCancellable) { delay(1_000); throw replacedError }
+        }
+        coEvery { repository.searchLocal("new") } coAnswers { delay(10); throw currentError }
+        val (viewModel, collected) = viewModel()
+
+        viewModel.search("slow")
+        advanceTimeBy(5)
+        viewModel.search("new")
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        val errors = collected.events.filterIsInstance<ArticleSearchEvent.ShowError>().map { it.error }
+        assertEquals(listOf<Throwable>(currentError), errors)
+        assertFalse(viewModel.state.value.isSearching)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun oneSearch_showsSearchingWhileItRuns_thenTheResults() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        coEvery { repository.searchLocal("river") } coAnswers { delay(100); listOf(new) }
+        val (viewModel, _) = viewModel()
+
+        viewModel.search("river")
+        advanceTimeBy(50)
+        runCurrent()
+        assertTrue(viewModel.state.value.isSearching)
+
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+        assertEquals(listOf(new), viewModel.state.value.searchResults)
+        assertFalse(viewModel.state.value.isSearching)
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database
+
+import android.content.Context
+import android.util.Log
+import androidx.room3.Room
+import androidx.room3.useReaderConnection
+import androidx.test.ext.junit.runners.AndroidJUnit4
+import androidx.test.platform.app.InstrumentationRegistry
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.models.ArticleItem
+import com.jayteealao.trails.testing.SearchCorpus
+import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
+import org.json.JSONObject
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import java.nio.ByteBuffer
+import java.nio.ByteOrder
+
+/**
+ * Records how long a search takes on 5,000 articles ([SearchCorpus]) on the device, through
+ * a file database opened exactly as the app opens it ([configureAppDatabase]). This is the
+ * figure the 200 ms search budget applies to.
+ *
+ * Each input: 3 warm-up and [TIMED] timed calls, median and p90 in milliseconds. One JSON
+ * line per input goes to logcat under the tag [TAG].
+ */
+@RunWith(AndroidJUnit4::class)
+class SearchBenchmarkTest {
+
+    private val dbName = "search-benchmark"
+    private val context: Context = InstrumentationRegistry.getInstrumentation().targetContext
+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        context.deleteDatabase(dbName)
+        db = Room.databaseBuilder(context, AppDatabase::class.java, dbName)
+            .configureAppDatabase(context)
+            .build()
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+        context.deleteDatabase(dbName)
+    }
+
+    /** The search as the app runs it: one FTS4 phrase pattern, then a sort by matchinfo in Kotlin. */
+    private suspend fun search(input: String): List<ArticleItem> {
+        val pattern = "*\"" + "*$input*".replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\"*"
+        return db.articleDao().searchArticlesWithMatchInfo(pattern)
+            .sortedByDescending { score(it.matchInfo) }
+            .map { it.article }
+    }
+
+    /** A copy of the app's matchinfo score: the sum of the phrase-hit counts. */
+    private fun score(matchInfo: ByteArray): Int {
+        val ints = ByteBuffer.wrap(matchInfo).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN).asIntBuffer()
+        ints.position(2)
+        var score = 0
+        while (ints.remaining() >= 3) {
+            score += ints.get()
+            ints.get()
+            ints.get()
+        }
+        return score
+    }
+
+    private suspend fun time(input: String): JSONObject {
+        repeat(3) { search(input) }
+        var rows = 0
+        val ms = (1..TIMED).map {
+            val start = System.nanoTime()
+            rows = search(input).size
+            (System.nanoTime() - start) / 1_000_000.0
+        }.sorted()
+        return JSONObject()
+            .put("input", input)
+            .put("rows", rows)
+            .put("medianMs", (ms[TIMED / 2 - 1] + ms[TIMED / 2]) / 2)
+            .put("p90Ms", ms[(TIMED * 9 + 9) / 10 - 1])
+            .put("iterations", TIMED)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun searchOnFiveThousandArticles_isRecorded() = runBlocking {
+        val articles = SearchCorpus.articles()
+        val seedStart = System.nanoTime()
+        db.articleDao().upsertArticles(articles)
+        val seedMs = (System.nanoTime() - seedStart) / 1_000_000.0
+        val sqliteVersion = db.useReaderConnection { c -> c.usePrepared("SELECT sqlite_version()") { it.step(); it.getText(0) } }
+
+        SearchCorpus.INPUTS.forEach { input ->
+            val entry = time(input)
+                .put("path", PATH)
+                .put("engine", "bundled SQLite (device)")
+                .put("sqliteVersion", sqliteVersion)
+                .put("articles", articles.size)
+                .put("seedMs", seedMs)
+            Log.i(TAG, entry.toString())
+        }
+    }
+
+    private companion object {
+        const val TAG = "SearchBenchmark"
+        const val TIMED = 10
+        const val PATH = "version 9: FTS4 phrase pattern + matchinfo sort in Kotlin"
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.testing
+
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.Article
+import kotlin.random.Random
+
+/**
+ * A deterministic library of [ROWS] articles for the search benchmarks.
+ *
+ * Titles have 6–10 words and texts 500 words, drawn from a fixed vocabulary of
+ * [VOCABULARY_SIZE] made-up words. Every article has `timeAdded` and `timeUpdated`; 5% are
+ * deleted and 10% archived. Three real words are planted: [HARBOR] in 5% of the articles,
+ * [LANTERN] in 2% and [MERIDIAN] in 40% (far more matches than one result page holds).
+ * The same seed gives the same library on the host and on a device.
+ *
+ * The androidTest source set keeps an identical copy of this file.
+ */
+object SearchCorpus {
+    const val ROWS = 5_000
+    const val VOCABULARY_SIZE = 2_000
+    const val TEXT_WORDS = 500
+    const val HARBOR = "harbor"
+    const val LANTERN = "lantern"
+    const val MERIDIAN = "meridian"
+
+    /** The inputs a person types, as the benchmarks time them. */
+    val INPUTS = listOf("$HARBOR $LANTERN", MERIDIAN, HARBOR)
+
+    private val planted = setOf(HARBOR, LANTERN, MERIDIAN)
+
+    /** Made-up words of two to four syllables; none is a real word the corpus plants. */
+    val vocabulary: List<String> by lazy {
+        val random = Random(7)
+        val consonants = "bdfgklmnprstvz"
+        val vowels = "aeiou"
+        val words = LinkedHashSet<String>()
+        while (words.size < VOCABULARY_SIZE) {
+            val word = buildString {
+                repeat(2 + random.nextInt(3)) {
+                    append(consonants[random.nextInt(consonants.length)])
+                    append(vowels[random.nextInt(vowels.length)])
+                }
+            }
+            if (word !in planted) words += word
+        }
+        words.toList()
+    }
+
+    fun articles(rows: Int = ROWS): List<Article> {
+        val random = Random(42)
+        val words = vocabulary
+        fun phrase(count: Int) = List(count) { words[random.nextInt(words.size)] }
+        return (1..rows).map { n ->
+            val title = phrase(6 + random.nextInt(5)).toMutableList()
+            val text = phrase(TEXT_WORDS).toMutableList()
+            if (random.nextInt(100) < 5) text[random.nextInt(text.size)] = HARBOR
+            if (random.nextInt(100) < 2) title[random.nextInt(title.size)] = LANTERN
+            if (random.nextInt(100) < 40) text[random.nextInt(text.size)] = MERIDIAN
+            val time = 1_600_000_000_000L + n * 60_000L
+            Article(
+                itemId = "s$n",
+                title = title.joinToString(" ").replaceFirstChar { it.uppercase() },
+                url = "https://example.com/search/$n",
+                text = text.joinToString(" "),
+                timeAdded = time,
+                timeUpdated = time,
+                deletedAt = if (random.nextInt(100) < 5) time + 1 else null,
+                archivedAt = if (random.nextInt(100) < 10) time + 2 else null,
+            )
+        }
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database
+
+import android.app.Application
+import androidx.room3.useReaderConnection
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.models.ArticleItem
+import com.jayteealao.trails.testing.JvmRoomDatabase
+import com.jayteealao.trails.testing.SearchCorpus
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.json.JSONArray
+import org.json.JSONObject
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+import java.io.File
+import java.nio.ByteBuffer
+import java.nio.ByteOrder
+import kotlin.time.Duration.Companion.minutes
+
+/**
+ * Records how long a search takes on 5,000 articles ([SearchCorpus]) on the host's bundled
+ * SQLite engine, the same engine the app ships. The numbers are recorded, not gated; the
+ * device figure from the instrumented benchmark is the one the 200 ms budget applies to.
+ *
+ * Each input is searched exactly as the app's search runs it: 3 warm-up and [TIMED] timed
+ * calls, median and p90 in milliseconds. Written to `build/reports/benchmark/search.json`.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class SearchBenchmarkJvmTest {
+
+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+    private lateinit var dao: ArticleDao
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        db = JvmRoomDatabase.create(RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication())
+        dao = db.articleDao()
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+    }
+
+    /** The search as the app runs it: one FTS4 phrase pattern, then a sort by matchinfo in Kotlin. */
+    private suspend fun search(input: String): List<ArticleItem> {
+        val pattern = "*\"" + "*$input*".replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\"*"
+        return dao.searchArticlesWithMatchInfo(pattern)
+            .sortedByDescending { score(it.matchInfo) }
+            .map { it.article }
+    }
+
+    /** A copy of the app's matchinfo score: the sum of the phrase-hit counts. */
+    private fun score(matchInfo: ByteArray): Int {
+        val ints = ByteBuffer.wrap(matchInfo).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN).asIntBuffer()
+        ints.position(2)
+        var score = 0
+        while (ints.remaining() >= 3) {
+            score += ints.get()
+            ints.get()
+            ints.get()
+        }
+        return score
+    }
+
+    private suspend fun time(input: String): JSONObject {
+        repeat(3) { search(input) }
+        var rows = 0
+        val ms = (1..TIMED).map {
+            val start = System.nanoTime()
+            rows = search(input).size
+            (System.nanoTime() - start) / 1_000_000.0
+        }.sorted()
+        return JSONObject()
+            .put("input", input)
+            .put("rows", rows)
+            .put("medianMs", (ms[TIMED / 2 - 1] + ms[TIMED / 2]) / 2)
+            .put("p90Ms", ms[(TIMED * 9 + 9) / 10 - 1])
+            .put("iterations", TIMED)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun searchOnFiveThousandArticles_isRecorded() = runTest(timeout = 10.minutes) {
+        val articles = SearchCorpus.articles()
+        val seedStart = System.nanoTime()
+        dao.upsertArticles(articles)
+        val seedMs = (System.nanoTime() - seedStart) / 1_000_000.0
+        println("seeded ${articles.size} articles in $seedMs ms")
+        val sqliteVersion = db.useReaderConnection { c -> c.usePrepared("SELECT sqlite_version()") { it.step(); it.getText(0) } }
+
+        val report = JSONArray()
+        SearchCorpus.INPUTS.forEach { input ->
+            report.put(
+                time(input)
+                    .put("path", PATH)
+                    .put("engine", "bundled SQLite (JVM)")
+                    .put("sqliteVersion", sqliteVersion)
+                    .put("articles", articles.size)
+                    .put("seedMs", seedMs)
+            )
+        }
+        val text = report.toString(2)
+        println(text)
+        val file = File("build/reports/benchmark/search.json")
+        file.parentFile?.mkdirs()
+        file.writeText(text)
+    }
+
+    private companion object {
+        const val TIMED = 10
+        const val PATH = "version 9: FTS4 phrase pattern + matchinfo sort in Kotlin"
+    }
+}

diff --git a/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/ArticleQueryPlanTest.kt b/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/ArticleQueryPlanTest.kt

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database
+
+import android.app.Application
+import androidx.paging.PagingSource
+import androidx.room3.useReaderConnection
+import androidx.room3.executeSQL
+import androidx.room3.useWriterConnection
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.models.ArticleItem
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleTags
+import com.jayteealao.trails.testing.JvmRoomDatabase
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.json.JSONArray
+import org.json.JSONObject
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+import java.io.File

+/**
+ * Records how long the first page of each list takes on 5,000 articles, on the bundled
+ * SQLite engine the app ships. The numbers are recorded, not gated: they are the list-query
+ * figures that the search work compares against.
+ *
+ * For the library, favourite, archive and tag lists: the first page through the DAO's
+ * PagingSource, and the raw first page as Room's paging wraps it. Then the seven indexes are
+ * dropped and the version 8 library query (the GROUP BY form) is timed on the same rows as
+ * the before figure. Written to `build/reports/benchmark/list-query.json`.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class ListQueryBenchmarkTest {
+
+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+    private lateinit var dao: ArticleDao
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        db = JvmRoomDatabase.create(RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication())
+        dao = db.articleDao()
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+    }
+
+    /** 5,000 articles: 10% archived, 5% deleted, 10% favourite, two tags each. */
+    private suspend fun seed() {
+        val articles = (1..ROWS).map { n ->
+            Article(
+                itemId = "a$n",
+                resolvedId = "r$n",
+                title = "Article $n",
+                url = "https://example.com/a$n",
+                normalizedUrl = "example.com/a$n",
+                excerpt = "Excerpt of article $n",
+                timeAdded = n * 10L,
+                timeUpdated = n * 10L,
+                favorite = if (n % 10 == 1) "1" else null,
+                timeFavorited = if (n % 10 == 1) n * 10L else 0,
+                archivedAt = if (n % 10 == 2) n * 10L else null,
+                deletedAt = if (n % 20 == 3) n * 10L else null,
+            )
+        }
+        val tags = (1..ROWS).flatMap { n ->
+            listOf(
+                ArticleTags(itemId = "a$n", tag = "topic${n % 50}", sortId = null, type = null),
+                ArticleTags(itemId = "a$n", tag = "source${n % 7}", sortId = null, type = null),
+            )
+        }
+        dao.upsertArticles(articles)
+        dao.insertArticleTags(tags)
+    }
+
+    private suspend fun PagingSource<Int, ArticleItem>.firstPageSize(): Int {
+        val result = load(PagingSource.LoadParams.Refresh(key = null, loadSize = PAGE, placeholdersEnabled = false))
+        return (result as PagingSource.LoadResult.Page).data.size
+    }
+
+    private suspend fun rawFirstPage(sql: String): Int = db.useReaderConnection { connection ->
+        connection.usePrepared("SELECT * FROM ($sql) LIMIT $PAGE OFFSET 0") { s ->
+            if (sql.contains(":tag")) s.bindText(1, TAG)
+            var rows = 0
+            while (s.step()) rows++
+            rows
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** 3 warm-up and [TIMED] timed runs of [block], each of which must return a full page. */
+    private suspend fun time(name: String, block: suspend () -> Int): JSONObject {
+        repeat(3) { assertEquals(name, PAGE, block()) }
+        val ms = (1..TIMED).map {
+            val start = System.nanoTime()
+            val rows = block()
+            val elapsed = (System.nanoTime() - start) / 1_000_000.0
+            assertEquals(name, PAGE, rows)
+            elapsed
+        }.sorted()
+        return JSONObject()
+            .put("query", name)
+            .put("medianMs", (ms[TIMED / 2 - 1] + ms[TIMED / 2]) / 2)
+            .put("p90Ms", ms[(TIMED * 9 + 9) / 10 - 1])
+            .put("iterations", TIMED)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun firstPageOfEachList_onFiveThousandArticles_isRecorded() = runTest {
+        seed()
+        val sqliteVersion = db.useReaderConnection { c -> c.usePrepared("SELECT sqlite_version()") { it.step(); it.getText(0) } }
+
+        val entries = mutableListOf<JSONObject>()
+        entries += time("MAIN_LIST paging") { dao.getArticlesWithTags().firstPageSize() }
+        entries += time("FAVORITE_LIST paging") { dao.getFavoriteArticlesWithTags().firstPageSize() }
+        entries += time("ARCHIVE_LIST paging") { dao.getArchivedArticlesWithTags().firstPageSize() }
+        entries += time("TAG_LIST_BY_TAG paging") { dao.getArticlesWithTag(TAG).firstPageSize() }
+        entries += time("MAIN_LIST raw") { rawFirstPage(ArticleSql.MAIN_LIST) }
+        entries += time("FAVORITE_LIST raw") { rawFirstPage(ArticleSql.FAVORITE_LIST) }
+        entries += time("ARCHIVE_LIST raw") { rawFirstPage(ArticleSql.ARCHIVE_LIST) }
+        entries += time("TAG_LIST_BY_TAG raw") { rawFirstPage(ArticleSql.TAG_LIST_BY_TAG) }
+
+        // Before: the version 8 library query without the version 9 indexes, on the same rows.
+        db.useWriterConnection { c -> NEW_INDEXES.forEach { c.executeSQL("DROP INDEX $it") } }
+        entries += time("MAIN_LIST version 8 (GROUP BY, no indexes) raw") { rawFirstPage(VERSION_8_MAIN_LIST) }
+
+        val report = JSONArray()
+        entries.forEach {
+            report.put(it.put("engine", "bundled SQLite (JVM)").put("sqliteVersion", sqliteVersion).put("rows", ROWS))
+        }
+        val text = report.toString(2)
+        println(text)
+        val file = File("build/reports/benchmark/list-query.json")
+        file.parentFile?.mkdirs()
+        file.writeText(text)
+    }
+
+    private companion object {
+        const val ROWS = 5_000
+        const val PAGE = 50
+        const val TIMED = 10
+        const val TAG = "topic7"
+
+        val NEW_INDEXES = listOf(
+            "index_article_deleted_at_archived_at_timeAdded",
+            "index_article_deleted_at_archived_at_timeFavorited_timeAdded",
+            "index_article_timeUpdated_itemId",
+            "index_article_backed_up_at_deleted_at_timeAdded",
+            "index_article_resolvedId",
+            "index_article_normalized_url",
+            "index_article_tags_tag",
+        )
+
+        /** The library query as version 8 shipped it, kept here only as the before figure. */
+        const val VERSION_8_MAIN_LIST = """
+            SELECT art.itemId, art.title, COALESCE(art.url, art.givenUrl) AS url, art.image,
+            CASE WHEN art.favorite = '1' OR art.timeFavorited > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS favorite,
+            CASE WHEN art.timeRead IS NOT NULL AND art.timeRead > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS isRead,
+            GROUP_CONCAT(tag.tag) AS tagsString
+            FROM article AS art
+            LEFT JOIN article_tags AS tag ON art.itemId = tag.itemId
+            WHERE art.archived_at IS NULL AND art.deleted_at IS NULL
+            GROUP BY art.itemId
+            ORDER BY art.timeAdded DESC
+        """
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database
+
+import android.app.Application
+import android.content.Context
+import androidx.room3.Room
+import androidx.room3.useReaderConnection
+import androidx.sqlite.SQLiteConnection
+import androidx.sqlite.SQLiteStatement
+import androidx.sqlite.driver.bundled.BundledSQLiteDriver
+import androidx.sqlite.execSQL
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.json.JSONObject
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+import java.io.File
+
+/**
+ * A version 8 library opens in the current app with every row intact and the seven new
+ * indexes in place.
+ *
+ * The version 8 file is built on the host from the exported version 8 schema with the bundled
+ * engine, then opened through [configureAppDatabase] exactly as the app opens it, so Room runs
+ * the 8 to 9 migration and validates every table, indexes included, against the current
+ * schema. The instrumented [MigrationTest] case checks the same step on a device.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class Migration8To9JvmTest {
+
+    private val dbName = "migration-8-9-jvm"
+    private lateinit var context: Context
+    private lateinit var schema8: JSONObject
+
+    private val seededTables = listOf("article", "article_tags", "article_images", "article_authors", "local_archive")
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        context = RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication()
+        context.deleteDatabase(dbName)
+        context.getSharedPreferences("db_migrations", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit().clear().commit()
+        schema8 = JSONObject(schemaFile(8).readText()).getJSONObject("database")
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        context.deleteDatabase(dbName)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun version8Library_migratesTo9_keepsEveryRow_andAddsTheIndexes() = runTest {
+        val before = createVersion8Library()
+        assertEquals(30, before.getValue("article").size)
+        assertEquals(40, before.getValue("article_tags").size)
+        assertEquals(2, before.getValue("article_images").size)
+        assertEquals(1, before.getValue("article_authors").size)
+        assertEquals(1, before.getValue("local_archive").size)
+
+        val db = Room.databaseBuilder(context, AppDatabase::class.java, dbName)
+            .configureAppDatabase(context)
+            .build()
+        try {
+            // The first DAO call opens the database: Room migrates 8 to 9 and validates the schema.
+            // Every fifth article (5, 10, …, 30) is deleted, so 24 are in the library.
+            assertEquals(24, db.articleDao().countAllArticles())
+
+            db.useReaderConnection { connection ->
+                assertEquals(listOf("9"), connection.usePrepared("PRAGMA user_version") { it.texts() })
+                // Every column of every row reads back unchanged.
+                for ((table, rows) in before) {
+                    val columns = columns(table)
+                    val after = connection.usePrepared(selectAll(table, columns)) { it.rows(columns.size) }
+                    assertEquals("rows of $table", rows, after)
+                }
+                assertEquals(
+                    setOf(
+                        "index_article_deleted_at_archived_at_timeAdded",
+                        "index_article_deleted_at_archived_at_timeFavorited_timeAdded",
+                        "index_article_timeUpdated_itemId",
+                        "index_article_backed_up_at_deleted_at_timeAdded",
+                        "index_article_resolvedId",
+                        "index_article_normalized_url",
+                        "sqlite_autoindex_article_1",
+                    ),
+                    connection.usePrepared("SELECT name FROM pragma_index_list('article')") { it.texts() }.toSet(),
+                )
+                assertEquals(
+                    setOf("index_article_tags_tag", "sqlite_autoindex_article_tags_1"),
+                    connection.usePrepared("SELECT name FROM pragma_index_list('article_tags')") { it.texts() }.toSet(),
+                )
+            }
+        } finally {
+            db.close()
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** Creates the version 8 file from `8.json` and fills it; returns every row of each seeded table. */
+    private fun createVersion8Library(): Map<String, List<List<String?>>> {
+        val path = context.getDatabasePath(dbName)
+        path.parentFile?.mkdirs()
+        val connection = BundledSQLiteDriver().open(path.absolutePath)
+        try {
+            val entities = schema8.getJSONArray("entities")
+            for (i in 0 until entities.length()) {
+                val entity = entities.getJSONObject(i)
+                val table = entity.getString("tableName")
+                connection.execSQL(entity.getString("createSql").replace("\${TABLE_NAME}", table))
+                entity.optJSONArray("indices")?.let { indices ->
+                    for (j in 0 until indices.length()) {
+                        connection.execSQL(indices.getJSONObject(j).getString("createSql").replace("\${TABLE_NAME}", table))
+                    }
+                }
+                entity.optJSONArray("contentSyncTriggers")?.let { triggers ->
+                    for (j in 0 until triggers.length()) connection.execSQL(triggers.getString(j))
+                }
+            }
+            val setup = schema8.getJSONArray("setupQueries")
+            for (i in 0 until setup.length()) connection.execSQL(setup.getString(i))
+            connection.execSQL("PRAGMA user_version = 8")
+
+            for (n in 1..30) connection.insertArticle(n)
+            // 40 tags: one on each article, and a second on the first ten, one keyed by a resolved id.
+            for (n in 1..30) connection.execSQL("INSERT INTO article_tags (itemId, tag, sortId, type) VALUES ('a$n', 'tag${n % 4}', $n, 'user')")
+            for (n in 1..9) connection.execSQL("INSERT INTO article_tags (itemId, tag) VALUES ('a$n', 'extra')")
+            connection.execSQL("INSERT INTO article_tags (itemId, tag) VALUES ('r3', 'resolved')")
+            for (n in 1..2) {
+                connection.execSQL(
+                    "INSERT INTO article_images (imageId, itemId, src, width, height, credit, caption) " +
+                        "VALUES ('img$n', 'a$n', 'https://example.com/$n.png', 640, 480, 'Ann', 'Caption $n')"
+                )
+            }
+            connection.execSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO article_authors (authorId, itemId, name, url) VALUES ('au1', 'a1', 'Ann', 'https://example.com/ann')"
+            )
+            connection.execSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO local_archive (itemId, archiveKey, localPath, compressedSizeBytes, originalSizeBytes, downloadedAt, status) " +
+                    "VALUES ('a1', 'k1', '/archives/a1', 10, 20, 3000, 'complete')"
+            )
+
+            assertEquals(listOf("8"), connection.texts("PRAGMA user_version"))
+            return seededTables.associateWith { connection.dumpTable(it, columns(it)) }
+        } finally {
+            connection.close()
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Article [n]: every fifth deleted, every fourth archived, every third favourite, odd ones
+     * with a resolved id, all with a normalized URL (so the open callback's URL backfill
+     * leaves them as they are), and article 7 at two failed pushes.
+     */
+    private fun SQLiteConnection.insertArticle(n: Int) {
+        val deletedAt = if (n % 5 == 0) "${5_000 + n}" else "NULL"
+        val archivedAt = if (n % 4 == 0) "${4_000 + n}" else "NULL"
+        val favorite = if (n % 3 == 0) "'1'" else "NULL"
+        val timeFavorited = if (n % 3 == 0) 3_000 + n else 0
+        val resolvedId = if (n % 2 == 1) "'r$n'" else "NULL"
+        val syncFailures = if (n == 7) 2 else 0
+        execSQL(
+            "INSERT INTO article (itemId, resolvedId, title, givenTitle, url, givenUrl, normalized_url, excerpt, " +
+                "wordCount, favorite, status, hasImage, hasVideo, hasAudio, sortId, timeAdded, timeUpdated, timeFavorited, " +
+                "listenDurationEstimate, text, articleId, resolved, deleted_at, archived_at, text_source, backed_up_at, " +
+                "sync_failures) " +
+                "VALUES ('a$n', $resolvedId, 'Title $n', 'Title $n', 'https://example.com/a$n', " +
+                "'https://example.com/a$n', 'example.com/a$n', 'About $n', 120, $favorite, '0', 1, 0, 0, $n, " +
+                "${1_000 + n}, ${2_000 + n}, $timeFavorited, 60, 'Body $n', 'p$n', 1, $deletedAt, $archivedAt, 'warg', " +
+                "${if (n % 2 == 0) "${6_000 + n}" else "NULL"}, $syncFailures)"
+        )
+    }
+
+    private fun columns(table: String): List<String> {
+        val entities = schema8.getJSONArray("entities")
+        for (i in 0 until entities.length()) {
+            val entity = entities.getJSONObject(i)
+            if (entity.getString("tableName") != table) continue
+            val fields = entity.getJSONArray("fields")
+            return (0 until fields.length()).map { fields.getJSONObject(it).getString("columnName") }
+        }
+        error("table $table is not in the version 8 schema")
+    }
+
+    private fun SQLiteConnection.dumpTable(table: String, columns: List<String>): List<List<String?>> =
+        prepare(selectAll(table, columns)).use { it.rows(columns.size) }
+
+    private fun selectAll(table: String, columns: List<String>): String {
+        val list = columns.joinToString(", ") { "`$it`" }
+        return "SELECT $list FROM `$table` ORDER BY $list"
+    }
+
+    private fun SQLiteStatement.rows(columnCount: Int): List<List<String?>> = buildList {
+        while (step()) add((0 until columnCount).map { if (isNull(it)) null else getText(it) })
+    }
+
+    private fun SQLiteStatement.texts(): List<String> = buildList { while (step()) add(getText(0)) }
+
+    private fun SQLiteConnection.texts(sql: String): List<String> = prepare(sql).use { it.texts() }
+
+    /** The exported schema of [version]; Gradle runs unit tests from the module directory. */
+    private fun schemaFile(version: Int): File {
+        val relative = "schemas/com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.AppDatabase/$version.json"
+        return listOf(File(relative), File("app/$relative")).firstOrNull { it.isFile }
+            ?: error("schema $relative not found from ${File(".").absolutePath}")
+    }
+}

    wip(android): test that rows changed during a sync push are each pushed exactly once

diff --git a/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/services/firestore/KeysetPushTest.kt b/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/services/firestore/KeysetPushTest.kt
