+package com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore
+
+import android.app.Application
+import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
+import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseUser
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreException
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.AppDatabase
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.Article
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.ArticleDao
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.ArticleSql
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.MAX_SYNC_FAILURES
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleImages
+import com.jayteealao.trails.testing.JvmRoomDatabase
+import com.jayteealao.trails.testing.TestLocalDataOwner
+import io.mockk.coEvery
+import io.mockk.every
+import io.mockk.mockk
+import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
+import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
+import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.StandardTestDispatcher
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+
+/**
+ * The push loop pages by key over its run window, on the real DAO, the real
+ * [FirestoreSyncManager] and a real in-memory database; only the Firestore calls are faked.
+ *
+ * The main case is the one OFFSET paging got wrong: while the loop runs, every pushed row
+ * gets a newer `timeUpdated`, and on the first call five rows of a later page do too.
+ * With `ORDER BY timeUpdated DESC LIMIT n OFFSET k`, each such write moved rows between
+ * pages, so some rows were skipped and others read twice. With keyset pages of the window
+ * (last sync, run start], a row changed during the run leaves this run's window and is
+ * pushed by the next run, and every other row is pushed exactly once.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class KeysetPushTest {
+
+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+    private lateinit var dao: ArticleDao
+    private lateinit var backup: FirestoreBackupService
+    private lateinit var manager: FirestoreSyncManager
+
+    private var remoteFirstSync = false
+    private var remoteLastSync: Long? = 1_000L
+
+    /** Item ids of every backup call that succeeded, one count per push. */
+    private val pushed = mutableMapOf<String, Int>()
+    private val calls = mutableListOf<List<String>>()
+    private val rowsArguments = mutableListOf<Map<String, ArticleRows>>()
+
+    /** Runs inside each backup call before it answers; real DAO writes while the loop runs. */
+    private var duringPush: suspend (List<Article>) -> Unit = {}
+    private var failing: Set<String> = emptySet()
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        val context = RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication()
+        db = JvmRoomDatabase.create(context)
+        dao = db.articleDao()
+
+        val user = mockk<FirebaseUser>().also { every { it.uid } returns "u1" }
+        val auth = mockk<FirebaseAuth>().also { every { it.currentUser } returns user }
+        backup = mockk()
+        coEvery { backup.getUserMetaSnapshot() } answers {
+            Result.success(FirestoreBackupService.UserMetaSnapshot(isFirstSync = remoteFirstSync, lastSyncTimestamp = remoteLastSync))
+        }
+        coEvery { backup.updateLastSyncTimestamp(any()) } answers {
+            remoteLastSync = firstArg()
+            remoteFirstSync = false
+            Result.success(Unit)
+        }
+        coEvery { backup.backupArticlesPaginated(any(), any(), any(), any()) } coAnswers {
+            val articles = firstArg<List<Article>>()
+            calls += articles.map { it.itemId }
+            rowsArguments += arg<Map<String, ArticleRows>>(3)
+            duringPush(articles)
+            if (articles.any { it.itemId in failing }) {
+                Result.failure(FirebaseFirestoreException("too big", FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.INVALID_ARGUMENT))
+            } else {
+                articles.forEach { pushed.merge(it.itemId, 1, Int::plus) }
+                Result.success(articles.size)
+            }
+        }
+
+        manager = FirestoreSyncManager(
+            context = context,
+            firestore = mockk<FirebaseFirestore>(relaxed = true),
+            auth = auth,
+            articleDao = dao,
+            firestoreBackupService = backup,
+            scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + StandardTestDispatcher()),
+            localDataOwner = TestLocalDataOwner.ownsEveryUid(),
+        )
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+    }
+
+    private fun id(n: Int) = "k%03d".format(n)
+
+    /** Backed up before, so only the push loop (not the never-backed-up sweep) sends them. */
+    private fun article(n: Int, timeUpdated: Long, deleted: Boolean = false, syncFailures: Int = 0) = Article(
+        itemId = id(n),
+        title = "Article $n",
+        url = "https://example.com/${id(n)}",
+        timeAdded = 1_500,
+        timeUpdated = timeUpdated,
+        deletedAt = if (deleted) timeUpdated else null,
+        backedUpAt = 1_500,
+        syncFailures = syncFailures,
+    )
+
+    /**
+     * One push run on a real dispatcher: the fake backup call writes to Room, which suspends,
+     * and on the test scheduler's virtual clock the push timeout would fire at once.
+     */
+    private suspend fun push(): SyncRunResult = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) { manager.pushLocalChanges() }
+
+    /** Waits until the clock is past [millis], so the next run's window covers it. */
+    private fun clockPast(millis: Long) {
+        while (System.currentTimeMillis() <= millis) Thread.sleep(2)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowsChangedDuringTheLoop_eachArticlePushedExactlyOnce() = runTest {
+        // 500 rows changed after last sync (1_000): timeUpdated 2_000…2_499, 50 deleted, and
+        // rows 90…109 share one timeUpdated, across the boundary of pages 2 and 3, so the
+        // itemId tiebreak decides their order.
+        dao.upsertArticles(
+            (0 until 500).map { n -> article(n, timeUpdated = if (n in 90..109) 2_090L else 2_000L + n, deleted = n % 10 == 3) }
+        )
+        val bumpedBeforeRead = (480 until 485).map(::id).toSet() // all on the last page
+        var maxBump = 0L
+        var firstCall = true
+        duringPush = { articles ->
+            val ids = articles.map { it.itemId } + if (firstCall) bumpedBeforeRead else emptySet()
+            firstCall = false
+            for (itemId in ids) {
+                val at = System.currentTimeMillis() + 1
+                dao.updateTimeUpdated(itemId, at)
+                maxBump = maxOf(maxBump, at)
+            }
+        }
+
+        val first = push()
+
+        assertEquals(SyncRunStatus.SUCCESS, first.status)
+        val everyId = (0 until 500).map(::id).toSet()
+        assertEquals(everyId - bumpedBeforeRead, pushed.keys)
+        assertTrue("an article was pushed twice in one run: $pushed", pushed.values.all { it == 1 })
+        assertEquals(10, calls.size)
+
+        // The next run's window starts at this run's start: it pushes every row changed
+        // during the first run once, the five rows changed before they were read included.
+        duringPush = {}
+        clockPast(maxBump)
+        val afterFirst = pushed.toMap()
+        pushed.clear()
+        val second = push()
+
+        assertEquals(SyncRunStatus.SUCCESS, second.status)
+        assertEquals(everyId, pushed.keys)
+        assertTrue(pushed.values.all { it == 1 })
+        // Across both runs: once for each row changed before it was read, twice for each row
+        // changed after its push (that later change is the second run's to push).
+        val total = everyId.associateWith { (afterFirst[it] ?: 0) + (pushed[it] ?: 0) }
+        assertEquals(everyId.associateWith { if (it in bumpedBeforeRead) 1 else 2 }, total)
+
+        // Nothing changed during the second run, so a third run pushes nothing.
+        pushed.clear()
+        clockPast(System.currentTimeMillis())
+        assertEquals(SyncRunStatus.SUCCESS, push().status)
+        assertTrue("the third run pushed $pushed", pushed.isEmpty())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun firstSync_pushesEveryRowOnceInPagesOfFifty() = runTest {
+        remoteFirstSync = true
+        remoteLastSync = null
+        dao.upsertArticles((0 until 250).map { article(it, timeUpdated = 100L + it) })
+
+        val result = push()
+
+        assertEquals(SyncRunStatus.SUCCESS, result.status)
+        assertEquals(5, calls.size)
+        assertTrue(calls.all { it.size == 50 })
+        assertEquals((0 until 250).map(::id).toSet(), pushed.keys)
+        assertTrue(pushed.values.all { it == 1 })
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun thirdStrikeDuringTheRun_shiftsNoOtherRow() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(
+            (0 until 120).map { n -> article(n, timeUpdated = 2_000L + n, syncFailures = if (n == 10) MAX_SYNC_FAILURES - 1 else 0) }
+        )
+        failing = setOf(id(10))
+
+        val result = push()
+
+        assertEquals(SyncRunStatus.PARTIAL_FAILURE, result.status)
+        assertEquals(MAX_SYNC_FAILURES, dao.getArticleById(id(10))!!.syncFailures)
+        assertEquals((0 until 120).map(::id).toSet() - id(10), pushed.keys)
+        assertTrue(pushed.values.all { it == 1 })
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun imageRowsRideTheKeysetPages() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles((0 until 3).map { article(it, timeUpdated = 2_000L + it) })
+        val image = ArticleImages(imageId = "img1", itemId = id(1), src = "https://example.com/i.png", width = 1, height = 1, credit = "", caption = "")
+        dao.insertArticleImages(listOf(image))
+
+        push()
+
+        assertEquals(1, calls.size)
+        assertEquals(listOf(image), rowsArguments.single().getValue(id(1)).images)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun syncQueriesUseNoOffset() {
+        for (sql in listOf(
+            ArticleSql.CHANGED_SINCE_KEYSET,
+            ArticleSql.COUNT_CHANGED_SINCE,
+            ArticleSql.NEVER_BACKED_UP,
+            ArticleSql.COUNT_NEVER_BACKED_UP,
+        )) {
+            assertFalse(sql, sql.contains("OFFSET", ignoreCase = true))
+        }
+        assertTrue(ArticleDao::class.java.methods.none { it.name == "getArticlesModifiedSincePaginated" })
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database
+
+import android.app.Application
+import androidx.room3.useReaderConnection
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleTags
+import com.jayteealao.trails.testing.JvmRoomDatabase
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+import java.io.File
+
+/**
+ * Runs `EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN` on the bundled SQLite engine (the one the app ships) for every
+ * list, tag, lookup and sync query in [ArticleSql], the same text the DAO declares, and
+ * checks that each one reads `article` through its index: the expected index is named, no
+ * step reads `article` without an index, and the ordered lists and the sync pages need no
+ * sort of their own. Unbound `:name` parameters are NULL here, which does not change the
+ * index the planner picks.
+ *
+ * Every plan is printed and written to `build/reports/query-plans/indexes-keyset-paging.txt`.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class ArticleQueryPlanTest {
+
+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+
+    private data class Case(val name: String, val sql: String, val index: List<String>, val noSort: Boolean)
+
+    private val cases = listOf(
+        Case("MAIN_LIST", ArticleSql.MAIN_LIST, listOf("index_article_deleted_at_archived_at_timeAdded"), noSort = true),
+        // Room's PagingSource wraps the list query this way for each page.
+        Case(
+            "MAIN_LIST (paged)",
+            "SELECT * FROM (${ArticleSql.MAIN_LIST}) LIMIT 50 OFFSET 0",
+            listOf("index_article_deleted_at_archived_at_timeAdded"),
+            noSort = true,
+        ),
+        Case("FAVORITE_LIST", ArticleSql.FAVORITE_LIST, listOf("index_article_deleted_at_archived_at_timeFavorited_timeAdded"), noSort = true),
+        Case("ARCHIVE_LIST", ArticleSql.ARCHIVE_LIST, listOf("index_article_deleted_at_archived_at_timeAdded"), noSort = true),
+        Case("TAG_LIST_BY_TAG", ArticleSql.TAG_LIST_BY_TAG, listOf("index_article_tags_tag", "index_article_resolvedId"), noSort = false),
+        Case("ALL_TAGS", ArticleSql.ALL_TAGS, listOf("index_article_resolvedId"), noSort = false),
+        Case("CHANGED_SINCE_KEYSET", ArticleSql.CHANGED_SINCE_KEYSET, listOf("index_article_timeUpdated_itemId"), noSort = true),
+        Case("COUNT_CHANGED_SINCE", ArticleSql.COUNT_CHANGED_SINCE, listOf("index_article_timeUpdated_itemId"), noSort = false),
+        Case("NEVER_BACKED_UP", ArticleSql.NEVER_BACKED_UP, listOf("index_article_backed_up_at_deleted_at_timeAdded"), noSort = false),
+        Case(
+            "COUNT_NEVER_BACKED_UP",
+            ArticleSql.COUNT_NEVER_BACKED_UP,
+            listOf("index_article_backed_up_at_deleted_at_timeAdded"),
+            noSort = false,
+        ),
+        Case("BY_RESOLVED_ID", ArticleSql.BY_RESOLVED_ID, listOf("index_article_resolvedId"), noSort = false),
+        Case("BY_NORMALIZED_URL", ArticleSql.BY_NORMALIZED_URL, listOf("index_article_normalized_url"), noSort = false),
+    )
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        db = JvmRoomDatabase.create(RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication())
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+    }
+
+    /** 200 articles and 300 tags, so the plans are those of a filled library. */
+    private suspend fun seed() {
+        val dao = db.articleDao()
+        dao.upsertArticles(
+            (1..200).map { n ->
+                Article(
+                    itemId = "a$n",
+                    resolvedId = "r$n",
+                    title = "Article $n",
+                    url = "https://example.com/a$n",
+                    normalizedUrl = "example.com/a$n",
+                    timeAdded = n * 10L,
+                    timeUpdated = n * 10L,
+                    timeFavorited = if (n % 10 == 0) n * 10L else 0,
+                    archivedAt = if (n % 7 == 0) n * 10L else null,
+                    deletedAt = if (n % 13 == 0) n * 10L else null,
+                    backedUpAt = if (n % 3 == 0) null else n * 10L,
+                )
+            }
+        )
+        dao.insertArticleTags(
+            (1..300).map { n -> ArticleTags(itemId = "a${n % 200 + 1}", tag = "tag${n % 15}", sortId = null, type = null) }
+        )
+    }
+
+    private suspend fun plan(sql: String): List<String> = db.useReaderConnection { connection ->
+        connection.usePrepared("EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN $sql") { s -> buildList { while (s.step()) add(s.getText(3)) } }
+    }
+
+    /** A step that reads `article` (aliases art, a) without an index, or a bare scan of it. */
+    private fun readsArticleWithoutIndex(line: String): Boolean {
+        val match = Regex("^(SCAN|SEARCH) (article|art|a)\\b").find(line) ?: return false
+        return match.value.isNotEmpty() && !line.contains("INDEX")
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun everyListAndSyncQuery_readsArticleThroughItsIndex() = runTest {
+        seed()
+
+        val failures = mutableListOf<String>()
+        val report = StringBuilder()
+        for (case in cases) {
+            val lines = plan(case.sql)
+            report.append("== ${case.name}\n")
+            lines.forEach { report.append("   $it\n") }
+            for (index in case.index) {
+                if (lines.none { it.contains(index) }) failures += "${case.name}: $index not used"
+            }
+            lines.filter(::readsArticleWithoutIndex).forEach { failures += "${case.name}: reads article without an index: $it" }
+            if (case.noSort) {
+                lines.filter { it.contains("USE TEMP B-TREE") }.forEach { failures += "${case.name}: sorts: $it" }
+            }
+        }
+
+        println(report)
+        val file = File("build/reports/query-plans/indexes-keyset-paging.txt")
+        file.parentFile?.mkdirs()
+        file.writeText(report.toString())
+
+        assertTrue(failures.joinToString("\n", prefix = "query plans:\n") + "\n\n$report", failures.isEmpty())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun readsArticleWithoutIndex_flagsScansOnly() {
+        assertTrue(readsArticleWithoutIndex("SCAN article"))
+        assertTrue(readsArticleWithoutIndex("SCAN art"))
+        assertTrue(readsArticleWithoutIndex("SEARCH a USING ROWID SEARCH ON TABLE article"))
+        assertTrue(!readsArticleWithoutIndex("SEARCH art USING INDEX index_article_resolvedId (resolvedId=?)"))
+        assertTrue(!readsArticleWithoutIndex("SCAN t USING COVERING INDEX index_article_tags_tag"))
+        assertTrue(!readsArticleWithoutIndex("SEARCH article_tags USING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_article_tags_1 (itemId=?)"))
+    }
+}

diff --git a/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/ArticleSql.kt b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/ArticleSql.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9e50740
--- /dev/null
+++ b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/ArticleSql.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,117 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database
+
+/**
+ * SQL of the list, tag, lookup and sync queries. [ArticleDao] declares each `@Query` with
+ * one of these constants, and the query-plan tests run the same text through
+ * `EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN` (`@Query` is not readable at run time, so the string is shared).
+ * The index each query relies on is named on [Article] and [com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleTags].
+ */
+internal object ArticleSql {
+
+    /** Library list: live, not archived, newest first. Tags come from a per-row subquery, so there is no GROUP BY. */
+    const val MAIN_LIST = """
+        SELECT art.itemId, art.title, COALESCE(art.url, art.givenUrl) AS url, art.image,
+        CASE WHEN art.favorite = '1' OR art.timeFavorited > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS favorite,
+        CASE WHEN art.timeRead IS NOT NULL AND art.timeRead > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS isRead,
+        (SELECT GROUP_CONCAT(t.tag) FROM article_tags AS t WHERE t.itemId = art.itemId) AS tagsString
+        FROM article AS art
+        WHERE art.archived_at IS NULL AND art.deleted_at IS NULL
+        ORDER BY art.timeAdded DESC
+    """
+
+    /** Favourite list: live, not archived, latest favourite first. */
+    const val FAVORITE_LIST = """
+        SELECT art.itemId, art.title, COALESCE(art.url, art.givenUrl) AS url, art.image,
+        CASE WHEN art.favorite = '1' OR art.timeFavorited > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS favorite,
+        CASE WHEN art.timeRead IS NOT NULL AND art.timeRead > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS isRead,
+        (SELECT GROUP_CONCAT(t.tag) FROM article_tags AS t WHERE t.itemId = art.itemId) AS tagsString
+        FROM article AS art
+        WHERE art.timeFavorited > 0 AND art.archived_at IS NULL AND art.deleted_at IS NULL
+        ORDER BY art.timeFavorited DESC, art.timeAdded DESC
+    """
+
+    /** Archive list: live and archived, latest archived first. */
+    const val ARCHIVE_LIST = """
+        SELECT art.itemId, art.title, COALESCE(art.url, art.givenUrl) AS url, art.image,
+        CASE WHEN art.favorite = '1' OR art.timeFavorited > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS favorite,
+        CASE WHEN art.timeRead IS NOT NULL AND art.timeRead > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS isRead,
+        (SELECT GROUP_CONCAT(t.tag) FROM article_tags AS t WHERE t.itemId = art.itemId) AS tagsString
+        FROM article AS art
+        WHERE art.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND art.deleted_at IS NULL
+        ORDER BY art.archived_at DESC, art.timeAdded DESC
+    """
+
+    /**
+     * Live articles carrying [tag] under their itemId or their resolvedId, newest first. The
+     * two matches are two indexed lookups joined by UNION, not one OR join across columns.
+     */
+    const val TAG_LIST_BY_TAG = """
+        SELECT art.itemId, art.title, COALESCE(art.url, art.givenUrl) AS url, art.image,
+        CASE WHEN art.favorite = '1' OR art.timeFavorited > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS favorite,
+        CASE WHEN art.timeRead IS NOT NULL AND art.timeRead > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS isRead,
+        (SELECT GROUP_CONCAT(t.tag) FROM article_tags AS t
+            WHERE t.itemId IN (art.itemId, art.resolvedId)) AS tagsString
+        FROM article AS art
+        WHERE art.deleted_at IS NULL
+          AND art.itemId IN (
+            SELECT st.itemId FROM article_tags AS st WHERE st.tag = :tag
+            UNION
+            SELECT a.itemId FROM article_tags AS st JOIN article AS a ON a.resolvedId = st.itemId
+            WHERE st.tag = :tag)
+        ORDER BY art.timeAdded DESC
+    """
+
+    /**
+     * Tags with at least one non-deleted article, matched by itemId or resolvedId, so the
+     * list matches [TAG_LIST_BY_TAG]. Reading `article` also makes the Flow re-emit on deletes.
+     */
+    const val ALL_TAGS = """
+        SELECT t.tag
+        FROM article_tags AS t
+        WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM article AS a WHERE a.itemId = t.itemId AND a.deleted_at IS NULL)
+           OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM article AS a WHERE a.resolvedId = t.itemId AND a.deleted_at IS NULL)
+        GROUP BY t.tag
+        ORDER BY LOWER(t.tag)
+    """
+
+    /**
+     * One keyset page of the push window (since, until]: rows after the cursor
+     * (afterTimeUpdated, afterItemId) in (timeUpdated, itemId) order. Includes deleted rows
+     * (a deletion is pushed as `deletedAt`); skips rows at MAX_SYNC_FAILURES. No OFFSET:
+     * a row that changes or drops out during the run moves no later page.
+     */
+    const val CHANGED_SINCE_KEYSET = """
+        SELECT * FROM article
+        WHERE timeUpdated > :since AND timeUpdated <= :until
+          AND sync_failures < $MAX_SYNC_FAILURES
+          AND (timeUpdated, itemId) > (:afterTimeUpdated, :afterItemId)
+        ORDER BY timeUpdated ASC, itemId ASC
+        LIMIT :limit
+    """
+
+    /** Rows in the push window (since, until], as [CHANGED_SINCE_KEYSET] selects them. */
+    const val COUNT_CHANGED_SINCE = """
+        SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article
+        WHERE timeUpdated > :since AND timeUpdated <= :until
+          AND sync_failures < $MAX_SYNC_FAILURES
+    """
+
+    /**
+     * The oldest never-backed-up live rows, first page only: each sweep stamps the rows it
+     * pushes, so they leave the predicate and the next first page is the next batch.
+     */
+    const val NEVER_BACKED_UP = """
+        SELECT * FROM article
+        WHERE backed_up_at IS NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL AND sync_failures < $MAX_SYNC_FAILURES
+        ORDER BY timeAdded ASC
+        LIMIT :limit
+    """
+
+    /** Rows matching [NEVER_BACKED_UP]'s predicate. */
+    const val COUNT_NEVER_BACKED_UP =
+        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article WHERE backed_up_at IS NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL AND sync_failures < $MAX_SYNC_FAILURES"
+
+    const val BY_RESOLVED_ID = "SELECT * FROM article WHERE resolvedId = :resolvedId ORDER BY timeAdded DESC LIMIT 1"
+
+    const val BY_NORMALIZED_URL = "SELECT * FROM article WHERE normalized_url = :normalizedUrl LIMIT 1"
+}

+val MIGRATION_8_9 = object : Migration(8, 9) {
+    override suspend fun migrate(connection: SQLiteConnection) {
+        // Adds indexes only; no row changes. Each statement is the createSql Room
+        // exports for version 9, so the migrated tables match the declared indexes.
+        connection.execSQL("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS `index_article_deleted_at_archived_at_timeAdded` ON `article` (`deleted_at`, `archived_at`, `timeAdded`)")

+/**
+ * Indexes, each for named queries (equality columns first, the ORDER BY column last):
+ * - deleted_at, archived_at, timeAdded: the library list and the archive list.
+ * - timeFavorited, timeAdded: the favourite list.
+ * - timeUpdated, itemId: the sync push pages and their count (keyset order).
+ * - backed_up_at, deleted_at, timeAdded: the never-backed-up sweep and its count.
+ * - resolvedId: the tag view and tag list matched by resolved id, getArticleByResolvedId.
+ * - normalized_url: getArticleByNormalizedUrl (the save dedup). Not unique: existing
+ *   libraries may hold duplicates.
+ */

diff --git a/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/ListQueriesTest.kt b/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/ListQueriesTest.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..10edb4e

+++ b/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/ListQueriesTest.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,151 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database
+
+import android.app.Application
+import androidx.paging.PagingSource
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.models.ArticleItem
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleTags
+import com.jayteealao.trails.testing.JvmRoomDatabase
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+
+/**
+ * Pins what the library, favourite, archive and tag lists return (ids in order and each
+ * row's tags) on a real in-memory database, so a rewrite of their SQL keeps the results.
+ *
+ * Seed: a1…a8 with timeAdded 100…800; a2 archived; a3 deleted; a4 and a5 favourite
+ * (a4 favourited later, so it sorts first although it was added earlier); a6 carries
+ * resolvedId r6. Tags: x on a1, a2, a3 and on r6 (keyed by the resolved id); y on a1 and a4.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class ListQueriesTest {
+
+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+    private lateinit var dao: ArticleDao
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        db = JvmRoomDatabase.create(RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication())
+        dao = db.articleDao()
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+    }
+
+    private fun article(n: Int, block: (Article) -> Article = { it }) = block(
+        Article(
+            itemId = "a$n",
+            title = "Article $n",
+            url = "https://example.com/a$n",
+            timeAdded = n * 100L,
+            timeUpdated = n * 100L,
+        )
+    )
+
+    private fun tag(itemId: String, tag: String) = ArticleTags(itemId = itemId, tag = tag, sortId = null, type = null)
+
+    private suspend fun seed() {
+        dao.upsertArticles(
+            listOf(
+                article(1),
+                article(2) { it.copy(archivedAt = 2_000L) },
+                article(3) { it.copy(deletedAt = 3_000L) },
+                article(4) { it.copy(favorite = "1", timeFavorited = 2_000L) },
+                article(5) { it.copy(favorite = "1", timeFavorited = 1_000L) },
+                article(6) { it.copy(resolvedId = "r6") },
+                article(7),
+                article(8),
+            )
+        )
+        dao.insertArticleTags(
+            listOf(
+                tag("a1", "x"), tag("a2", "x"), tag("a3", "x"), tag("r6", "x"),
+                tag("a1", "y"), tag("a4", "y"),
+            )
+        )
+    }
+
+    private suspend fun PagingSource<Int, ArticleItem>.firstPage(): List<ArticleItem> {
+        val result = load(PagingSource.LoadParams.Refresh(key = null, loadSize = 50, placeholdersEnabled = false))
+        return (result as PagingSource.LoadResult.Page).data
+    }
+
+    /** Ids in list order, each with its tags as a set (GROUP_CONCAT order is not defined). */
+    private fun List<ArticleItem>.idsAndTags(): List<Pair<String, Set<String>>> =
+        map { item -> item.itemId to item.tagsString.orEmpty().split(',').filter { it.isNotEmpty() }.toSet() }
+
+    @Test
+    fun mainList_liveUnarchivedNewestFirst() = runTest {
+        seed()
+
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                "a8" to emptySet(),
+                "a7" to emptySet(),
+                "a6" to emptySet(),
+                "a5" to emptySet(),
+                "a4" to setOf("y"),
+                "a1" to setOf("x", "y"),
+            ),
+            dao.getArticlesWithTags().firstPage().idsAndTags(),
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun favorites_latestFavouriteFirst() = runTest {
+        seed()
+
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf("a4" to setOf("y"), "a5" to emptySet()),
+            dao.getFavoriteArticlesWithTags().firstPage().idsAndTags(),
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun archive_archivedLiveOnly() = runTest {

commit 7b15ab4c809bca9d09297910ac91aaa67dbc6cb8
Author: jayteealao <jayteealao@gmail.com>
Date:   Fri Oct 23 17:11:47 2026 +0100

    fix(android): let the wipe-check emulator script open its database copy under Git Bash
    
    The script turns off MSYS path conversion, so its default work folder
    reached the native sqlite3 as /c/... and every snapshot, strike and
    seed-archive run failed to open the pulled database. The default now
    uses the C:/... form from `pwd -W` when it is available.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

@@ -38,7 +38,8 @@ dev() { adb -s "$serial" "$@"; }
 
 # Working copies live in a folder beside the script's output (or the current folder): a
 # native sqlite3 on Windows cannot open /tmp paths.
-work_root=${WIPE_CHECK_WORK:-$PWD}
+# Under Git Bash, `pwd -W` gives the C:/... form that a native sqlite3 can open.
+work_root=${WIPE_CHECK_WORK:-$(pwd -W 2>/dev/null || pwd)}
 work="$work_root/.wipe-check-work"
 mkdir -p "$work"
 trap 'rm -rf "$work"' EXIT

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.account
+
+import android.app.Application
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.SharedPreferencesManager
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+
+/**
+ * The owner of the local library, stored in the app's real preferences: no owner adopts,
+ * the same uid keeps, another uid replaces; clearing the owner or all preferences forgets it.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class LocalDataOwnerTest {
+
+    private lateinit var prefs: SharedPreferencesManager
+    private lateinit var owner: LocalDataOwner
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        prefs = SharedPreferencesManager(RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication())
+        prefs.clear()
+        owner = LocalDataOwner(prefs)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun noOwner_adoptsAndOwnsEveryUid() {
+        assertNull(owner.uid())
+        assertEquals(OwnerDecision.ADOPT, owner.decide("a"))
+        assertTrue(owner.isOwnedBy("a"))
+        assertTrue(owner.isOwnedBy("b"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun sameUid_isSameOwner_otherUid_isDifferentOwner() {
+        owner.adopt("a", "a@example.com")
+
+        assertEquals("a", owner.uid())
+        assertEquals("a@example.com", owner.label())
+        assertEquals(OwnerDecision.SAME_OWNER, owner.decide("a"))
+        assertEquals(OwnerDecision.DIFFERENT_OWNER, owner.decide("b"))
+        assertTrue(owner.isOwnedBy("a"))
+        assertFalse(owner.isOwnedBy("b"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun guestOwner_hasNoLabel_andAdoptingWithoutLabelDropsTheOldOne() {
+        owner.adopt("a", "a@example.com")
+        owner.adopt("g", null)
+
+        assertEquals("g", owner.uid())
+        assertNull(owner.label())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun clear_forgetsTheOwner() {
+        owner.adopt("a", "a@example.com")
+
+        owner.clear()
+
+        assertNull(owner.uid())
+        assertNull(owner.label())
+        assertEquals(OwnerDecision.ADOPT, owner.decide("b"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun clearingAllPreferences_forgetsTheOwner() {
+        owner.adopt("a", "a@example.com")
+
+        prefs.clear()
+
+        assertNull(owner.uid())
+        assertNull(owner.label())
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.account

+import android.app.Application

+/**
+ * The device side of "Clear data" on a real in-memory database: the wipe empties every
+ * table and the search index, deletes the archive folder and cancels the sync and restore
+ * work; the unsynced count counts exactly the rows Firestore has not confirmed; a push
+ * counts as done only on SUCCESS.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class LocalDataResetTest {
+
+    private lateinit var context: Context
+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+    private lateinit var dao: ArticleDao
+    private lateinit var syncManager: FirestoreSyncManager
+    private lateinit var reset: LocalDataReset
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        context = RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication()
+        WorkManagerTestInitHelper.initializeTestWorkManager(
+            context,
+            Configuration.Builder().setExecutor(SynchronousExecutor()).build()
+        )
+        db = JvmRoomDatabase.create(context)
+        dao = db.articleDao()
+        syncManager = mockk()
+        reset = LocalDataReset(context, dao, syncManager)
+        File(context.filesDir, LocalDataReset.ARCHIVES_DIR).deleteRecursively()
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+        File(context.filesDir, LocalDataReset.ARCHIVES_DIR).deleteRecursively()
+    }
+
+    private fun article(
+        itemId: String,
+        title: String = "Article $itemId",
+        timeUpdated: Long = 2_000,
+        backedUpAt: Long? = null,
+        deletedAt: Long? = null,
+        syncFailures: Int = 0,
+    ) = Article(
+        itemId = itemId,
+        title = title,
+        url = "https://example.com/$itemId",
+        timeAdded = 1_000,
+        timeUpdated = timeUpdated,
+        backedUpAt = backedUpAt,
+        deletedAt = deletedAt,
+        syncFailures = syncFailures,
+    )
+
+    private suspend fun count(sql: String): Long = db.useReaderConnection { connection ->
+        connection.usePrepared(sql) { stmt ->
+            stmt.step()
+            stmt.getLong(0)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun wipe_emptiesEveryTableAndSearchIndex() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(
+            listOf(
+                article("a1", title = "The river bank"),
+                article("a2", title = "Down the river"),
+                article("a3", title = "River stones"),
+            )
+        )
+        db.useWriterConnection { connection ->
+            connection.executeSQL("INSERT INTO article_tags (itemId, tag, sortId, type) VALUES ('a1', 'water', 1, 'user')")
+            connection.executeSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO article_images (imageId, itemId, src, width, height, credit, caption) " +
+                    "VALUES ('i1', 'a1', 'https://example.com/i.png', 1, 1, '', '')"
+            )
+            connection.executeSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO article_videos (videoId, itemId, src, width, height, type, vid) " +
+                    "VALUES ('v1', 'a1', 'https://example.com/v.mp4', 1, 1, 'mp4', 'v1')"
+            )
+            connection.executeSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO article_authors (authorId, itemId, name, url) VALUES ('au1', 'a1', 'Ann', 'https://example.com/ann')"
+            )
+            connection.executeSQL("INSERT INTO DomainMetadata (name, logo, domain) VALUES ('Example', NULL, 'example.com')")
+            connection.executeSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO local_archive (itemId, archiveKey, localPath, compressedSizeBytes, originalSizeBytes, downloadedAt, status) " +
+                    "VALUES ('a1', 'readability', 'archives/a1/readability.gz', 10, 20, 1000, 'complete')"
+            )
+        }
+        val archives = File(context.filesDir, LocalDataReset.ARCHIVES_DIR)
+        File(archives, "a1").mkdirs()
+        File(archives, "a1/readability.gz").writeText("archived page")
+        val workManager = WorkManager.getInstance(context)
+        val syncRequest = FirestoreSyncWorker.startUpSyncWork()
+        workManager.enqueueUniqueWork("FirestoreBackupWork", ExistingWorkPolicy.KEEP, syncRequest).result.get()
+        val restoreRequest = FirestoreRestoreWorker.startUpRestoreWork()
+        workManager.enqueue(restoreRequest).result.get()
+        assertEquals(3L, count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article_fts WHERE article_fts MATCH 'river'"))
+
+        reset.wipeLocalData()
+
+        for (table in TABLES) assertEquals("rows in $table", 0L, count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $table"))
+        assertTrue(dao.searchArticlesWithMatchInfo("*\"*river*\"*").isEmpty())
+        assertEquals(0L, count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article_fts WHERE article_fts MATCH 'river'"))
+        assertFalse(archives.exists())
+        assertEquals(WorkInfo.State.CANCELLED, workManager.getWorkInfoById(syncRequest.id).get()!!.state)
+        assertEquals(WorkInfo.State.CANCELLED, workManager.getWorkInfoById(restoreRequest.id).get()!!.state)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun wipe_withoutArchiveFolder_stillEmptiesTables() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("a1")))
+
+        reset.wipeLocalData()
+
+        assertEquals(0L, count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun unsyncedCount_neverBackedUp() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("a", backedUpAt = null)))
+        assertEquals(1, reset.pendingChangeCount())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun unsyncedCount_editedAfterBackup() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("a", timeUpdated = 3_000, backedUpAt = 2_000)))
+        assertEquals(1, reset.pendingChangeCount())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun unsyncedCount_deletedAndNotPushed() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("a", timeUpdated = 3_000, backedUpAt = 2_000, deletedAt = 3_000)))
+        assertEquals(1, reset.pendingChangeCount())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun unsyncedCount_skippedAfterThreeFailures() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(
+            listOf(article("a", timeUpdated = 3_000, backedUpAt = 2_000, syncFailures = MAX_SYNC_FAILURES))
+        )
+        assertEquals(1, reset.pendingChangeCount())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun unsyncedCount_stampedAtOrAfterEdit_isZero() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(
+            listOf(
+                article("same", timeUpdated = 2_000, backedUpAt = 2_000),
+                article("later", timeUpdated = 2_000, backedUpAt = 5_000),
+            )
+        )
+        assertEquals(0, reset.pendingChangeCount())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun unsyncedCount_afterPushStamp_isZero() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("a"), article("b", timeUpdated = 3_000, backedUpAt = 1_000)))
+        assertEquals(2, reset.pendingChangeCount())
+
+        dao.recordPushResults(listOf("a", "b"), emptyList(), 9_000)
+
+        assertEquals(0, reset.pendingChangeCount())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun push_successOnlyCountsAsDone() = runTest {
+        coEvery { syncManager.pushLocalChanges() } returns SyncRunResult(2, 0)
+        assertTrue(reset.pushPendingChanges())
+
+        coEvery { syncManager.pushLocalChanges() } returns SyncRunResult(1, 1)
+        assertFalse(reset.pushPendingChanges())
+
+        coEvery { syncManager.pushLocalChanges() } returns SyncRunResult(0, 0, interruptedBy = SyncTimeoutException(120_000))
+        assertFalse(reset.pushPendingChanges())
+
+        coEvery { syncManager.pushLocalChanges() } returns SyncRunResult(0, 0, noUser = true)
+        assertFalse(reset.pushPendingChanges())
+
+        coEvery { syncManager.pushLocalChanges() } throws IllegalStateException("offline")
+        assertFalse(reset.pushPendingChanges())
+    }
+
+    private companion object {
+        val TABLES = listOf(
+            "article", "article_tags", "article_images", "article_videos",
+            "article_authors", "DomainMetadata", "local_archive",
+        )
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.account
+
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.account.UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Hidden
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.account.UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.account.UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Pushing
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.account.UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Wiping
+import io.mockk.coEvery
+import io.mockk.coVerify
+import io.mockk.coVerifyOrder
+import io.mockk.mockk
+import kotlinx.coroutines.CompletableDeferred
+import kotlinx.coroutines.async
+import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.advanceUntilIdle
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.currentTime
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runCurrent
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import kotlin.time.Duration.Companion.seconds
+
+/**
+ * The push-then-wipe sequence: the wipe never runs before the push attempt, and after a
+ * failed, timed-out or skipped push never before the person confirms.
+ */
+class PushThenWipeTest {
+
+    private lateinit var reset: LocalDataReset
+    private lateinit var sequence: PushThenWipe
+    private val states = mutableListOf<UnsyncedChangesDialogState>()
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        reset = mockk()
+        coEvery { reset.wipeLocalData() } returns Unit
+        sequence = PushThenWipe(reset)
+    }
+
+    private suspend fun run(
+        canPush: Boolean = true,
+        reason: WipeReason = WipeReason.CLEAR_DATA,
+        confirm: suspend (PushFailed) -> Boolean = { error("confirm must not be asked") },
+    ) = sequence.run(reason, LABEL, canPush, onState = { states += it }, confirm = confirm)
+
+    @Test
+    fun nothingPending_wipesWithoutPushOrQuestion() = runTest {
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returns 0
+
+        val result = run()
+
+        assertEquals(PushThenWipeResult.WIPED, result)
+        assertEquals(listOf(Wiping, Hidden), states)
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.pushPendingChanges() }
+        coVerify(exactly = 1) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun pushSucceeds_pushesThenWipes() = runTest {
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returns 5
+        coEvery { reset.pushPendingChanges() } returns true
+
+        val result = run()
+
+        assertEquals(PushThenWipeResult.WIPED, result)
+        assertEquals(listOf(Pushing(5), Wiping, Hidden), states)
+        coVerifyOrder {
+            reset.pushPendingChanges()
+            reset.wipeLocalData()
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun pushFails_asks_andKeepingWipesNothing() = runTest {
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returnsMany listOf(5, 3)
+        coEvery { reset.pushPendingChanges() } returns false
+        var asked: PushFailed? = null
+
+        val result = run(confirm = {
+            asked = it
+            coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+            false
+        })
+
+        assertEquals(PushThenWipeResult.KEPT, result)
+        assertEquals(PushFailed(3, WipeReason.CLEAR_DATA, LABEL), asked)
+        assertEquals(listOf(Pushing(5), PushFailed(3, WipeReason.CLEAR_DATA, LABEL), Hidden), states)
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun pushFails_confirmingWipesOnce() = runTest {
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returnsMany listOf(5, 3)
+        coEvery { reset.pushPendingChanges() } returns false
+
+        val result = run(confirm = {
+            coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+            true
+        })
+
+        assertEquals(PushThenWipeResult.WIPED, result)
+        assertEquals(listOf(Pushing(5), PushFailed(3, WipeReason.CLEAR_DATA, LABEL), Wiping, Hidden), states)
+        coVerify(exactly = 1) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun pushTakesTooLong_asksAfter15Seconds_andWaitsForTheAnswer() = runTest {
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returnsMany listOf(5, 4)
+        coEvery { reset.pushPendingChanges() } coAnswers {
+            delay(20.seconds)
+            true
+        }
+        val answer = CompletableDeferred<Boolean>()
+        var askedAt = -1L
+
+        val run = async {
+            run(confirm = {
+                askedAt = currentTime
+                answer.await()
+            })
+        }
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertEquals(15_000L, askedAt)
+        assertEquals(PushFailed(4, WipeReason.CLEAR_DATA, LABEL), states.last())
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+
+        answer.complete(true)
+        assertEquals(PushThenWipeResult.WIPED, run.await())
+        coVerify(exactly = 1) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun cannotPush_asksWithoutPushing() = runTest {
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returns 3
+
+        val result = run(canPush = false, reason = WipeReason.ACCOUNT_SWITCH, confirm = { false })
+
+        assertEquals(PushThenWipeResult.KEPT, result)
+        assertEquals(listOf(PushFailed(3, WipeReason.ACCOUNT_SWITCH, LABEL), Hidden), states)
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.pushPendingChanges() }
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun failedPushThatLeftNothing_wipesWithoutQuestion() = runTest {
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returnsMany listOf(5, 0)
+        coEvery { reset.pushPendingChanges() } returns false
+
+        val result = run()
+
+        assertEquals(PushThenWipeResult.WIPED, result)
+        assertFalse(states.any { it is PushFailed })
+        coVerify(exactly = 1) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun cancelWhilePushing_wipesNothing_andHidesTheDialog() = runTest {
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returns 5
+        coEvery { reset.pushPendingChanges() } coAnswers {
+            delay(10.seconds)
+            true
+        }
+
+        val job = async { run() }
+        runCurrent() // reaches the push, which waits on virtual time
+        assertEquals(listOf<UnsyncedChangesDialogState>(Pushing(5)), states)
+
+        job.cancel()
+        runCurrent()
+
+        assertTrue(job.isCancelled)
+        assertEquals(Hidden, states.last())
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun unsyncedChangesText_pluralsAndCap() {
+        assertEquals("1 change", unsyncedChangesText(1))
+        assertEquals("42 changes", unsyncedChangesText(42))
+        assertEquals("999 changes", unsyncedChangesText(999))
+        assertEquals("More than 999 changes", unsyncedChangesText(1000))
+    }
+
+    private companion object {
+        const val LABEL = "a@example.com"
+    }
+}

+import android.app.Application

+/**
+ * Sign-in and the owner of the local library: the first account adopts it, the same uid
+ * (a link included) keeps it, and another account gets it only after the device is
+ * cleared; with changes not backed up the person decides, and keeping them signs the new
+ * account out. Nothing is ever pushed as the new account.
+ */
+@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class AuthViewModelOwnerTest {
+
+    private val dispatcher = StandardTestDispatcher()
+    private lateinit var authRepository: AuthRepository
+    private lateinit var owner: LocalDataOwner
+    private lateinit var reset: LocalDataReset
+    private val credential = mockk<AuthCredential>()
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        Dispatchers.setMain(dispatcher)
+        val prefs = SharedPreferencesManager(RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication())
+        prefs.clear()
+        owner = LocalDataOwner(prefs)
+        authRepository = mockk(relaxed = true)
+        every { authRepository.getCurrentUser() } returns null
+        reset = mockk()
+        coEvery { reset.wipeLocalData() } returns Unit
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        Dispatchers.resetMain()
+    }
+
+    private fun user(uid: String, email: String?) = mockk<FirebaseUser>().also {
+        every { it.uid } returns uid
+        every { it.email } returns email
+    }
+
+    private fun result(user: FirebaseUser) = mockk<AuthResult>().also { every { it.user } returns user }
+
+    private fun viewModel() = AuthViewModel(authRepository, owner, PushThenWipe(reset))
+
+    @Test
+    fun firstSignIn_adoptsTheLibrary() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        val a = user("a", "a@example.com")
+        coEvery { authRepository.signInWithCredential(credential) } returns result(a)
+        val vm = viewModel()
+
+        vm.signInWithCredential(credential)
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertEquals("a", owner.uid())
+        assertEquals("a@example.com", owner.label())
+        assertTrue(vm.state.value is AuthUiState.SignedIn)
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.pendingChangeCount() }
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun sameAccount_keepsTheLibrary() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        owner.adopt("a", "a@example.com")
+        coEvery { authRepository.signInWithCredential(credential) } returns result(user("a", "a@example.com"))
+        val vm = viewModel()
+
+        vm.signInWithCredential(credential)
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertTrue(vm.state.value is AuthUiState.SignedIn)
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.pendingChangeCount() }
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun guestLinkedToGoogle_keepsTheLibraryAndTakesTheEmail() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        owner.adopt("a", null)
+        coEvery { authRepository.linkWithCredential(credential) } returns result(user("a", "a@example.com"))
+        val vm = viewModel()
+
+        vm.linkWithCredential(credential)
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertTrue(vm.state.value is AuthUiState.SignedIn)
+        assertEquals("a", owner.uid())
+        assertEquals("a@example.com", owner.label())
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun otherAccount_nothingPending_clearsBeforeHandingOver() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        owner.adopt("a", "a@example.com")
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returns 0
+        val vm = viewModel()
+        var ownerAtWipe: String? = null
+        var stateAtWipe: AuthUiState? = null
+        coEvery { reset.wipeLocalData() } answers {
+            ownerAtWipe = owner.uid()
+            stateAtWipe = vm.state.value
+        }
+        coEvery { authRepository.signInAnonymously() } returns result(user("b", null))
+
+        vm.signInAnonymously()
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertEquals("a", ownerAtWipe)
+        assertEquals(AuthUiState.Loading, stateAtWipe)
+        assertEquals("b", owner.uid())
+        assertNull(owner.label())
+        assertTrue(vm.state.value is AuthUiState.SignedIn)
+        coVerify(exactly = 1) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.pushPendingChanges() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun otherAccount_changesPending_asks_andKeepingSignsTheNewAccountOut() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        owner.adopt("a", "a@example.com")
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returns 3
+        coEvery { authRepository.signInWithCredential(credential) } returns result(user("b", "b@example.com"))
+        val vm = viewModel()
+
+        vm.signInWithCredential(credential)
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertEquals(
+            UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(3, WipeReason.ACCOUNT_SWITCH, "a@example.com"),
+            vm.unsyncedChangesDialog.value,
+        )
+        assertEquals(AuthUiState.Loading, vm.state.value)
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+
+        vm.keepPreviousAccountData()
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        verify(exactly = 1) { authRepository.signOut() }
+        assertEquals(AuthUiState.SignedOut, vm.state.value)
+        assertEquals(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Hidden, vm.unsyncedChangesDialog.value)
+        assertEquals("a", owner.uid())
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.pushPendingChanges() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun otherAccount_changesPending_switchAnywayClearsAndAdopts() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        owner.adopt("a", "a@example.com")
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returns 3
+        coEvery { authRepository.signInWithCredential(credential) } returns result(user("b", "b@example.com"))
+        val vm = viewModel()
+
+        vm.signInWithCredential(credential)
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+        vm.switchAccountAnyway()
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        coVerify(exactly = 1) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+        assertEquals("b", owner.uid())
+        assertEquals("b@example.com", owner.label())
+        assertTrue(vm.state.value is AuthUiState.SignedIn)
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.pushPendingChanges() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun start_withAnotherAccountSignedIn_signsItOut() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        owner.adopt("a", "a@example.com")
+        every { authRepository.getCurrentUser() } returns user("b", "b@example.com")
+
+        val vm = viewModel()
+
+        verify(exactly = 1) { authRepository.signOut() }
+        assertEquals(AuthUiState.SignedOut, vm.state.value)
+        assertEquals("a", owner.uid())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun start_withNoOwner_adoptsTheSignedInAccount() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        every { authRepository.getCurrentUser() } returns user("a", "a@example.com")
+
+        val vm = viewModel()
+
+        assertEquals("a", owner.uid())
+        assertTrue(vm.state.value is AuthUiState.SignedIn)
+        verify(exactly = 0) { authRepository.signOut() }
+    }

+/**
+ * "Logout only" and "Clear data" from the Settings view model down to a real in-memory
+ * database and the real archive folder: logging out keeps every row and file; clearing
+ * with nothing pending empties every table and deletes the folder without asking.
+ */
+@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class LogoutWipeIntegrationTest {
+
+    private lateinit var context: Context
+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+    private lateinit var prefs: SharedPreferencesManager
+    private lateinit var authRepository: AuthRepository
+    private lateinit var archives: File
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        Dispatchers.setMain(UnconfinedTestDispatcher())
+        context = RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication()
+        WorkManagerTestInitHelper.initializeTestWorkManager(
+            context,
+            Configuration.Builder().setExecutor(SynchronousExecutor()).build()
+        )
+        db = JvmRoomDatabase.create(context)
+        prefs = SharedPreferencesManager(context)
+        prefs.clear()
+        authRepository = mockk(relaxed = true)
+        val user = mockk<FirebaseUser>().also {
+            every { it.uid } returns "owner-a"
+            every { it.email } returns "a@example.com"
+            every { it.isAnonymous } returns false
+        }
+        every { authRepository.getCurrentUser() } returns user
+        archives = File(context.filesDir, LocalDataReset.ARCHIVES_DIR)
+        archives.deleteRecursively()
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+        archives.deleteRecursively()
+        Dispatchers.resetMain()
+    }
+
+    private fun viewModel(): SettingsViewModel {
+        val syncManager = mockk<FirestoreSyncManager>()
+        every { syncManager.isSyncing } returns MutableStateFlow(false)
+        every { syncManager.lastSyncTime } returns MutableStateFlow(0L)
+        every { syncManager.syncStatus } returns MutableStateFlow<SyncStatus>(SyncStatus.Idle)
+        every { syncManager.lastError } returns MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
+        coEvery { syncManager.pushLocalChanges() } returns SyncRunResult(0, 0)
+        val owner = LocalDataOwner(prefs).also { it.adopt("owner-a", "a@example.com") }
+        val reset = LocalDataReset(context, db.articleDao(), syncManager)
+        return SettingsViewModel(
+            articleDao = db.articleDao(),
+            sharedPreferencesManager = prefs,
+            firestoreSyncManager = syncManager,
+            authRepository = authRepository,
+            ioDispatcher = Dispatchers.IO,
+            pushThenWipe = PushThenWipe(reset),
+            localDataOwner = owner,
+        )
+    }
+
+    /** Every table seeded, every article stamped as backed up, one archive file on disk. */
+    private suspend fun seed() {
+        db.articleDao().upsertArticles(
+            listOf("a1", "a2").map {
+                Article(
+                    itemId = it,
+                    title = "River $it",
+                    url = "https://example.com/$it",
+                    timeAdded = 1_000,
+                    timeUpdated = 2_000,
+                    backedUpAt = 2_000,
+                )
+            }
+        )
+        db.useWriterConnection { connection ->
+            connection.executeSQL("INSERT INTO article_tags (itemId, tag) VALUES ('a1', 'water')")
+            connection.executeSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO article_images (imageId, itemId, src, width, height, credit, caption) " +
+                    "VALUES ('i1', 'a1', 'https://example.com/i.png', 1, 1, '', '')"
+            )
+            connection.executeSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO article_videos (videoId, itemId, src, width, height, type, vid) " +
+                    "VALUES ('v1', 'a1', 'https://example.com/v.mp4', 1, 1, 'mp4', 'v1')"
+            )
+            connection.executeSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO article_authors (authorId, itemId, name, url) VALUES ('au1', 'a1', 'Ann', 'https://example.com/ann')"
+            )
+            connection.executeSQL("INSERT INTO DomainMetadata (name, domain) VALUES ('Example', 'example.com')")
+            connection.executeSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO local_archive (itemId, archiveKey, localPath, compressedSizeBytes, originalSizeBytes, downloadedAt, status) " +
+                    "VALUES ('a1', 'readability', 'archives/a1/readability.gz', 10, 20, 1000, 'complete')"
+            )
+        }
+        File(archives, "a1").mkdirs()
+        File(archives, "a1/readability.gz").writeText("archived page")
+    }
+
+    private suspend fun counts(): Map<String, Long> = db.useReaderConnection { connection ->
+        TABLES.associateWith { table ->
+            connection.usePrepared("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $table") { stmt ->
+                stmt.step()
+                stmt.getLong(0)
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private fun archiveListing(): List<String> =
+        archives.walkTopDown().map { it.relativeTo(context.filesDir).path.replace('\\', '/') }.sorted().toList()
+
+    @Test
+    fun logoutOnly_keepsEveryRowAndFile_clearDataEmptiesEverything() = runTest {
+        seed()
+        val before = counts()
+        val filesBefore = archiveListing()
+        assertTrue(before.values.all { it > 0 })
+        val vm = viewModel()
+        val events = Channel<SettingsEvent>(Channel.UNLIMITED)
+        val dialogs = mutableListOf<Any>()
+        backgroundScope.launch(UnconfinedTestDispatcher(testScheduler)) { vm.event.collect { events.send(it) } }
+        backgroundScope.launch(UnconfinedTestDispatcher(testScheduler)) {
+            vm.state.collect { dialogs += it.unsyncedChangesDialog }
+        }
+
+        vm.logout(clearData = false)
+        awaitLoggedOut(events)
+
+        assertEquals(before, counts())
+        assertEquals(filesBefore, archiveListing())
+
+        vm.logout(clearData = true)
+        awaitLoggedOut(events)
+
+        assertTrue(counts().values.all { it == 0L })
+        assertFalse(archives.exists())
+        // Nothing was pending, so the person was never asked (a question would block the run).
+        assertTrue(dialogs.none { it is com.jayteealao.trails.data.account.UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed })
+        verify(exactly = 2) { authRepository.signOut() }
+    }
+
+    /** Room and the view model work on real threads here, so wait in real time. */
+    private suspend fun awaitLoggedOut(events: Channel<SettingsEvent>) = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
+        withTimeout(10_000) {
+            while (events.receive() != SettingsEvent.LoggedOut) Unit
+        }
+    }
+
+    private companion object {
+        val TABLES = listOf(
+            "article", "article_tags", "article_images", "article_videos",
+            "article_authors", "DomainMetadata", "local_archive",
+        )
+    }
+}

+/**
+ * Settings logout, "Clear data" and the sync-failure Retry, over the real push-then-wipe
+ * sequence with the device operations mocked: "Logout only" clears nothing; "Clear data"
+ * asks when a push fails, keeps everything on "Keep data", and clears, forgets preferences
+ * and signs out in that order on "Clear anyway".
+ */
+@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
+class SettingsViewModelTest {
+
+    private val dispatcher = StandardTestDispatcher()
+    private lateinit var articleDao: ArticleDao
+    private lateinit var prefs: SharedPreferencesManager
+    private lateinit var syncManager: FirestoreSyncManager
+    private lateinit var authRepository: AuthRepository
+    private lateinit var reset: LocalDataReset
+    private lateinit var owner: LocalDataOwner
+    private val failureCount = MutableStateFlow(0)
+    private val user = mockk<FirebaseUser>().also {
+        every { it.uid } returns "owner-a"
+        every { it.email } returns "a@example.com"
+        every { it.isAnonymous } returns false
+    }
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        Dispatchers.setMain(dispatcher)
+        articleDao = mockk()
+        every { articleDao.observeSyncFailureCount() } returns failureCount
+        prefs = mockk(relaxed = true)
+        every { prefs.booleanFlow(any(), any()) } returns flowOf(false)
+        every { prefs.getBoolean(any(), any()) } returns false
+        syncManager = mockk()
+        every { syncManager.isSyncing } returns MutableStateFlow(false)
+        every { syncManager.lastSyncTime } returns MutableStateFlow(0L)
+        every { syncManager.syncStatus } returns MutableStateFlow<SyncStatus>(SyncStatus.Idle)
+        every { syncManager.lastError } returns MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
+        authRepository = mockk(relaxed = true)
+        every { authRepository.getCurrentUser() } returns user
+        reset = mockk()
+        coEvery { reset.wipeLocalData() } returns Unit
+        owner = mockk()
+        every { owner.label() } returns "a@example.com"
+        every { owner.isOwnedBy("owner-a") } returns true
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        Dispatchers.resetMain()
+    }
+
+    private fun viewModel() = SettingsViewModel(
+        articleDao = articleDao,
+        sharedPreferencesManager = prefs,
+        firestoreSyncManager = syncManager,
+        authRepository = authRepository,
+        ioDispatcher = dispatcher,
+        pushThenWipe = PushThenWipe(reset),
+        localDataOwner = owner,
+    )
+
+    /** Subscribes to state and events for the rest of the test; returns the event list. */
+    private fun TestScope.observe(vm: SettingsViewModel): MutableList<SettingsEvent> {
+        val events = mutableListOf<SettingsEvent>()
+        backgroundScope.launch(UnconfinedTestDispatcher(testScheduler)) { vm.state.collect {} }
+        backgroundScope.launch(UnconfinedTestDispatcher(testScheduler)) { vm.event.collect { events += it } }
+        runCurrent()
+        return events
+    }
+
+    private fun dialog(vm: SettingsViewModel) = vm.state.value.unsyncedChangesDialog
+
+    @Test
+    fun logoutOnly_signsOutAndClearsNothing() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        val vm = viewModel()
+        val events = observe(vm)
+
+        vm.logout(clearData = false)
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        verify(exactly = 1) { authRepository.signOut() }
+        assertEquals(listOf<SettingsEvent>(SettingsEvent.LoggedOut), events)
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.pendingChangeCount() }
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+        verify(exactly = 0) { prefs.clear() }
+        verify(exactly = 0) { owner.clear() }
+        verify(exactly = 0) { owner.adopt(any(), any()) }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun clearData_failedPush_asks_andKeepDataChangesNothing() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returns 2
+        coEvery { reset.pushPendingChanges() } returns false
+        val vm = viewModel()
+        val events = observe(vm)
+
+        vm.logout(clearData = true)
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertEquals(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(2, WipeReason.CLEAR_DATA, "a@example.com"), dialog(vm))
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+
+        vm.keepLocalData()
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertEquals(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Hidden, dialog(vm))
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+        verify(exactly = 0) { authRepository.signOut() }
+        verify(exactly = 0) { prefs.clear() }
+        assertTrue(events.none { it == SettingsEvent.LoggedOut })
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun clearData_failedPush_clearAnyway_wipesThenForgetsThenSignsOut() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returns 2
+        coEvery { reset.pushPendingChanges() } returns false
+        val vm = viewModel()
+        val events = observe(vm)
+
+        vm.logout(clearData = true)
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+        vm.clearAnyway()
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        coVerifyOrder {
+            reset.wipeLocalData()
+            prefs.clear()
+            authRepository.signOut()
+        }
+        assertEquals(listOf<SettingsEvent>(SettingsEvent.LoggedOut), events)
+        assertEquals(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Hidden, dialog(vm))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun clearData_pushOver15Seconds_asks() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returns 4
+        coEvery { reset.pushPendingChanges() } coAnswers {
+            delay(20.seconds)
+            true
+        }
+        val vm = viewModel()
+        observe(vm)
+
+        vm.logout(clearData = true)
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertEquals(15_000L, currentTime)
+        assertEquals(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(4, WipeReason.CLEAR_DATA, "a@example.com"), dialog(vm))
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun clearData_cancelWhilePushing_hidesAndWipesNothing() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returns 4
+        coEvery { reset.pushPendingChanges() } coAnswers {
+            delay(10.seconds)
+            true
+        }
+        val vm = viewModel()
+        val events = observe(vm)
+
+        vm.logout(clearData = true)
+        runCurrent()
+        assertEquals(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Pushing(4), dialog(vm))
+
+        vm.cancelUnsyncedPush()
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertEquals(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Hidden, dialog(vm))
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.wipeLocalData() }
+        verify(exactly = 0) { authRepository.signOut() }
+        assertTrue(events.isEmpty())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun clearData_signedOutUser_doesNotPush() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        every { authRepository.getCurrentUser() } returns null
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returns 1
+        val vm = viewModel()
+        observe(vm)
+
+        vm.logout(clearData = true)
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertEquals(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(1, WipeReason.CLEAR_DATA, "a@example.com"), dialog(vm))
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { reset.pushPendingChanges() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun failureCount_reachesState() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        failureCount.value = 2
+        val vm = viewModel()
+        observe(vm)
+
+        assertEquals(2, vm.state.value.syncFailureCount)
+        assertFalse(vm.state.value.isRetryingFailures)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun retry_resetsThenPushes_showingProgressWhileItRuns() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        failureCount.value = 2
+        coEvery { articleDao.resetSyncFailures(any()) } answers {
+            failureCount.value = 0
+            2
+        }
+        val gate = CompletableDeferred<Unit>()
+        coEvery { syncManager.pushLocalChanges() } coAnswers {
+            gate.await()
+            SyncRunResult(2, 0)
+        }
+        val vm = viewModel()
+        val events = observe(vm)
+
+        vm.retrySyncFailures()
+        runCurrent()
+
+        assertTrue(vm.state.value.isRetryingFailures)
+        assertEquals(2, vm.state.value.syncFailureCount)
+
+        gate.complete(Unit)
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertFalse(vm.state.value.isRetryingFailures)
+        assertEquals(0, vm.state.value.syncFailureCount)
+        coVerifyOrder {
+            articleDao.resetSyncFailures(any())
+            syncManager.pushLocalChanges()
+        }
+        assertTrue(events.isEmpty())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun retry_failedPush_showsToast() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        failureCount.value = 2
+        coEvery { articleDao.resetSyncFailures(any()) } returns 2
+        coEvery { syncManager.pushLocalChanges() } returns SyncRunResult(0, 2)
+        val vm = viewModel()
+        val events = observe(vm)
+
+        vm.retrySyncFailures()
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        assertEquals(1, events.size)
+        assertTrue(events.single() is SettingsEvent.ShowToast)
+        assertFalse(vm.state.value.isRetryingFailures)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun manualSync_isIgnoredWhileTheDialogShows() = runTest(dispatcher) {
+        coEvery { reset.pendingChangeCount() } returns 2
+        coEvery { reset.pushPendingChanges() } returns false
+        coEvery { syncManager.performFullSync() } returns Unit
+        val vm = viewModel()
+        observe(vm)
+        vm.logout(clearData = true)
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        vm.performManualSync()
+        advanceUntilIdle()
+
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { syncManager.performFullSync() }
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.screens.settings
+
+import android.app.Application
+import androidx.compose.ui.test.assertHeightIsAtLeast
+import androidx.compose.ui.test.assertIsEnabled
+import androidx.compose.ui.test.assertIsNotEnabled
+import androidx.compose.ui.test.junit4.createComposeRule
+import androidx.compose.ui.test.onNodeWithContentDescription
+import androidx.compose.ui.test.onNodeWithText
+import androidx.compose.ui.test.performClick
+import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
+import com.jayteealao.trails.screens.theme.TrailsTheme
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Rule
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+
+/**
+ * The Settings row for articles skipped after 3 failed sync runs: absent at 0, the count
+ * and Retry otherwise, progress and a disabled Retry while a retry runs.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], qualifiers = "w412dp-h915dp", application = Application::class)
+class SyncFailureRowTest {
+
+    @get:Rule
+    val rule = createComposeRule()
+
+    private var retries = 0
+
+    private fun show(count: Int, isRetrying: Boolean = false) {
+        rule.setContent {
+            TrailsTheme(darkTheme = false) {
+                SyncFailureRow(count = count, isRetrying = isRetrying, onRetry = { retries++ })
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun zero_rendersNothing() {
+        show(0)
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("failed to sync", substring = true).assertDoesNotExist()
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Retry").assertDoesNotExist()
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun two_showsCountSubtitleAndRetry() {
+        show(2)
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("2 articles failed to sync").assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Skipped after 3 tries. Their latest changes are only on this device.").assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Retry").assertIsEnabled().assertHeightIsAtLeast(48.dp)
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Retry").performClick()
+        assertEquals(1, retries)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun one_isSingular() {
+        show(1)
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("1 article failed to sync").assertExists()
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun retrying_showsProgressAndDisablesRetry() {
+        show(2, isRetrying = true)
+
+        rule.onNodeWithContentDescription("Retrying").assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Retry").assertIsNotEnabled()
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.screens.settings
+
+import android.app.Application
+import androidx.compose.ui.test.assertCountEquals
+import androidx.compose.ui.test.assertHeightIsAtLeast

+import com.jayteealao.trails.screens.theme.TrailsTheme
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Rule
+import org.junit.Test

+/**
+ * Every state of the unsynced-changes dialog renders the board copy and buttons, the
+ * buttons call their actions, and every button is at least 48 dp tall.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], qualifiers = "w412dp-h915dp", application = Application::class)
+class UnsyncedChangesDialogTest {
+
+    @get:Rule
+    val rule = createComposeRule()
+
+    private var keeps = 0
+    private var confirms = 0
+    private var cancels = 0
+
+    private fun show(state: UnsyncedChangesDialogState) {
+        rule.setContent {
+            TrailsTheme(darkTheme = false) {
+                UnsyncedChangesDialog(
+                    state = state,
+                    onKeep = { keeps++ },
+                    onConfirm = { confirms++ },
+                    onCancelPush = { cancels++ },
+                )
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private fun assertButton(text: String) {
+        rule.onNodeWithText(text).assertHeightIsAtLeast(48.dp)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun pushFailed_clearData_many() {
+        show(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(42, WipeReason.CLEAR_DATA, "jo.reader@example.com"))
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Changes not backed up").assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithText("42 changes on this device are not backed up. Clearing data deletes them.").assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Current account").assertDoesNotExist()
+        assertButton("Keep data")
+        assertButton("Clear anyway")
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Keep data").performClick()
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Clear anyway").performClick()
+        assertEquals(1, keeps)
+        assertEquals(1, confirms)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun pushFailed_clearData_one() {
+        show(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(1, WipeReason.CLEAR_DATA, null))
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Change not backed up").assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithText("1 change on this device is not backed up. Clearing data deletes it.").assertExists()
+        assertButton("Keep data")
+        assertButton("Clear anyway")
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun pushFailed_accountSwitch_namesTheCurrentAccount() {
+        show(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(3, WipeReason.ACCOUNT_SWITCH, "jo.reader@example.com"))
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Sign in to a different account?").assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithText(
+            "3 changes on this device are not backed up. Signing in to another account clears this device and deletes them."
+        ).assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Current account").assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithText("jo.reader@example.com").assertExists()
+        assertButton("Keep data")
+        assertButton("Switch anyway")
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Switch anyway").performClick()
+        assertEquals(1, confirms)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun pushFailed_accountSwitch_guestOwner() {
+        show(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(3, WipeReason.ACCOUNT_SWITCH, null))
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Guest account").assertExists()
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun worstCase_capsTheCount_andKeepsBothButtons() {
+        show(
+            UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(
+                1000,
+                WipeReason.ACCOUNT_SWITCH,
+                "aleksandra.wisniewska-kowalczyk.reading-archive+trails@mail.research.example.co.uk",
+            )
+        )
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("More than 999 changes", substring = true).assertExists()
+        assertButton("Keep data")
+        assertButton("Switch anyway")
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun pushing_showsProgressAndOneCancel() {
+        show(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Pushing(42))
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Backing up changes").assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Sending 42 changes to your account before clearing this device.").assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithText("This stops on its own if there is no connection.").assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithContentDescription("Backing up").assertExists()
+        rule.onAllNodes(hasClickAction()).assertCountEquals(1)
+        assertButton("Cancel")
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Cancel").performClick()
+        assertEquals(1, cancels)
+        assertEquals(0, keeps + confirms)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun wiping_hasNoButtons() {
+        show(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Wiping)
+
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Clearing data").assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithText("Deleting articles, tags and archived pages from this device.").assertExists()
+        rule.onNodeWithContentDescription("Clearing").assertExists()
+        rule.onAllNodes(hasClickAction()).assertCountEquals(0)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun hidden_rendersNothing() {
+        show(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Hidden)
+
+        rule.onAllNodesWithText("not backed up", substring = true).assertCountEquals(0)
+        rule.onAllNodes(hasClickAction()).assertCountEquals(0)
+    }
+}
