+package com.jayteealao.trails.screens.theme
+
+import android.app.Application
+import androidx.compose.material3.AlertDialogDefaults
+import androidx.compose.material3.ColorScheme
+import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
+import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
+import androidx.compose.ui.test.junit4.createComposeRule
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Rule
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+
+/**
+ * Alert dialogs take the palette's container colour in both schemes, and the dark scheme
+ * has a readable error colour with its on-error partner.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class TrailsThemeColorsTest {
+
+    @get:Rule
+    val rule = createComposeRule()
+
+    private fun capture(dark: Boolean): Pair<ColorScheme, Color> {
+        var scheme: ColorScheme? = null
+        var dialogContainer: Color? = null
+        rule.setContent {
+            TrailsTheme(darkTheme = dark) {
+                scheme = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
+                dialogContainer = AlertDialogDefaults.containerColor
+            }
+        }
+        rule.waitForIdle()
+        return scheme!! to dialogContainer!!
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun light_dialogContainerIsIce() {
+        val (scheme, dialogContainer) = capture(dark = false)
+
+        assertEquals(TrailsIce, scheme.surfaceContainerHigh)
+        assertEquals(scheme.surfaceContainerHigh, dialogContainer)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun dark_dialogContainerIsMidnightVariant_andErrorPairIsReadable() {
+        val (scheme, dialogContainer) = capture(dark = true)
+
+        assertEquals(TrailsMidnightVariant, scheme.surfaceContainerHigh)
+        assertEquals(scheme.surfaceContainerHigh, dialogContainer)
+        assertEquals(Color(0xFFFFB4AB), scheme.error)
+        assertEquals(Color(0xFF690005), scheme.onError)
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore
+
+import android.app.Application
+import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
+import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseUser
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.SharedPreferencesManager
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.account.LocalDataOwner
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.ArticleDao
+import io.mockk.coVerify
+import io.mockk.confirmVerified
+import io.mockk.every
+import io.mockk.mockk
+import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
+import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.StandardTestDispatcher
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+
+/**
+ * The owner guard of the sync: a signed-in user who does not own the local library gets
+ * neither a push nor a restore, and nothing is read before the check. The owner's own
+ * session syncs as before.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class OwnerGuardSyncTest {
+
+    private lateinit var owner: LocalDataOwner
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        val prefs = SharedPreferencesManager(RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication())
+        prefs.clear()
+        owner = LocalDataOwner(prefs)
+        owner.adopt("owner-a", "a@example.com")
+    }
+
+    private fun manager(signedInUid: String, dao: ArticleDao, backup: FirestoreBackupService): FirestoreSyncManager {
+        val user = mockk<FirebaseUser>().also { every { it.uid } returns signedInUid }
+        val auth = mockk<FirebaseAuth>().also { every { it.currentUser } returns user }
+        return FirestoreSyncManager(
+            context = RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication(),
+            firestore = mockk<FirebaseFirestore>(),
+            auth = auth,
+            articleDao = dao,
+            firestoreBackupService = backup,
+            scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + StandardTestDispatcher()),
+            localDataOwner = owner,
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun push_byNonOwner_readsAndWritesNothing() = runTest {
+        // Strict mocks: any DAO or backup call would throw.
+        val dao = mockk<ArticleDao>()
+        val backup = mockk<FirestoreBackupService>()
+
+        val result = manager("user-b", dao, backup).pushLocalChanges()
+
+        assertEquals(SyncRunStatus.NO_USER, result.status)
+        confirmVerified(dao, backup)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun fullSync_byNonOwner_restoresNothing() = runTest {
+        val dao = mockk<ArticleDao>()
+        val backup = mockk<FirestoreBackupService>()
+
+        val result = manager("user-b", dao, backup).fullSync()
+
+        assertEquals(SyncRunStatus.NO_USER, result.status)
+        confirmVerified(dao, backup)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun push_byOwner_proceeds() = runTest {
+        val dao = mockk<ArticleDao>(relaxed = true)
+        val backup = mockk<FirestoreBackupService>(relaxed = true)
+
+        // Relaxed mocks may not satisfy the rest of the run; only reaching Firestore matters.
+        runCatching { manager("owner-a", dao, backup).pushLocalChanges() }
+
+        coVerify(exactly = 1) { backup.getUserMetaSnapshot() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun push_afterOwnerCleared_proceedsForAnyone() = runTest {
+        owner.clear()
+        val dao = mockk<ArticleDao>(relaxed = true)
+        val backup = mockk<FirestoreBackupService>(relaxed = true)
+
+        // Relaxed mocks may not satisfy the rest of the run; only reaching Firestore matters.
+        runCatching { manager("user-b", dao, backup).pushLocalChanges() }
+
+        coVerify(exactly = 1) { backup.getUserMetaSnapshot() }
+    }
+}

commit 415c8be3a9865efd5b5453fd5e5bb17570e498ca
Author: jayteealao <jayteealao@gmail.com>
Date:   Fri Oct 23 16:04:11 2026 +0100

    wip(android): emulator script to snapshot local tables, seed an archive and mark skipped articles
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

diff --git a/android/tools/db-upgrade-check/wipe-check.sh b/android/tools/db-upgrade-check/wipe-check.sh
new file mode 100755
index 0000000..f506438
--- /dev/null
+++ b/android/tools/db-upgrade-check/wipe-check.sh
@@ -0,0 +1,121 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# Emulator helpers for checking the "Clear data" wipe and the sync-failure row.
+#
+#   wipe-check.sh snapshot     <emulator-serial> <out-dir> <label>
+#       Writes <out-dir>/<label>.txt: the row count of every local table, the number of
+#       unsynced changes (rows whose current version Firestore has not confirmed), the
+#       number of articles skipped after 3 failed sync runs, and a listing of the archive
+#       folder (files/archives).
+#   wipe-check.sh seed-archive <emulator-serial> <itemId>
+#       Writes one gzipped archive file at files/archives/<itemId>/readability.gz and its
+#       local_archive row, the same layout the archive download uses.
+#   wipe-check.sh strike       <emulator-serial> <n>
+#       Marks the <n> newest live articles as skipped after 3 failed sync runs.
+#
+# Every mode force-stops the app before it reads or writes the database. Only emulators are
+# accepted, and the app must be a debug build (run-as). Needs sqlite3 on PATH.
+set -euo pipefail
+export MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 # keep device paths intact under Git Bash on Windows
+
+usage() {
+  echo "usage: $0 snapshot <emulator-serial> <out-dir> <label>" >&2
+  echo "       $0 seed-archive <emulator-serial> <itemId>" >&2
+  echo "       $0 strike <emulator-serial> <n>" >&2
+  exit 2
+}
+
+[ $# -ge 3 ] || usage
+mode=$1 serial=$2
+case "$serial" in
+  emulator-*) ;;
+  *) echo "refusing $serial: only an emulator serial (emulator-NNNN) is accepted" >&2; exit 2 ;;
+esac
+command -v sqlite3 >/dev/null || { echo "sqlite3 is not on PATH" >&2; exit 2; }
+
+pkg=com.jayteealao.trails
+tables="article article_tags article_images article_authors article_videos local_archive DomainMetadata"
+dev() { adb -s "$serial" "$@"; }
+
+# Working copies live in a folder beside the script's output (or the current folder): a
+# native sqlite3 on Windows cannot open /tmp paths.
+work_root=${WIPE_CHECK_WORK:-$PWD}
+work="$work_root/.wipe-check-work"
+mkdir -p "$work"
+trap 'rm -rf "$work"' EXIT
+
+stop_app() { dev shell am force-stop "$pkg"; }
+
+pull_db() { # -> $work/Pocket with the write-ahead log folded in; returns 1 when absent
+  rm -f "$work"/Pocket*
+  for f in Pocket Pocket-wal Pocket-shm; do
+    if dev shell run-as "$pkg" ls "databases/$f" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
+      dev exec-out run-as "$pkg" cat "databases/$f" >"$work/$f"
+    fi
+  done
+  [ -s "$work/Pocket" ] || return 1
+  sqlite3 "$work/Pocket" "PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE);" >/dev/null
+}
+
+push_db() { # $work/Pocket -> the app's database, dropping the stale write-ahead log
+  dev push "$work/Pocket" /data/local/tmp/trails-wipe-check.db >/dev/null
+  dev shell chmod 644 /data/local/tmp/trails-wipe-check.db
+  dev shell run-as "$pkg" sh -c "'cat /data/local/tmp/trails-wipe-check.db > databases/Pocket && rm -f databases/Pocket-wal databases/Pocket-shm'"
+  dev shell rm -f /data/local/tmp/trails-wipe-check.db
+}
+
+case "$mode" in
+  snapshot)
+    [ $# -eq 4 ] || usage
+    out=$3 label=$4
+    mkdir -p "$out"
+    stop_app
+    {
+      if pull_db; then
+        for t in $tables; do
+          echo "$t $(sqlite3 "$work/Pocket" "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t;")"
+        done
+        echo "unsynced $(sqlite3 "$work/Pocket" 'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article WHERE backed_up_at IS NULL OR timeUpdated > backed_up_at;')"
+        echo "skipped $(sqlite3 "$work/Pocket" 'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article WHERE sync_failures >= 3;')"
+      else
+        echo "database absent"
+      fi
+      echo "archives:"
+      dev shell run-as "$pkg" ls -R files/archives 2>&1 | tr -d '\r' || true
+    } >"$out/$label.txt"
+    cat "$out/$label.txt"
+    ;;
+  seed-archive)
+    [ $# -eq 3 ] || usage
+    item=$3
+    case "$item" in
+      *[!A-Za-z0-9_-]*) echo "itemId may hold only letters, digits, - and _" >&2; exit 2 ;;
+    esac
+    stop_app
+    pull_db || { echo "no database on the device; open the app once first" >&2; exit 1; }
+    printf '# Seeded archive\n\nWritten by wipe-check.sh.\n' | gzip -c >"$work/readability.gz"
+    size=$(wc -c <"$work/readability.gz" | tr -d ' ')
+    dev push "$work/readability.gz" /data/local/tmp/trails-wipe-check.gz >/dev/null
+    dev shell chmod 644 /data/local/tmp/trails-wipe-check.gz
+    dev shell run-as "$pkg" sh -c "'mkdir -p files/archives/$item && cat /data/local/tmp/trails-wipe-check.gz > files/archives/$item/readability.gz'"
+    dev shell rm -f /data/local/tmp/trails-wipe-check.gz
+    now=$(($(date +%s) * 1000))
+    sqlite3 "$work/Pocket" "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO local_archive (itemId, archiveKey, localPath, compressedSizeBytes, originalSizeBytes, downloadedAt, status) VALUES ('$item', 'readability', 'archives/$item/readability.gz', $size, 45, $now, 'complete');"
+    push_db
+    echo "seeded files/archives/$item/readability.gz and its local_archive row"
+    ;;
+  strike)
+    [ $# -eq 3 ] || usage
+    n=$3
+    case "$n" in
+      '' | *[!0-9]*) echo "n must be a whole number" >&2; exit 2 ;;
+    esac
+    stop_app
+    pull_db || { echo "no database on the device; open the app once first" >&2; exit 1; }
+    sqlite3 "$work/Pocket" "UPDATE article SET sync_failures = 3 WHERE itemId IN (SELECT itemId FROM article WHERE deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY timeAdded DESC LIMIT $n);"
+    push_db
+    echo "skipped articles now: $(sqlite3 "$work/Pocket" 'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article WHERE sync_failures >= 3;')"
+    ;;
+  *)
+    usage
+    ;;
+esac

diff --git a/android/app/src/debug/java/com/jayteealao/trails/debug/DesignStatesActivity.kt b/android/app/src/debug/java/com/jayteealao/trails/debug/DesignStatesActivity.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..a78e482
--- /dev/null
+++ b/android/app/src/debug/java/com/jayteealao/trails/debug/DesignStatesActivity.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,120 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.debug
+
+import android.os.Bundle
+import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
+import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
+import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
+import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
+import androidx.compose.material3.Surface
+import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
+import com.jayteealao.trails.BuildConfig
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.account.UnsyncedChangesDialogState
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.account.WipeReason
+import com.jayteealao.trails.screens.settings.SettingsScreenContent
+import com.jayteealao.trails.screens.settings.SettingsState
+import com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore.SyncStatus
+import com.jayteealao.trails.screens.theme.TrailsTheme
+import io.yumemi.tartlet.ViewStore
+
+/**
+ * Debug builds only: holds one state of the Settings sync section or the unsynced-changes
+ * dialog on screen so it can be captured on a device or emulator.
+ *
+ * Extras: `surface` (`dialog` | `settings`), `state` (`pushing` | `push-failed` |
+ * `push-failed-one` | `push-failed-switch` | `wiping` | `worst` | `failures` | `no-failures`),
+ * `count` (Int), `label` (String), `dark` (Boolean). Both surfaces render the Settings
+ * screen (the boards' backdrop); `state` alone picks the dialog or the failure row, so
+ * `surface` only names the board family in the capture commands.
+ *
+ * `adb shell am start -n com.jayteealao.trails/.debug.DesignStatesActivity
+ *   --es surface dialog --es state push-failed --ei count 42 --ez dark false`
+ */
+class DesignStatesActivity : ComponentActivity() {
+
+    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
+        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
+        val state = intent.getStringExtra(EXTRA_STATE) ?: "push-failed"
+        val count = intent.getIntExtra(EXTRA_COUNT, -1)
+        val label = intent.getStringExtra(EXTRA_LABEL)
+        val dark = intent.getBooleanExtra(EXTRA_DARK, false)
+        val fakeState = fixtureState(state, count, label, dark)
+
+        setContent {
+            TrailsTheme(darkTheme = dark) {
+                Surface(
+                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
+                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.background,
+                ) {
+                    SettingsScreenContent(viewStore = ViewStore { fakeState })
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    companion object {
+        const val EXTRA_SURFACE = "surface"
+        const val EXTRA_STATE = "state"
+        const val EXTRA_COUNT = "count"
+        const val EXTRA_LABEL = "label"
+        const val EXTRA_DARK = "dark"
+
+        /** The account of the board sample data. */
+        const val SAMPLE_LABEL = "jo.reader@example.com"
+
+        /** The long address of the worst-case board. */
+        const val WORST_LABEL =
+            "aleksandra.wisniewska-kowalczyk.reading-archive+trails@mail.research.example.co.uk"
+
+        private const val MINUTE_MS = 60_000L
+
+        /**
+         * The Settings state for one board. `count` below 0 takes the board's sample count;
+         * `label` null takes the sample account ("Guest account" needs `--es label ""`, which
+         * is read as no label).
+         */
+        fun fixtureState(state: String, count: Int, label: String?, dark: Boolean): SettingsState {
+            val ownerLabel = when (label) {
+                null -> if (state == "worst") WORST_LABEL else SAMPLE_LABEL
+                "" -> null
+                else -> label
+            }
+            fun countOr(sample: Int) = if (count >= 0) count else sample
+
+            val dialog = when (state) {
+                "pushing" -> UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Pushing(countOr(42))
+                "push-failed" -> UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(
+                    countOr(42), WipeReason.CLEAR_DATA, ownerLabel,
+                )
+                "push-failed-one" -> UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(
+                    1, WipeReason.CLEAR_DATA, ownerLabel,
+                )
+                "push-failed-switch" -> UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(
+                    countOr(3), WipeReason.ACCOUNT_SWITCH, ownerLabel,
+                )
+                "worst" -> UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(
+                    countOr(1000), WipeReason.ACCOUNT_SWITCH, ownerLabel,
+                )
+                "wiping" -> UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Wiping
+                else -> UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Hidden
+            }
+            val failures = when (state) {
+                "failures" -> countOr(2)
+                else -> 0
+            }
+            val lastSyncAgo = if (state == "failures") 12 * MINUTE_MS else 2 * MINUTE_MS
+
+            return SettingsState(
+                darkTheme = dark,
+                useCardLayout = true,
+                versionName = BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME,
+                versionCode = BuildConfig.VERSION_CODE,
+                lastSyncTime = System.currentTimeMillis() - lastSyncAgo,
+                syncStatus = SyncStatus.Idle,
+                userEmail = SAMPLE_LABEL,
+                isAnonymous = false,
+                syncFailureCount = failures,
+                unsyncedChangesDialog = dialog,
+            )
+        }
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.screens.settings
+
+import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement
+import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Column
+import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Row

+import androidx.compose.material3.Icon
+import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
+import androidx.compose.material3.Text
+import androidx.compose.material3.TextButton
+import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
+import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
+import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
+import androidx.compose.ui.res.painterResource
+import androidx.compose.ui.semantics.contentDescription
+import androidx.compose.ui.semantics.semantics
+import androidx.compose.ui.text.SpanStyle
+import androidx.compose.ui.text.buildAnnotatedString
+import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
+import androidx.compose.ui.text.withStyle
+import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
+import com.jayteealao.trails.R
+
+/**
+ * The Settings sync-section row for articles the sync skipped after 3 failed runs.
+ * Not composed at all when [count] is 0. Retry resets the counters and runs one sync;
+ * while it runs the row shows progress and Retry is disabled.
+ */
+@Composable
+fun SyncFailureRow(
+    count: Int,
+    isRetrying: Boolean,
+    onRetry: () -> Unit,
+    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
+) {
+    if (count <= 0) return
+    Row(
+        modifier = modifier
+            .fillMaxWidth()
+            .padding(vertical = 12.dp),
+        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
+        verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top,
+    ) {
+        Icon(
+            painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_sync_problem),
+            // The text carries the meaning.
+            contentDescription = null,
+            tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error,
+        )
+        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
+            Text(
+                text = buildAnnotatedString {
+                    withStyle(SpanStyle(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)) { append(count.toString()) }
+                    append(if (count == 1) " article failed to sync" else " articles failed to sync")
+                },
+                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
+                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface,
+            )
+            Text(
+                text = "Skipped after 3 tries. Their latest changes are only on this device.",
+                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
+                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
+            )
+        }
+        Row(
+            modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.CenterVertically),
+            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
+        ) {
+            if (isRetrying) {
+                CircularProgressIndicator(
+                    modifier = Modifier
+                        .size(16.dp)
+                        .semantics { contentDescription = "Retrying" },
+                    strokeWidth = 2.dp,
+                )
+            }
+            TextButton(
+                onClick = onRetry,
+                enabled = !isRetrying,
+                modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = 48.dp),
+            ) {
+                Text(text = "Retry", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

+    /**
+     * "Logout only" ([clearData] false) keeps every table and the archive folder.
+     * "Clear data" pushes pending changes first (at most 15 seconds), then clears every
+     * table and the archive folder; when changes stay unsent the person decides, and
+     * keeping the data leaves them signed in with nothing changed.
+     */

-            if (clearData) {
-                // Clear all local data

+            viewModelScope.launch(ioDispatcher) {
+                authRepository.signOut()
+                _event.emit(SettingsEvent.LoggedOut)
+            }
+            return
+        }
+        if (clearDataJob?.isActive == true) return
+        clearDataJob = viewModelScope.launch(ioDispatcher) {
+            val user = authRepository.getCurrentUser()
+            val result = pushThenWipe.run(
+                reason = WipeReason.CLEAR_DATA,
+                ownerLabel = localDataOwner.label(),
+                canPush = user != null && localDataOwner.isOwnedBy(user.uid),
+                onState = { unsyncedChangesDialog.value = it },
+                confirm = {
+                    val answer = CompletableDeferred<Boolean>()
+                    clearAnswer = answer
+                    try {
+                        answer.await()
+                    } finally {
+                        clearAnswer = null
+                    }
+                },
+            )
+            if (result == PushThenWipeResult.WIPED) {
+                // Also clears the owner, so the next sign-in takes the empty device.
                 sharedPreferencesManager.clear()
+                authRepository.signOut()
+                _event.emit(SettingsEvent.LoggedOut)

+    /** Cancel while pending changes are pushed: nothing is cleared. */
+    fun cancelUnsyncedPush() {
+        if (unsyncedChangesDialog.value is UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Pushing) clearDataJob?.cancel()
+    }
+
+    fun keepLocalData() {
+        clearAnswer?.complete(false)
+    }
+
+    fun clearAnyway() {
+        clearAnswer?.complete(true)
+    }
+
+    /** Retry on the failure row: reset the 3-strike counters, then push once. */
+    fun retrySyncFailures() {
+        if (retryingFailureCount.value != null) return
+        retryingFailureCount.value = state.value.syncFailureCount
+        viewModelScope.launch(ioDispatcher) {
+            try {
+                articleDao.resetSyncFailures(System.currentTimeMillis())
+                if (firestoreSyncManager.pushLocalChanges().status != SyncRunStatus.SUCCESS) {
+                    _event.emit(SettingsEvent.ShowToast(RETRY_FAILED_MESSAGE))
+                }
+            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
+                throw e
+            } catch (e: Exception) {
+                _event.emit(SettingsEvent.ShowToast(RETRY_FAILED_MESSAGE))
+            } finally {
+                retryingFailureCount.value = null
+            }
+        }
+    }

+
+/**
+ * The dialog of a local-data wipe: backing up pending changes, the push-failed question
+ * (clear data or account switch), and the wipe itself. Renders nothing when [state] is
+ * [Hidden]. Colours come from the theme only: the container is the theme's dialog role and
+ * the destructive action uses `colorScheme.error`.
+ */
+@Composable
+fun UnsyncedChangesDialog(
+    state: UnsyncedChangesDialogState,
+    onKeep: () -> Unit,
+    onConfirm: () -> Unit,
+    onCancelPush: () -> Unit,
+) {
+    when (state) {
+        Hidden -> Unit
+        is Pushing -> AlertDialog(
+            onDismissRequest = onCancelPush,
+            properties = DialogProperties(dismissOnClickOutside = false),
+            title = { Text("Backing up changes") },
+            text = {
+                Column {
+                    Text(countText("Sending ", state.count, " to your account before clearing this device."))
+                    WipeProgress(description = "Backing up")
+                    Text(
+                        text = "This stops on its own if there is no connection.",
+                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
+                        modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp),
+                    )
+                }
+            },
+            confirmButton = { DialogTextButton("Cancel", onClick = onCancelPush) },
+        )
+        is PushFailed -> {
+            val one = state.count == 1
+            val switch = state.reason == WipeReason.ACCOUNT_SWITCH
+            val verb = if (one) " is" else " are"
+            val pronoun = if (one) "it" else "them"
+            AlertDialog(
+                onDismissRequest = onKeep,
+                properties = DialogProperties(dismissOnClickOutside = false),
+                title = {
+                    Text(
+                        when {
+                            switch -> "Sign in to a different account?"
+                            one -> "Change not backed up"
+                            else -> "Changes not backed up"
+                        }
+                    )
+                },
+                text = {
+                    Column {
+                        val consequence = if (switch) {
+                            " Signing in to another account clears this device and deletes $pronoun."
+                        } else {
+                            " Clearing data deletes $pronoun."
+                        }
+                        Text(countText("", state.count, " on this device$verb not backed up.$consequence"))
+                        if (switch) CurrentAccount(state.ownerLabel)
+                    }
+                },
+                dismissButton = { DialogTextButton("Keep data", onClick = onKeep) },
+                confirmButton = {
+                    DialogTextButton(
+                        text = if (switch) "Switch anyway" else "Clear anyway",
+                        onClick = onConfirm,
+                        destructive = true,
+                    )
+                },
+            )
+        }
+        Wiping -> AlertDialog(
+            // The wipe cannot be interrupted half-way: no buttons, back does nothing.
+            onDismissRequest = {},
+            properties = DialogProperties(dismissOnClickOutside = false, dismissOnBackPress = false),
+            title = { Text("Clearing data") },
+            text = {
+                Column {
+                    Text("Deleting articles, tags and archived pages from this device.")
+                    WipeProgress(description = "Clearing")
+                }
+            },
+            confirmButton = {},
+        )
+    }
+}
+
+/** [prefix], then the count in bold ("42 changes"), then [suffix]: the count leads what is read. */
+@Composable
+private fun countText(prefix: String, count: Int, suffix: String): AnnotatedString = buildAnnotatedString {
+    append(prefix)
+    withStyle(SpanStyle(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface)) {
+        append(unsyncedChangesText(count))
+    }
+    append(suffix)
+}
+
+@Composable
+private fun WipeProgress(description: String) {
+    LinearProgressIndicator(
+        modifier = Modifier
+            .fillMaxWidth()
+            .padding(top = 24.dp)
+            .semantics { contentDescription = description },
+    )
+}
+
+@Composable
+private fun CurrentAccount(ownerLabel: String?) {
+    Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 16.dp)) {
+        Text(text = "Current account", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall)
+        Text(

+            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
+            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
+            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface,
+            maxLines = 1,
+            // A long address keeps its start and its domain, as the worst-case board draws it.
+            overflow = TextOverflow.MiddleEllipsis,
+        )
+    }
+}
+

+private fun DialogTextButton(text: String, onClick: () -> Unit, destructive: Boolean = false) {

+        onClick = onClick,
+        modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = 48.dp),

+        },

+    }

    wip(android): push pending changes for up to 15 seconds before clearing, and ask when some stay unsent
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

diff --git a/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/account/PushThenWipe.kt b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/account/PushThenWipe.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..fd9fd45
--- /dev/null
+++ b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/account/PushThenWipe.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,80 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.account
+
+import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeoutOrNull
+import javax.inject.Inject
+import kotlin.time.Duration.Companion.seconds
+
+/** Why the local library is being cleared. */
+enum class WipeReason { CLEAR_DATA, ACCOUNT_SWITCH }
+
+enum class PushThenWipeResult { WIPED, KEPT }
+
+/** What the unsynced-changes dialog shows. */
+sealed interface UnsyncedChangesDialogState {
+    data object Hidden : UnsyncedChangesDialogState
+    data class Pushing(val count: Int) : UnsyncedChangesDialogState
+    data class PushFailed(
+        val count: Int,
+        val reason: WipeReason,
+        val ownerLabel: String?,
+    ) : UnsyncedChangesDialogState
+    data object Wiping : UnsyncedChangesDialogState
+}
+
+/**
+ * Clear the local library without silently losing changes: pending changes are pushed
+ * first (at most [PUSH_LIMIT]), and when some stay unsent the person decides.
+ */
+class PushThenWipe @Inject constructor(
+    private val reset: LocalDataReset,
+) {
+    /**
+     * @param canPush true only when the signed-in user owns the local data; otherwise
+     *   the push is treated as failed, since it would copy the data into another account.
+     * @param onState receives each dialog state; always ends with [UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Hidden].
+     * @param confirm asks the person whether to clear anyway after a failed push.
+     *
+     * Cancelling the caller while pushing wipes nothing.
+     */
+    suspend fun run(
+        reason: WipeReason,
+        ownerLabel: String?,
+        canPush: Boolean,
+        onState: (UnsyncedChangesDialogState) -> Unit,
+        confirm: suspend (UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed) -> Boolean,
+    ): PushThenWipeResult {
+        try {
+            var count = reset.pendingChangeCount()
+            if (count > 0) {
+                var pushed = false
+                if (canPush) {
+                    onState(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Pushing(count))
+                    pushed = withTimeoutOrNull(PUSH_LIMIT) { reset.pushPendingChanges() } == true
+                    // A partial push stamps what it sent, so count what is left.
+                    if (!pushed) count = reset.pendingChangeCount()
+                }
+                if (!pushed && count > 0) {
+                    val failed = UnsyncedChangesDialogState.PushFailed(count, reason, ownerLabel)
+                    onState(failed)
+                    if (!confirm(failed)) return PushThenWipeResult.KEPT
+                }
+            }
+            onState(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Wiping)
+            reset.wipeLocalData()
+            return PushThenWipeResult.WIPED
+        } finally {
+            onState(UnsyncedChangesDialogState.Hidden)
+        }
+    }
+
+    companion object {
+        val PUSH_LIMIT = 15.seconds
+    }
+}
+
+/** "1 change", "42 changes", or "More than 999 changes" above 999. */
+internal fun unsyncedChangesText(count: Int): String = when {
+    count == 1 -> "1 change"
+    count > 999 -> "More than 999 changes"
+    else -> "$count changes"
+}

diff --git a/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/account/LocalDataReset.kt b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/account/LocalDataReset.kt

+++ b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/account/LocalDataReset.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.account
+
+import android.content.Context
+import androidx.work.WorkManager
+import androidx.work.await
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.ArticleDao
+import com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore.FirestoreSyncManager
+import com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore.SyncRunStatus
+import com.jayteealao.trails.sync.workers.FirestoreRestoreWorker
+import com.jayteealao.trails.sync.workers.FirestoreSyncWorker
+import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
+import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
+import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
+import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
+import timber.log.Timber
+import java.io.File
+import javax.inject.Inject
+import javax.inject.Singleton
+
+/** The device-side operations behind "Clear data" and an account switch. */
+@Singleton
+class LocalDataReset @Inject constructor(
+    @ApplicationContext private val context: Context,
+    private val articleDao: ArticleDao,
+    private val firestoreSyncManager: FirestoreSyncManager,
+) {
+    suspend fun pendingChangeCount(): Int = articleDao.countUnsyncedChanges()
+
+    /** True only when the push finished with nothing failed. */
+    suspend fun pushPendingChanges(): Boolean = try {
+        firestoreSyncManager.pushLocalChanges().status == SyncRunStatus.SUCCESS
+    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
+        throw e
+    } catch (e: Exception) {
+        Timber.w(e, "Push before clearing data failed")
+        false
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Cancel every sync and restore request. Request builders tag each request with its
+     * worker class name, so this covers the one-time backup and restore work and the
+     * periodic sync.
+     */
+    suspend fun cancelSyncWork() {
+        val workManager = WorkManager.getInstance(context)
+        workManager.cancelAllWorkByTag(FirestoreSyncWorker::class.java.name).await()
+        workManager.cancelAllWorkByTag(FirestoreRestoreWorker::class.java.name).await()
+    }
+
+    /** Stop sync work, delete every table, then delete the archive folder. */
+    suspend fun wipeLocalData() {
+        cancelSyncWork()
+        articleDao.wipeAllLocalData()
+        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
+            val archives = File(context.filesDir, ARCHIVES_DIR)
+            if (!archives.deleteRecursively() && archives.exists()) {
+                Timber.w("Archive folder not fully deleted; the next wipe removes the rest")
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    companion object {
+        /** Same folder as ArchiveService.archivesDir (filesDir/archives). */
+        const val ARCHIVES_DIR = "archives"
+    }
+}

commit a256b6bec852ebcd08e336360aa213ce91ec7ac8
Author: jayteealao <jayteealao@gmail.com>
Date:   Fri Oct 23 15:45:00 2026 +0100

    wip(android): decide whether a sign-in keeps, adopts or replaces the local library
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

diff --git a/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/account/LocalDataOwner.kt b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/account/LocalDataOwner.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..956c2d4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/account/LocalDataOwner.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,43 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.account
+
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.SharedPreferencesManager
+import javax.inject.Inject
+import javax.inject.Singleton
+
+/** What a sign-in means for the local library. */
+enum class OwnerDecision {
+    /** No owner is stored (first sign-in after the update, or after "Clear data"): take the data. */
+    ADOPT,
+
+    /** The signed-in uid already owns the data. */
+    SAME_OWNER,
+
+    /** Another uid owns the data: the device is cleared before this user gets it. */
+    DIFFERENT_OWNER,
+}
+
+/**
+ * The uid that owns the local library. A different uid signing in clears the device
+ * first; a link keeps the uid, so it keeps the data.
+ */
+@Singleton
+class LocalDataOwner @Inject constructor(
+    private val prefs: SharedPreferencesManager,
+) {
+    fun uid(): String? = prefs.getLocalDataOwnerUid()
+
+    /** The owner's email, or null for a guest owner. */
+    fun label(): String? = prefs.getLocalDataOwnerLabel()
+
+    fun decide(signedInUid: String): OwnerDecision {
+        val owner = uid() ?: return OwnerDecision.ADOPT
+        return if (owner == signedInUid) OwnerDecision.SAME_OWNER else OwnerDecision.DIFFERENT_OWNER
+    }
+
+    /** True when no owner is stored or [uid] is the owner. */
+    fun isOwnedBy(uid: String): Boolean = prefs.getLocalDataOwnerUid().let { it == null || it == uid }
+
+    fun adopt(uid: String, label: String?) = prefs.setLocalDataOwner(uid, label)
+
+    fun clear() = prefs.clearLocalDataOwner()
+}

+
+    /** The owner's display label (the email; null for a guest). */
+    fun getLocalDataOwnerLabel(): String? = sharedPreferences.getString(LOCAL_DATA_OWNER_LABEL, null)
+
+    fun setLocalDataOwner(uid: String, label: String?) {
+        sharedPreferences.edit().apply {
+            putString(LOCAL_DATA_OWNER_UID, uid)
+            if (label != null) putString(LOCAL_DATA_OWNER_LABEL, label) else remove(LOCAL_DATA_OWNER_LABEL)
+        }.apply()
+    }
+
+    fun clearLocalDataOwner() {
+        sharedPreferences.edit()
+            .remove(LOCAL_DATA_OWNER_UID)
+            .remove(LOCAL_DATA_OWNER_LABEL)
+            .apply()
+    }

+/**
+ * Warg metadata becomes the article's lead-image row and byline row, through the
+ * real [ArchiveService.applyWargMetadata] and a real in-memory database on the
+ * bundled engine. Firestore is a fake: the user doc names the shared article
+ * through `canonicalId`, the shared doc carries a fixture `metadata` map.

+@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)

+class LeadImageBylineRowsTest {
+
+    private val lead = "https://example.com/lead.jpg"

+    private lateinit var dao: ArticleDao
+    private lateinit var firestore: FirebaseFirestore

+    @Before

+        every { userArticles.document("a1") } returns userArticleDoc
+
+        articlesCollection = mockk()

+        shared1Doc = mockk()

+        every { articlesCollection.document("shared1") } returns shared1Doc
+
+        service = ArchiveService(
+            firestore = firestore,
+            auth = auth,
+            okHttpClient = mockk<OkHttpClient>(relaxed = true),
+            archiveUrlService = mockk<ArchiveUrlService>(relaxed = true),
+            remoteConfig = mockk<FirebaseRemoteConfig>(relaxed = true),

+        return snapshot
+    }

+    private fun sharedSnapshot(metadata: Map<String, Any?>?): DocumentSnapshot {
+        val snapshot = mockk<DocumentSnapshot>()
+        every { snapshot.exists() } returns true
+        every { snapshot.get("metadata") } returns metadata
+        return snapshot
+    }
+
+    private fun fixtureMetadata(leadImage: String? = lead): Map<String, Any?> = mapOf(
+        "title" to "T",
+        "byline" to "Jo Writer",
+        "word_count" to 120L,
+        "lead_image" to leadImage,
+    )
+
+    /** The user doc names shared1, which carries [metadata]. */
+    private fun linkToShared(metadata: Map<String, Any?>?) {
+        every { userArticleDoc.get() } returns Tasks.forResult(userSnapshot("shared1"))
+        every { shared1Doc.get() } returns Tasks.forResult(sharedSnapshot(metadata))
+    }

+    @Test
+    fun runTwice_storesExactlyOneLeadImageRowAndOneBylineRow() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article()))

+
+        service.applyWargMetadata("a1")

+        service.applyWargMetadata("a1")
+        val afterSecond = dao.getArticleById("a1")!!
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(expectedLeadRow), dao.getImagesForArticles(listOf("a1")))
+        val authors = dao.getAuthorsForArticles(listOf("a1"))
+        assertEquals(1, authors.size)
+        assertEquals("a1:byline", authors.single().authorId)
+        assertEquals("Jo Writer", authors.single().name)
+        assertEquals(lead, afterSecond.image)
+        assertTrue("the row write raises timeUpdated", afterFirst.timeUpdated > 2_000)
+        assertEquals("a repeat run writes nothing", afterFirst.timeUpdated, afterSecond.timeUpdated)
+        assertTrue(dao.getImagesForArticles(listOf("shared1")).isEmpty())
+        assertTrue(dao.getAuthorsForArticles(listOf("shared1")).isEmpty())

+    }

+

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.archive
+
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleAuthors
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleImages
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/**
+ * The pure rules that turn Warg's metadata into the article's lead-image row and
+ * byline row, and the lead-image-first choice of the displayed article image.
+ */
+class WargArticleRowsTest {
+
+    private val lead = "https://example.com/lead.jpg"
+
+    private fun metadata(leadImage: String? = lead, byline: String? = "Jo Writer") = WargMetadata(
+        title = "T",
+        excerpt = null,
+        byline = byline,
+        wordCount = 120,
+        leadImage = leadImage,
+    )
+
+    private fun importedImage() = ArticleImages(
+        imageId = "1",
+        itemId = "a1",
+        src = "https://example.com/imported.jpg",
+        width = 100,
+        height = 50,
+        credit = "",
+        caption = "",
+    )
+
+    private fun importedAuthor() = ArticleAuthors(
+        authorId = "77",
+        itemId = "a1",
+        name = "Imported Author",
+        url = "",
+        domain = null,
+        image = null,
+        bio = null,
+        twitter = null,
+    )
+
+    // ── usableLeadImage ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+    @Test
+    fun usableLeadImage_keepsHttpAndHttpsTrimmed() {
+        assertEquals("https://a/b.jpg", usableLeadImage("https://a/b.jpg"))
+        assertEquals("http://a/b.jpg", usableLeadImage("  http://a/b.jpg "))
+        assertEquals("HTTPS://a/b.jpg", usableLeadImage("HTTPS://a/b.jpg"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun usableLeadImage_rejectsOtherSchemesAndBlanks() {
+        assertNull(usableLeadImage("data:image/png;base64,AAA"))
+        assertNull(usableLeadImage("javascript:alert(1)"))
+        assertNull(usableLeadImage("ftp://a/b.jpg"))
+        assertNull(usableLeadImage("/relative/b.jpg"))
+        assertNull(usableLeadImage(""))
+        assertNull(usableLeadImage("  "))
+        assertNull(usableLeadImage(null))
+    }
+
+    // ── planWargRows ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+    @Test
+    fun planWargRows_noRows_writesOneLeadImageRowAndOneBylineRow() {
+        val plan = planWargRows("a1", metadata(), null, emptyList(), emptyList())
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(ArticleImages("lead", "a1", lead, 0, 0, "", "")), plan.images)
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(ArticleAuthors("a1:byline", "a1", "Jo Writer", "", null, null, null, null)),
+            plan.authors,
+        )
+        assertEquals(ImageChoice.UseLeadImage(lead), plan.image)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun planWargRows_blankByline_writesNoAuthorRow() {
+        val plan = planWargRows("a1", metadata(byline = "  "), null, emptyList(), emptyList())
+
+        assertTrue(plan.authors.isEmpty())
+        assertEquals(1, plan.images.size)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun planWargRows_noLeadImage_writesNoImageRowAndTakesTheScreenshot() {
+        val plan = planWargRows("a1", metadata(leadImage = null), null, emptyList(), emptyList())
+
+        assertTrue(plan.images.isEmpty())
+        assertEquals(1, plan.authors.size)
+        assertEquals(ImageChoice.UseScreenshot, plan.image)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun planWargRows_unusableLeadImage_isTreatedAsNoLeadImage() {
+        val plan = planWargRows("a1", metadata(leadImage = "ftp://x/y.jpg"), null, emptyList(), emptyList())
+
+        assertTrue(plan.images.isEmpty())
+        assertEquals(ImageChoice.UseScreenshot, plan.image)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun planWargRows_identicalRowsStored_writesNothing() {
+        val first = planWargRows("a1", metadata(), null, emptyList(), emptyList())
+
+        val second = planWargRows("a1", metadata(), lead, first.images, first.authors)
+
+        assertTrue(second.images.isEmpty())
+        assertTrue(second.authors.isEmpty())
+        assertEquals(ImageChoice.Keep, second.image)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun planWargRows_leadRowWithAnotherSrc_writesOneUpdatedRow() {
+        val stale = ArticleImages("lead", "a1", "https://example.com/old.jpg", 0, 0, "", "")
+
+        val plan = planWargRows("a1", metadata(), stale.src, listOf(stale), emptyList())
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(ArticleImages("lead", "a1", lead, 0, 0, "", "")), plan.images)
+        assertEquals(ImageChoice.UseLeadImage(lead), plan.image)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun planWargRows_importedImageRow_writesNoImageRowAndKeepsTheImage() {
+        val plan = planWargRows("a1", metadata(), importedImage().src, listOf(importedImage()), emptyList())
+
+        assertTrue(plan.images.isEmpty())
+        assertEquals(ImageChoice.Keep, plan.image)
+        assertEquals(1, plan.authors.size)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun planWargRows_importedAuthorRow_writesNoAuthorRow() {

diff --git a/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/archive/ArchiveService.kt b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/archive/ArchiveService.kt

+    // ── g) Metadata → image and author rows ─────────────────────────────
+
+    /**
+     * Turn the Warg metadata of [itemId] into its lead-image row and byline row
+     * and choose the article image (lead image first, archived screenshot when
+     * there is none). Writes only missing or changed rows; the row write raises
+     * `timeUpdated`, so the next push carries the rows. A repeat run with the same
+     * metadata writes nothing. A missing or deleted article, or no metadata, is a
+     * no-op.
+     */
+    suspend fun applyWargMetadata(itemId: String): Unit = withContext(ioDispatcher) {
+        val article = articleDao.getArticleById(itemId) ?: return@withContext
+        if (article.deletedAt != null) return@withContext
+        val metadata = fetchWargMetadata(itemId) ?: return@withContext
+
+        val plan = planWargRows(
+            itemId = itemId,
+            metadata = metadata,
+            currentImage = article.image,
+            existingImages = articleDao.getImagesForArticles(listOf(itemId)),
+            existingAuthors = articleDao.getAuthorsForArticles(listOf(itemId)),
+        )
+        when (val choice = plan.image) {
+            is ImageChoice.UseLeadImage -> articleDao.updateImage(itemId, choice.url)
+            ImageChoice.UseScreenshot -> applyScreenshotAsImage(itemId)
+            ImageChoice.Keep -> Unit
+        }
+        if (plan.images.isNotEmpty() || plan.authors.isNotEmpty()) {
+            articleDao.addLocalArticleRows(itemId, plan.images, plan.authors, System.currentTimeMillis())
+        }
+        Timber.d(
+            "applyWargMetadata($itemId) — lead=${if (usableLeadImage(metadata.leadImage) != null) "present" else "absent"} " +
+                "byline=${if (metadata.byline.isNullOrBlank()) "absent" else "present"} " +
+                "rows=${plan.images.size + plan.authors.size} image=${plan.image::class.simpleName}",
+        )
+    }
+
     // ── Scoped archive download via per-user signed URL ─────────────────
 
     /**

Author: jayteealao <jayteealao@gmail.com>
Date:   Fri Oct 23 14:39:16 2026 +0100

    wip(android): rules that turn the lead image and byline into article rows and pick the article image
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

diff --git a/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/archive/WargArticleRows.kt b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/archive/WargArticleRows.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..c714cb2
--- /dev/null
+++ b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/archive/WargArticleRows.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,107 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.archive
+
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleAuthors
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleImages
+
+// Pure rules that turn Warg's archive metadata into the article's image and
+// author rows and pick the displayed article image. No Android, Firestore or
+// Room call lives here.
+
+/** Image row id of the page lead image (rows are keyed by imageId and itemId). */
+const val LEAD_IMAGE_ID = "lead"
+
+/** Author row id of the byline; per article, because author rows are keyed by authorId alone. */
+fun bylineAuthorId(itemId: String) = "$itemId:byline"
+
+/** The trimmed lead image when it is a non-blank http(s) URL, else null. */
+fun usableLeadImage(raw: String?): String? {
+    val value = raw?.trim()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } ?: return null
+    val lower = value.lowercase()
+    return value.takeIf { lower.startsWith("https://") || lower.startsWith("http://") }
+}
+
+fun leadImageRow(itemId: String, leadImage: String) = ArticleImages(
+    imageId = LEAD_IMAGE_ID,
+    itemId = itemId,
+    src = leadImage,
+    width = 0,
+    height = 0,
+    credit = "",
+    caption = "",
+)
+
+/** One row for the whole byline, several names included. */
+fun bylineAuthorRow(itemId: String, byline: String) = ArticleAuthors(
+    authorId = bylineAuthorId(itemId),
+    itemId = itemId,
+    name = byline.trim(),
+    url = "",
+    domain = null,
+    image = null,
+    bio = null,
+    twitter = null,
+)
+
+/** Which image the article shows after the metadata is applied. */
+sealed interface ImageChoice {
+    data class UseLeadImage(val url: String) : ImageChoice
+    data object UseScreenshot : ImageChoice
+    data object Keep : ImageChoice
+}
+
+/** The rows to write (only missing or changed ones) and the image choice. */
+data class WargRowsPlan(
+    val images: List<ArticleImages>,
+    val authors: List<ArticleAuthors>,
+    val image: ImageChoice,
+)
+
+/**
+ * Lead-image-first rule: rows imported before Warg keep the current image; a
+ * usable lead image is shown; with no lead image, a blank image takes the
+ * archived screenshot and any other image stays.
+ */
+fun chooseArticleImage(
+    currentImage: String?,
+    leadImage: String?,
+    hasImportedImageRows: Boolean,
+): ImageChoice = when {
+    hasImportedImageRows -> ImageChoice.Keep
+    leadImage != null -> if (currentImage == leadImage) ImageChoice.Keep else ImageChoice.UseLeadImage(leadImage)
+    currentImage.isNullOrBlank() -> ImageChoice.UseScreenshot
+    else -> ImageChoice.Keep
+}
+
+/**
+ * Plan the rows and the image for [itemId] from [metadata]. A row is written
+ * when no row with its id exists or the stored one differs; it is skipped when
+ * the article already has a row of that kind with another id (imported rows).
+ */
+fun planWargRows(
+    itemId: String,
+    metadata: WargMetadata,
+    currentImage: String?,
+    existingImages: List<ArticleImages>,
+    existingAuthors: List<ArticleAuthors>,
+): WargRowsPlan {
+    val lead = usableLeadImage(metadata.leadImage)
+    val hasImportedImageRows = existingImages.any { it.imageId != LEAD_IMAGE_ID }
+    val images = if (lead != null && !hasImportedImageRows) {
+        val row = leadImageRow(itemId, lead)
+        if (existingImages.none { it == row }) listOf(row) else emptyList()
+    } else {
+        emptyList()
+    }
+
+    val authorId = bylineAuthorId(itemId)
+    val byline = metadata.byline?.trim()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
+    val hasImportedAuthorRows = existingAuthors.any { it.authorId != authorId }
+    val authors = if (byline != null && !hasImportedAuthorRows) {
+        val row = bylineAuthorRow(itemId, byline)
+        if (existingAuthors.none { it == row }) listOf(row) else emptyList()
+    } else {
+        emptyList()
+    }
+
+    return WargRowsPlan(images, authors, chooseArticleImage(currentImage, lead, hasImportedImageRows))
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore
+
+import android.app.Application
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.AppDatabase

+/**
+ * An article's image and author rows survive a backup and a restore into an empty
+ * database: the real backup service writes them to a map-backed Firestore, the real
+ * sync manager's apply step reads them back, and a real database stores them. The push
+ * path sends each article's rows with it and sends nothing for an article without rows.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class ImageAuthorSyncRoundTripTest {
+
+    private val databases = mutableListOf<AppDatabase>()
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        databases.forEach { it.close() }
+    }
+
+    private fun newDao(): ArticleDao =
+        JvmRoomDatabase.create(RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication()).also { databases += it }.articleDao()
+
+    private fun manager(dao: ArticleDao, service: FirestoreBackupService, fake: MapBackedFirestore? = null) =
+        FirestoreSyncManager(
+            context = RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication(),
+            firestore = fake?.firestore ?: mockk<FirebaseFirestore>(relaxed = true),
+            auth = fake?.auth ?: MapBackedFirestore().auth,
+            articleDao = dao,
+            firestoreBackupService = service,
+            scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + StandardTestDispatcher()),
+        )
+
+    private val a1 = Article(
+        itemId = "a1",
+        title = "Article a1",
+        url = "https://example.com/a1",
+        timeAdded = 2_000,
+        timeUpdated = 2_000,
+    )
+
+    private val images = listOf(
+        ArticleImages("1", "a1", "https://example.com/a1/lead.jpg", 1200, 630, "Photo credit", "Lead"),
+        ArticleImages("2", "a1", "https://example.com/a1/inline.png", 640, 480, "", ""),
+    )
+
+    private val authors = listOf(
+        ArticleAuthors("a1:byline", "a1", "Ada Writer", "https://example.com/ada", "example.com", null, "Bio", "@ada"),
+    )
+
+    @Test
+    fun backupThenRestoreIntoAnEmptyDatabase_keepsTheImageAndAuthorRows() = runTest {
+        val fake = MapBackedFirestore()
+        val service = FirestoreBackupService(fake.firestore, fake.auth)
+
+        val backup = service.backupArticlesPaginated(listOf(a1), emptyMap(), rowsByArticleId = groupRows(images, authors))
+
+        assertEquals(Result.success(1), backup)
+        assertEquals(2, fake.docsUnder("users/u1/articles/a1/images").size)
+        assertEquals(1, fake.docsUnder("users/u1/articles/a1/authors").size)
+
+        val dao = newDao()
+        val failed = manager(dao, service, fake).applyRemoteArticles(listOf(a1))
+
+        assertEquals(0, failed)
+        assertTrue(dao.getArticleById("a1") != null)
+        assertEquals(images.toSet(), dao.getImagesForArticles(listOf("a1")).toSet())
+        assertEquals(2, dao.getImagesForArticles(listOf("a1")).size)
+        assertEquals(authors, dao.getAuthorsForArticles(listOf("a1")))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun push_sendsEachArticlesRows_andNothingForAnArticleWithoutRows() = runTest {
+        val dao = newDao()
+        val a2 = a1.copy(itemId = "a2", url = "https://example.com/a2")
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(a1, a2))
+        dao.insertArticleRows(images, authors)
+
+        val sentRows = mutableListOf<Map<String, ArticleRows>>()
+        val service = mockk<FirestoreBackupService>()
+        coEvery { service.getUserMetaSnapshot() } returns
+            Result.success(FirestoreBackupService.UserMetaSnapshot(isFirstSync = false, lastSyncTimestamp = 1_000L))
+        coEvery { service.updateLastSyncTimestamp(any()) } returns Result.success(Unit)
+        coEvery { service.backupArticlesPaginated(any(), any(), any(), any()) } answers {
+            sentRows += arg<Map<String, ArticleRows>>(3)
+            Result.success(firstArg<List<Article>>().size)
+        }
+
+        val result = manager(dao, service).pushLocalChanges()
+
+        assertEquals(2, result.pushed)
+        val rows = sentRows.reduce { acc, map -> acc + map }
+        assertEquals(setOf("a1"), rows.keys)
+        assertEquals(images.toSet(), rows.getValue("a1").images.toSet())
+        assertEquals(authors, rows.getValue("a1").authors)

+package com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore
+
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.Article
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleAuthors
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleImages

+/**
+ * The backup service writes each article's image and author rows as docs in the
+ * article's batch, writes nothing for an article without rows, keeps an article and
+ * its rows in one batch under the write threshold, and reads rows back through the
+ * row codec (never the object mapper).
+ */
+class FirestoreBackupServiceRowsTest {
+
+    private lateinit var fake: MapBackedFirestore
+    private lateinit var service: FirestoreBackupService
+
+    private val articles = "users/u1/articles"
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        fake = MapBackedFirestore()
+        service = FirestoreBackupService(fake.firestore, fake.auth)
+    }
+
+    private fun article(id: String) = Article(
+        itemId = id,
+        title = "Title $id",
+        url = "https://example.com/$id",
+        text = "body",
+    )
+
+    private fun image(itemId: String, imageId: String) = ArticleImages(
+        imageId = imageId,
+        itemId = itemId,
+        src = "https://example.com/$itemId/$imageId.jpg",
+        width = 10,
+        height = 20,
+        credit = "c",
+        caption = "cap",
+    )
+
+    private fun author(itemId: String, authorId: String) = ArticleAuthors(
+        authorId = authorId,
+        itemId = itemId,
+        name = "Name $authorId",
+        url = "https://example.com/$authorId",
+        domain = null,
+        image = null,
+        bio = null,
+        twitter = null,
+    )
+
+    @Test
+    fun articleWithoutRows_writesNoImageOrAuthorEntry() = runTest {
+        val tag = ArticleTags(itemId = "a1", tag = "t", sortId = null, type = null)
+
+        val result = service.backupArticlesPaginated(listOf(article("a1")), mapOf("a1" to listOf(tag)))
+
+        assertEquals(Result.success(1), result)
+        val articleDoc = fake.document("$articles/a1")
+        verify(exactly = 0) { articleDoc.collection("images") }
+        verify(exactly = 0) { articleDoc.collection("authors") }
+        assertEquals(listOf(listOf("$articles/a1", "$articles/a1/tags/a1_t")), fake.commits)
+    }

+    @Test
+    fun rowsAreWrittenInTheArticlesBatchWithTheCodecMap() = runTest {
+        val rows = ArticleRows(
+            images = listOf(image("a1", "1"), image("a1", "2")),
+            authors = listOf(author("a1", "7")),
+        )
+
+        val result = service.backupArticlesPaginated(listOf(article("a1")), rowsByArticleId = mapOf("a1" to rows))
+

+            listOf("$articles/a1", "$articles/a1/images/1", "$articles/a1/images/2", "$articles/a1/authors/7"),

+        assertEquals(image("a1", "2").toFirestoreFields(), fake.docs["$articles/a1/images/2"])
+        assertEquals(author("a1", "7").toFirestoreFields(), fake.docs["$articles/a1/authors/7"])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowsCountTowardTheWriteThreshold_andNoArticleIsSplit() = runTest {
+        val ids = (1..20).map { "a$it" }
+        // 1 article doc + 30 rows = 31 writes per article: 16 articles fit under 500.
+        val rowsById = ids.associateWith { id ->
+            ArticleRows(images = (1..20).map { image(id, "$it") }, authors = (1..10).map { author(id, "$id-$it") })
+        }
+
+        val result = service.backupArticlesPaginated(ids.map { article(it) }, rowsByArticleId = rowsById)
+
+        assertEquals(Result.success(20), result)

+        val seenArticles = mutableListOf<String>()
+        fake.commits.forEach { paths ->

+            val batchArticles = paths.map { it.removePrefix("$articles/").substringBefore('/') }.toSet()
+            batchArticles.forEach { id ->
+                // Every write of an article sits in the one batch that holds its doc.
+                assertEquals(31, paths.count { it.removePrefix("$articles/").substringBefore('/') == id })
+            }
+            seenArticles += batchArticles
+        }
+        assertEquals(ids.toSet(), seenArticles.toSet())
+        assertEquals(ids.size, seenArticles.size)
+    }
+

+    }
+

+        fake.docs["$articles/a1/images/2"] = image("a1", "2").toFirestoreFields()
+        fake.docs["$articles/a1/images/3"] = image("a1", "3").toFirestoreFields() - "src"
+        fake.docs["$articles/a1/authors/7"] = author("a1", "7").toFirestoreFields()
+        fake.docs["$articles/a1/authors/8"] = author("a1", "8").toFirestoreFields() + ("name" to "")
+
+        val images = service.restoreArticleImages("a1").getOrThrow()
+        val authors = service.restoreArticleAuthors("a1").getOrThrow()
+
+        assertEquals(setOf(image("a1", "1"), image("a1", "2")), images.toSet())
+        assertEquals(2, images.size)
+        assertEquals(listOf(author("a1", "7")), authors)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun batchRestoreArticleRows_leavesOutAnArticleWhoseReadFailed() = runTest {
+        fake.docs["$articles/a/images/1"] = image("a", "1").toFirestoreFields()
+        fake.docs["$articles/b/images/1"] = image("b", "1").toFirestoreFields()
+        fake.failingReads += "$articles/b/images"
+
+        val rows = service.batchRestoreArticleRows(listOf("a", "b", "c"))
+
+        assertEquals(setOf("a", "c"), rows.keys)
+        assertEquals(ArticleRows(images = listOf(image("a", "1"))), rows["a"])
+        assertEquals(ArticleRows(), rows["c"])
+    }
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,110 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.testing
+
+import com.google.android.gms.tasks.Task
+import com.google.android.gms.tasks.Tasks
+import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
+import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseUser
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.CollectionReference
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.DocumentReference
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.DocumentSnapshot
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.QueryDocumentSnapshot
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.QuerySnapshot
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.SetOptions
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.WriteBatch
+import io.mockk.every
+import io.mockk.mockk
+
+/**
+ * A Firestore stand-in that keeps committed docs in a map keyed by path, for JVM tests
+ * of the real backup service.
+ *
+ * Batches record each `set(ref, data, options)` and apply them to [docs] on `commit()`,
+ * merging fields as `SetOptions.merge()` does. A collection's `get()` serves the docs
+ * stored directly under its path as query snapshots (`id`, `data`); `toObject` is never
+ * stubbed, so the code under test has to read the stored maps. A field map is stored as
+ * it is; any other written object is stored under the key `"object"`.
+ *
+ * References are cached per path, so a test can verify calls on
+ * [document]`(path)` / [collection]`(path)`.
+ */
+class MapBackedFirestore(uid: String = "u1") {
+    val firestore: FirebaseFirestore = mockk()
+    val auth: FirebaseAuth = mockk()
+
+    /** Committed docs by path. */
+    val docs = linkedMapOf<String, Map<String, Any?>>()
+
+    /** One entry per committed batch: the doc paths it set, in order. */
+    val commits = mutableListOf<List<String>>()
+
+    /** Collection paths whose `get()` fails. */
+    val failingReads = mutableSetOf<String>()
+
+    private val collections = mutableMapOf<String, CollectionReference>()
+    private val documents = mutableMapOf<String, DocumentReference>()
+
+    init {
+        val user = mockk<FirebaseUser>()
+        every { user.uid } returns uid
+        every { auth.currentUser } returns user
+        every { firestore.collection(any()) } answers { collection(firstArg()) }
+        every { firestore.batch() } answers { newBatch() }
+    }
+
+    fun collection(path: String): CollectionReference = collections.getOrPut(path) {
+        val ref = mockk<CollectionReference>()
+        every { ref.path } returns path
+        every { ref.document(any()) } answers { document("$path/${firstArg<String>()}") }
+        every { ref.get() } answers { query(path) }
+        ref
+    }
+
+    fun document(path: String): DocumentReference = documents.getOrPut(path) {
+        val ref = mockk<DocumentReference>()
+        every { ref.path } returns path
+        every { ref.id } returns path.substringAfterLast('/')
+        every { ref.collection(any()) } answers { collection("$path/${firstArg<String>()}") }
+        ref
+    }
+
+    /** Docs stored directly under [collectionPath], keyed by doc id. */
+    fun docsUnder(collectionPath: String): Map<String, Map<String, Any?>> =
+        docs.filterKeys { it.startsWith("$collectionPath/") && '/' !in it.removePrefix("$collectionPath/") }
+            .mapKeys { it.key.removePrefix("$collectionPath/") }
+
+    private fun query(path: String): Task<QuerySnapshot> {
+        if (path in failingReads) return Tasks.forException(IllegalStateException("read failed: $path"))
+        val snapshots: List<DocumentSnapshot> = docsUnder(path).map { (id, data) ->
+            val snap = mockk<QueryDocumentSnapshot>()
+            every { snap.id } returns id
+            @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST") // sdlc-debt: Firestore types getData() as Map<String, Any>; the fake stores nullable values as written.
+            every { snap.data } returns data as Map<String, Any>
+            snap
+        }
+        val result = mockk<QuerySnapshot>()
+        every { result.documents } returns snapshots
+        every { result.isEmpty } returns snapshots.isEmpty()
+        every { result.size() } returns snapshots.size
+        return Tasks.forResult(result)
+    }
+
+    private fun newBatch(): WriteBatch {
+        val pending = mutableListOf<Pair<String, Map<String, Any?>>>()
+        val batch = mockk<WriteBatch>()
+        every { batch.set(any<DocumentReference>(), any<Any>(), any<SetOptions>()) } answers {
+            val ref = firstArg<DocumentReference>()
+            val data = secondArg<Any>()
+            @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST") // sdlc-debt: written field maps are Map<String, Any?>; anything else is wrapped.
+            pending += ref.path to ((data as? Map<String, Any?>) ?: mapOf("object" to data))
+            batch
+        }
+        every { batch.commit() } answers {
+            pending.forEach { (path, fields) -> docs[path] = docs[path].orEmpty() + fields }
+            commits += pending.map { it.first }
+            pending.clear()
+            Tasks.forResult(null)
+        }
+        return batch
+    }
+}

+import android.app.Application

+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleImages

+/**
+ * The image and author row queries against a real in-memory database on the bundled
+ * SQLite engine: the readers return only the asked articles' rows, the restore insert
+ * upserts and leaves the article alone, and rows added on this device bring the article
+ * back into the next push.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class ArticleRowsQueriesTest {

+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+    private lateinit var dao: ArticleDao
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        db = JvmRoomDatabase.create(RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication())
+        dao = db.articleDao()
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+    }
+
+    private fun article(itemId: String, timeUpdated: Long = 2_000) = Article(
+        itemId = itemId,
+        title = "Article $itemId",
+        url = "https://example.com/$itemId",
+        timeAdded = 1_000,
+        timeUpdated = timeUpdated,
+    )
+
+    private fun image(itemId: String, imageId: String = "1") = ArticleImages(
+        imageId = imageId,
+        itemId = itemId,
+        src = "https://example.com/$itemId/$imageId.jpg",
+        width = 100,
+        height = 50,
+        credit = "",
+        caption = "",
+    )
+
+    private fun author(itemId: String) = ArticleAuthors(
+        authorId = "$itemId:author",
+        itemId = itemId,
+        name = "Author of $itemId",
+        url = "",
+        domain = null,
+        image = null,
+        bio = null,
+        twitter = null,
+    )
+
+    @Test
+    fun readers_returnOnlyTheAskedArticlesRows() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("a"), article("b"), article("c")))
+        dao.insertArticleRows(
+            listOf(image("a"), image("a", "2"), image("b"), image("c")),
+            listOf(author("a"), author("b"), author("c")),
+        )
+
+        val images = dao.getImagesForArticles(listOf("a", "b"))
+        val authors = dao.getAuthorsForArticles(listOf("a", "b"))
+
+        assertEquals(setOf(image("a"), image("a", "2"), image("b")), images.toSet())
+        assertEquals(3, images.size)
+        assertEquals(setOf(author("a"), author("b")), authors.toSet())
+        assertEquals(2, authors.size)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun insertArticleRows_twice_keepsOneCopy() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("a")))
+
+        repeat(2) { dao.insertArticleRows(listOf(image("a")), listOf(author("a"))) }
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(image("a")), dao.getImagesForArticles(listOf("a")))
+        assertEquals(listOf(author("a")), dao.getAuthorsForArticles(listOf("a")))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun addLocalArticleRows_storesRowsAndBringsTheArticleBackIntoThePush() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("a", timeUpdated = 2_000), article("newer", timeUpdated = 10_000)))
+
+        dao.addLocalArticleRows("a", listOf(image("a")), listOf(author("a")), now = 9_000)
+        dao.addLocalArticleRows("newer", listOf(image("newer")), emptyList(), now = 9_000)
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(image("a")), dao.getImagesForArticles(listOf("a")))
+        assertEquals(listOf(author("a")), dao.getAuthorsForArticles(listOf("a")))
+        assertEquals(9_000L, dao.getArticleById("a")!!.timeUpdated)
+        // timeUpdated is never lowered.
+        assertEquals(10_000L, dao.getArticleById("newer")!!.timeUpdated)
+        val pushed = dao.getArticlesModifiedSincePaginated(5_000, 50, 0).map { it.itemId }
+        assertTrue("a" in pushed)
+    }

+package com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore
+
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleAuthors
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleImages
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+import java.net.URLDecoder
+
+/**
+ * The image and author row codec: what the backup writes reads back as the same row,
+ * Firestore's number and missing-field shapes are handled, and row ids make valid,
+ * reversible doc ids.
+ */
+class ArticleRowsCodecTest {
+
+    private val image = ArticleImages(
+        imageId = "1",
+        itemId = "a1",
+        src = "https://example.com/lead.jpg",
+        width = 640,
+        height = 480,
+        credit = "Photo: someone",
+        caption = "A caption",
+    )
+
+    private val author = ArticleAuthors(
+        authorId = "7",
+        itemId = "a1",
+        name = "Ada Writer",
+        url = "https://example.com/ada",
+        domain = "example.com",
+        image = null,
+        bio = "Writes things",
+        twitter = null,
+    )
+
+    @Test
+    fun image_roundTripsThroughTheFieldMap() {
+        val fields = image.toFirestoreFields()
+
+        assertEquals(image, imageFromFirestore(rowDocId(image.imageId), fields, "a1"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun author_roundTripsThroughTheFieldMap() {
+        val fields = author.toFirestoreFields()
+
+        assertEquals(author, authorFromFirestore(rowDocId(author.authorId), fields, "a1"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun image_firestoreLongNumbersBecomeInts() {
+        // Firestore hands integers back as Long.
+        val stored = image.toFirestoreFields() + mapOf("width" to 640L, "height" to 480L)
+
+        val read = imageFromFirestore("1", stored, "a1")!!
+
+        assertEquals(640, read.width)
+        assertEquals(480, read.height)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun image_missingOrBlankSrc_isSkipped() {
+        assertNull(imageFromFirestore("1", image.toFirestoreFields() - "src", "a1"))
+        assertNull(imageFromFirestore("1", image.toFirestoreFields() + ("src" to " "), "a1"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun author_missingOrBlankName_isSkipped() {
+        assertNull(authorFromFirestore("7", author.toFirestoreFields() - "name", "a1"))
+        assertNull(authorFromFirestore("7", author.toFirestoreFields() + ("name" to ""), "a1"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun missingOptionalFields_takeDefaults() {
+        val readImage = imageFromFirestore("1", mapOf("src" to "https://x/y.png"), "a1")!!
+        assertEquals(0, readImage.width)
+        assertEquals(0, readImage.height)
+        assertEquals("", readImage.credit)
+        assertEquals("", readImage.caption)
+
+        val readAuthor = authorFromFirestore("7", mapOf("name" to "N"), "a1")!!
+        assertEquals("", readAuthor.url)
+        assertNull(readAuthor.domain)
+        assertNull(readAuthor.image)
+        assertNull(readAuthor.bio)
+        assertNull(readAuthor.twitter)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun missingIdField_fallsBackToTheDecodedDocId() {
+        val docId = rowDocId("a/b c")
+
+        val readImage = imageFromFirestore(docId, image.toFirestoreFields() - "imageId", "a1")!!
+        val readAuthor = authorFromFirestore(docId, author.toFirestoreFields() - "authorId", "a1")!!
+
+        assertEquals("a/b c", readImage.imageId)
+        assertEquals("a/b c", readAuthor.authorId)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun parentItemId_winsOverAStoredOne() {
+        val stored = image.toFirestoreFields() + ("itemId" to "other")
+        val storedAuthor = author.toFirestoreFields() + ("itemId" to "other")
+
+        assertEquals("a1", imageFromFirestore("1", stored, "a1")!!.itemId)
+        assertEquals("a1", authorFromFirestore("7", storedAuthor, "a1")!!.itemId)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowDocId_keepsOrdinaryIds_andMakesEveryIdAValidReversibleDocId() {
+        assertEquals("1", rowDocId("1"))
+        for (id in listOf("a/b", ".", "..", "__a__", "x y", "a%2Fb", "a1:byline")) {
+            val docId = rowDocId(id)
+            assertFalse("$id -> $docId has a slash", docId.contains('/'))
+            assertFalse("$id -> $docId is a reserved id", docId == "." || docId == "..")
+            assertFalse(
+                "$id -> $docId is a reserved __...__ id",
+                docId.startsWith("__") && docId.endsWith("__")
+            )
+            assertEquals(id, URLDecoder.decode(docId, "UTF-8"))
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun groupRows_keysByItemId_andLeavesArticlesWithoutRowsOut() {
+        val imageB = image.copy(imageId = "2", itemId = "b1")
+        val authorC = author.copy(authorId = "9", itemId = "c1")
+
+        val grouped = groupRows(listOf(image, imageB), listOf(author, authorC))
+
+        assertEquals(setOf("a1", "b1", "c1"), grouped.keys)
+        assertEquals(ArticleRows(listOf(image), listOf(author)), grouped["a1"])
+        assertEquals(ArticleRows(listOf(imageB), emptyList()), grouped["b1"])
+        assertEquals(ArticleRows(emptyList(), listOf(authorC)), grouped["c1"])
+        assertTrue(groupRows(emptyList(), emptyList()).isEmpty())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun articleRows_countsOneWritePerRow() {
+        assertTrue(ArticleRows().isEmpty())
+        assertEquals(0, ArticleRows().writeCount)
+        assertEquals(3, ArticleRows(listOf(image, image.copy(imageId = "2")), listOf(author)).writeCount)
+    }

+    /**
+     * Restore authors for a specific article, through the row codec as
+     * [restoreArticleImages] does. A doc without a usable `name` is skipped.
+     */
+    suspend fun restoreArticleAuthors(articleId: String): Result<List<ArticleAuthors>> {
+        return withAuthenticatedUser { user ->
+            try {
+                val snapshot = getUserArticlesCollection(user.uid)
+                    .document(articleId)
+                    .collection(AUTHORS_COLLECTION)
+                    .get()
+                    .await()
+
+                val authors = snapshot.documents.mapNotNull { doc ->
+                    authorFromFirestore(doc.id, doc.data ?: emptyMap(), articleId)
+                }
+
+                Result.success(authors)
+            } catch (e: CancellationException) {
+                throw e
+            } catch (e: Exception) {
+                Timber.e(e, "Failed to restore authors for article $articleId")
+                Result.failure(e)
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Restore the image and author rows of several articles, [RESTORE_TAG_CHUNK_SIZE]
+     * articles per round in parallel (two reads per article).
+     *
+     * An article whose images or authors read failed is left out of the map, so the
+     * caller changes nothing for it; an article without rows maps to an empty
+     * [ArticleRows]. Returns an empty map when [articleIds] is empty or no user is
+     * signed in.
+     */
+    suspend fun batchRestoreArticleRows(articleIds: List<String>): Map<String, ArticleRows> {
+        if (articleIds.isEmpty()) return emptyMap()
+        if (getCurrentUser() == null) return emptyMap()
+        val restored = mutableMapOf<String, ArticleRows>()
+        for (chunk in articleIds.chunked(RESTORE_TAG_CHUNK_SIZE)) {
+            val results = coroutineScope {
+                chunk.map { articleId ->
+                    async { articleId to (restoreArticleImages(articleId) to restoreArticleAuthors(articleId)) }
+                }.awaitAll()
+            }
+            results.forEach { (articleId, reads) ->
+                val images = reads.first.getOrNull()
+                val authors = reads.second.getOrNull()
+                if (images == null || authors == null) {
+                    Timber.w(
+                        reads.first.exceptionOrNull() ?: reads.second.exceptionOrNull(),
+                        "batchRestoreArticleRows: failed to fetch rows for $articleId"
+                    )
+                } else {
+                    restored[articleId] = ArticleRows(images, authors)
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        return restored
+    }

      */

diff --git a/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/services/firestore/ArticleRowsCodec.kt b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/services/firestore/ArticleRowsCodec.kt

index 0000000..13b689a
