+++ b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/services/firestore/ArticleRowsCodec.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,102 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore
+
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleAuthors
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleImages
+import java.net.URLDecoder
+import java.net.URLEncoder
+
+/**
+ * The image and author rows of one article, as the backup writes them and the
+ * restore reads them back.
+ */
+data class ArticleRows(
+    val images: List<ArticleImages> = emptyList(),
+    val authors: List<ArticleAuthors> = emptyList(),
+) {
+    /** One Firestore doc per row. */
+    val writeCount: Int get() = images.size + authors.size
+
+    fun isEmpty(): Boolean = writeCount == 0
+}
+
+/**
+ * The Firestore doc id for a row id. URL-encoding keeps a `/` from nesting the path.
+ * Firestore also refuses the ids `.`, `..` and `__…__`, which URL-encoding leaves
+ * as they are, so those characters are escaped too. [URLDecoder] reverses every case.
+ */
+internal fun rowDocId(id: String): String {
+    val encoded = URLEncoder.encode(id, "UTF-8")
+    return when {
+        encoded == "." || encoded == ".." -> encoded.replace(".", "%2E")
+        encoded.length >= 4 && encoded.startsWith("__") && encoded.endsWith("__") ->
+            encoded.replace("_", "%5F")
+        else -> encoded
+    }
+}
+
+private fun idFromDocId(docId: String): String = URLDecoder.decode(docId, "UTF-8")
+
+internal fun ArticleImages.toFirestoreFields(): Map<String, Any?> = mapOf(
+    "imageId" to imageId,
+    "itemId" to itemId,
+    "src" to src,
+    "width" to width,
+    "height" to height,
+    "credit" to credit,
+    "caption" to caption,
+)
+
+internal fun ArticleAuthors.toFirestoreFields(): Map<String, Any?> = mapOf(
+    "authorId" to authorId,
+    "itemId" to itemId,
+    "name" to name,
+    "url" to url,
+    "domain" to domain,
+    "image" to image,
+    "bio" to bio,
+    "twitter" to twitter,
+)
+
+/**
+ * Read an image row from a stored doc. The parent article id [itemId] wins over a
+ * stored one. Returns null when the doc has no usable `src`.
+ */
+internal fun imageFromFirestore(docId: String, data: Map<String, Any?>, itemId: String): ArticleImages? {
+    val src = (data["src"] as? String)?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: return null
+    return ArticleImages(
+        imageId = data["imageId"] as? String ?: idFromDocId(docId),
+        itemId = itemId,
+        src = src,
+        width = (data["width"] as? Number)?.toInt() ?: 0,
+        height = (data["height"] as? Number)?.toInt() ?: 0,
+        credit = data["credit"] as? String ?: "",
+        caption = data["caption"] as? String ?: "",
+    )
+}
+
+/**
+ * Read an author row from a stored doc. The parent article id [itemId] wins over a
+ * stored one. Returns null when the doc has no usable `name`.
+ */
+internal fun authorFromFirestore(docId: String, data: Map<String, Any?>, itemId: String): ArticleAuthors? {
+    val name = (data["name"] as? String)?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: return null
+    return ArticleAuthors(
+        authorId = data["authorId"] as? String ?: idFromDocId(docId),
+        itemId = itemId,
+        name = name,
+        url = data["url"] as? String ?: "",
+        domain = data["domain"] as? String,
+        image = data["image"] as? String,
+        bio = data["bio"] as? String,
+        twitter = data["twitter"] as? String,
+    )
+}
+
+/** Group rows by article id; articles without rows are absent. */
+internal fun groupRows(images: List<ArticleImages>, authors: List<ArticleAuthors>): Map<String, ArticleRows> {
+    val imagesById = images.groupBy { it.itemId }
+    val authorsById = authors.groupBy { it.itemId }
+    return (imagesById.keys + authorsById.keys).associateWith { id ->
+        ArticleRows(imagesById[id].orEmpty(), authorsById[id].orEmpty())
+    }
+}

+        coEvery { firestoreBackupService.backupArticlesPaginated(listOf(good, poison), any(), any()) } returns Result.failure(tooBig)
+        coEvery { firestoreBackupService.backupArticlesPaginated(listOf(good), any(), any()) } returns Result.success(1)
+        coEvery { firestoreBackupService.backupArticlesPaginated(listOf(poison), any(), any()) } returns Result.failure(tooBig)
+
+        val result = manager.pushLocalChanges()
+
+        assertEquals(SyncRunStatus.PARTIAL_FAILURE, result.status)
+        assertEquals(1, result.pushed)
+        assertEquals(1, result.failed)
+        coVerify(exactly = 1) { articleDao.recordPushResults(listOf("p1"), listOf("p2"), any()) }
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { firestoreBackupService.updateLastSyncTimestamp(any()) }
+        // The sweep is skipped after a failure, so nothing else is pushed this run.
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { articleDao.getArticlesNeverBackedUp(any(), any()) }
+        assertEquals(0L, manager.lastSyncTime.value)
+    }

    wip(android): test the version 7 to 8 upgrade on the JVM

diff --git a/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/Migration7To8JvmTest.kt b/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/Migration7To8JvmTest.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..50f73a5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/data/local/database/Migration7To8JvmTest.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,178 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database
+
+import android.app.Application
+import android.content.Context
+import androidx.room3.Room
+import androidx.room3.useReaderConnection
+import androidx.sqlite.SQLiteConnection
+import androidx.sqlite.SQLiteStatement
+import androidx.sqlite.driver.bundled.BundledSQLiteDriver
+import androidx.sqlite.execSQL
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.json.JSONObject
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+import java.io.File
+
+/**
+ * A version 7 library opens in the current app with every row intact and the new failed-push
+ * counter at 0 on every article.
+ *
+ * The version 7 file is built on the host from the exported version 7 schema with the bundled
+ * engine, then opened through [configureAppDatabase] exactly as the app opens it, so Room runs
+ * the 7 to 8 migration and validates the result against the current schema. This runs on the
+ * JVM; the instrumented [MigrationTest] case checks the same step on a device.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class Migration7To8JvmTest {
+
+    private val dbName = "migration-7-8-jvm"
+    private lateinit var context: Context
+    private lateinit var schema7: JSONObject
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        context = RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication()
+        context.deleteDatabase(dbName)
+        context.getSharedPreferences("db_migrations", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit().clear().commit()
+        schema7 = JSONObject(schemaFile(7).readText()).getJSONObject("database")
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        context.deleteDatabase(dbName)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun version7Library_migratesTo8_keepsEveryRow_andCounterIsZero() = runTest {
+        val before = createVersion7Library()
+        assertEquals(3, before.getValue("article").size)
+        assertEquals(2, before.getValue("article_tags").size)
+        assertEquals(1, before.getValue("article_images").size)
+
+        val db = Room.databaseBuilder(context, AppDatabase::class.java, dbName)
+            .configureAppDatabase(context)
+            .build()
+        try {
+            // The first DAO call opens the database: Room migrates 7 to 8 and validates the schema.
+            // The library count leaves out the deleted row a2.
+            assertEquals(2, db.articleDao().countAllArticles())
+
+            db.useReaderConnection { connection ->
+                // Every version 7 column of every row reads back unchanged.
+                for ((table, rows) in before) {
+                    val columns = version7Columns(table)
+                    val after = connection.usePrepared(selectAll(table, columns)) { it.rows(columns.size) }
+                    assertEquals("rows of $table", rows, after)
+                }
+                assertEquals(
+                    listOf("0", "0", "0"),
+                    connection.usePrepared("SELECT sync_failures FROM article ORDER BY itemId") { it.texts() },
+                )
+                assertEquals(listOf("8"), connection.usePrepared("PRAGMA user_version") { it.texts() })
+            }
+        } finally {
+            db.close()
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** Creates the version 7 file from `7.json` and fills it; returns every row of each seeded table. */
+    private fun createVersion7Library(): Map<String, List<List<String?>>> {
+        val path = context.getDatabasePath(dbName)
+        path.parentFile?.mkdirs()
+        val connection = BundledSQLiteDriver().open(path.absolutePath)
+        try {
+            val entities = schema7.getJSONArray("entities")
+            for (i in 0 until entities.length()) {
+                val entity = entities.getJSONObject(i)
+                val table = entity.getString("tableName")
+                connection.execSQL(entity.getString("createSql").replace("\${TABLE_NAME}", table))
+                entity.optJSONArray("indices")?.let { indices ->
+                    for (j in 0 until indices.length()) {
+                        connection.execSQL(indices.getJSONObject(j).getString("createSql").replace("\${TABLE_NAME}", table))
+                    }
+                }
+                entity.optJSONArray("contentSyncTriggers")?.let { triggers ->
+                    for (j in 0 until triggers.length()) connection.execSQL(triggers.getString(j))
+                }
+            }
+            val setup = schema7.getJSONArray("setupQueries")
+            for (i in 0 until setup.length()) connection.execSQL(setup.getString(i))
+            connection.execSQL("PRAGMA user_version = 7")
+
+            // normalized_url is filled, so the open callback's URL backfill leaves the rows as they are.
+            connection.insertArticle("a1", "Growing roses", deletedAt = null, backedUpAt = null)
+            connection.insertArticle("a2", "Pruning apple trees", deletedAt = 5_000, backedUpAt = null)
+            connection.insertArticle("a3", "Birch bark notes", deletedAt = null, backedUpAt = 4_000)
+            connection.execSQL("INSERT INTO article_tags (itemId, tag, sortId, type) VALUES ('a1', 'garden', 1, 'user')")
+            connection.execSQL("INSERT INTO article_tags (itemId, tag) VALUES ('a3', 'trees')")
+            connection.execSQL(
+                "INSERT INTO article_images (imageId, itemId, src, width, height, credit, caption) " +
+                    "VALUES ('img1', 'a1', 'https://example.com/roses.png', 640, 480, 'Ann', 'Roses')"
+            )
+
+            assertEquals(listOf("7"), connection.texts("PRAGMA user_version"))
+            return listOf("article", "article_tags", "article_images")
+                .associateWith { connection.dumpTable(it, version7Columns(it)) }
+        } finally {
+            connection.close()
+        }
+    }
+
+    private fun SQLiteConnection.insertArticle(itemId: String, title: String, deletedAt: Long?, backedUpAt: Long?) {
+        execSQL(
+            "INSERT INTO article (itemId, resolvedId, title, givenTitle, url, givenUrl, normalized_url, excerpt, " +
+                "wordCount, status, hasImage, hasVideo, hasAudio, sortId, timeAdded, timeUpdated, timeFavorited, " +
+                "listenDurationEstimate, text, articleId, resolved, deleted_at, text_source, backed_up_at) " +
+                "VALUES ('$itemId', 'r-$itemId', '$title', '$title', 'https://example.com/$itemId', " +
+                "'https://example.com/$itemId', 'example.com/$itemId', 'About $title', 120, '0', 1, 0, 0, 3, " +
+                "1000, 2000, 0, 60, 'Body of $title', 'p-$itemId', 1, ${deletedAt ?: "NULL"}, 'warg', " +
+                "${backedUpAt ?: "NULL"})"
+        )
+    }
+
+    private fun version7Columns(table: String): List<String> {
+        val entities = schema7.getJSONArray("entities")
+        for (i in 0 until entities.length()) {
+            val entity = entities.getJSONObject(i)
+            if (entity.getString("tableName") != table) continue
+            val fields = entity.getJSONArray("fields")
+            return (0 until fields.length()).map { fields.getJSONObject(it).getString("columnName") }
+        }
+        error("table $table is not in the version 7 schema")
+    }
+
+    /** Every row of [table] as text, ordered by all of [columns]. */
+    private fun SQLiteConnection.dumpTable(table: String, columns: List<String>): List<List<String?>> =
+        prepare(selectAll(table, columns)).use { it.rows(columns.size) }
+            .also { assertTrue("no rows in $table", it.isNotEmpty()) }
+
+    private fun selectAll(table: String, columns: List<String>): String {
+        val list = columns.joinToString(", ") { "`$it`" }
+        return "SELECT $list FROM `$table` ORDER BY $list"
+    }
+
+    private fun SQLiteStatement.rows(columnCount: Int): List<List<String?>> = buildList {
+        while (step()) add((0 until columnCount).map { if (isNull(it)) null else getText(it) })
+    }
+
+    private fun SQLiteStatement.texts(): List<String> = buildList { while (step()) add(getText(0)) }
+
+    private fun SQLiteConnection.texts(sql: String): List<String> = prepare(sql).use { it.texts() }
+
+    /** The exported schema of [version]; Gradle runs unit tests from the module directory. */
+    private fun schemaFile(version: Int): File {
+        val relative = "schemas/com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.AppDatabase/$version.json"
+        return listOf(File(relative), File("app/$relative")).firstOrNull { it.isFile }
+            ?: error("schema $relative not found from ${File(".").absolutePath}")
+    }
+}

diff --git a/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/sync/workers/SyncWorkerResultTest.kt b/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/sync/workers/SyncWorkerResultTest.kt

+package com.jayteealao.trails.sync.workers
+
+import android.app.Application
+import android.content.Context
+import androidx.work.BackoffPolicy
+import androidx.work.Configuration
+import androidx.work.ListenableWorker
+import androidx.work.NetworkType
+import androidx.work.testing.SynchronousExecutor
+import androidx.work.testing.TestListenableWorkerBuilder
+import androidx.work.testing.WorkManagerTestInitHelper
+import com.google.firebase.FirebaseNetworkException
+import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
+import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseUser
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreException
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.ArticleRepositoryImpl

+import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOf
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.StandardTestDispatcher
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertSame
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+import java.io.IOException
+
+/**
+ * The sync and restore workers map each run outcome to the WorkManager result (retry on
+ * any failure, success when everything was pushed or nobody is signed in); every sync
+ * request uses exponential backoff and needs a connected network; a backup call that
+ * never completes stops the run as a timeout; and the failure classifier tells transient
+ * failures from an article's own.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class SyncWorkerResultTest {
+
+    private lateinit var context: Context
+    private lateinit var syncManager: FirestoreSyncManager
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        context = RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication()
+        // The workers' foreground notification reads WorkManager for its cancel intent.
+        WorkManagerTestInitHelper.initializeTestWorkManager(
+            context,
+            Configuration.Builder().setExecutor(SynchronousExecutor()).build()
+        )
+        syncManager = mockk()
+    }
+
+    private suspend fun runSyncWorker(): ListenableWorker.Result {
+        val worker = TestListenableWorkerBuilder<FirestoreSyncWorker>(context).build()
+        worker.firestoreSyncManager = syncManager
+        return worker.doWork()
+    }
+
+    private suspend fun runRestoreWorker(): ListenableWorker.Result {
+        val worker = TestListenableWorkerBuilder<FirestoreRestoreWorker>(context).build()
+        worker.firestoreSyncManager = syncManager
+        return worker.doWork()
+    }
+
+    // ---- Worker results ------------------------------------------------------
+
+    @Test
+    fun syncWorker_mapsEachOutcome() = runTest {
+        for ((outcome, expected) in OUTCOMES) {
+            coEvery { syncManager.pushLocalChanges() } returns outcome
+            assertEquals("outcome ${outcome.status}", expected, runSyncWorker())
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun syncWorker_retriesWhenTheRunThrows() = runTest {
+        coEvery { syncManager.pushLocalChanges() } throws IllegalStateException("boom")
+
+        assertEquals(ListenableWorker.Result.retry(), runSyncWorker())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun restoreWorker_mapsEachOutcome() = runTest {
+        for ((outcome, expected) in OUTCOMES) {
+            coEvery { syncManager.fullSync() } returns outcome
+            assertEquals("outcome ${outcome.status}", expected, runRestoreWorker())
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun restoreWorker_retriesWhenTheRunThrows() = runTest {
+        coEvery { syncManager.fullSync() } throws IllegalStateException("boom")
+
+        assertEquals(ListenableWorker.Result.retry(), runRestoreWorker())
+    }
+
+    // ---- Requests ------------------------------------------------------------
+
+    @Test
+    fun everySyncRequest_hasExponentialBackoffAndNeedsNetwork() {
+        val realManager = realManager(mockk(), mockk())
+        val specs = mapOf(
+            "sync" to FirestoreSyncWorker.startUpSyncWork().workSpec,
+            "restore" to FirestoreRestoreWorker.startUpRestoreWork().workSpec,
+            "periodic" to realManager.periodicSyncRequest().workSpec,
+        )
+        for ((name, spec) in specs) {
+            assertEquals(name, BackoffPolicy.EXPONENTIAL, spec.backoffPolicy)
+            assertEquals(name, NetworkType.CONNECTED, spec.constraints.requiredNetworkType)
+        }
+    }
+
+    // ---- Timeout -------------------------------------------------------------
+
+    @Test
+    fun backupThatNeverCompletes_stopsTheRunAsTimeout() = runTest {
+        val dao = mockk<ArticleDao>()
+        val backup = mockk<FirestoreBackupService>()
+        coEvery { backup.getUserMetaSnapshot() } returns
+            Result.success(FirestoreBackupService.UserMetaSnapshot(isFirstSync = false, lastSyncTimestamp = 1_000L))
+        coEvery { dao.countArticlesModifiedSince(1_000L) } returns 1
+        coEvery { dao.getArticlesModifiedSincePaginated(1_000L, 50, 0) } returns listOf(Article(itemId = "a1"))
+        coEvery { dao.getTagsForArticles(listOf("a1")) } returns emptyList()
+        // Offline: the commit waits for the server and never completes.
+        coEvery { backup.backupArticlesPaginated(any(), any(), any()) } coAnswers { awaitCancellation() }
+
+        // Virtual time under runTest: the push timeout elapses at once.
+        val result = realManager(dao, backup).pushLocalChanges()
+
+        assertTrue(result.interruptedBy is SyncTimeoutException)
+        assertEquals(0, result.pushed)
+        assertEquals(0, result.failed)
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { dao.recordPushResults(any(), any(), any()) }
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { backup.updateLastSyncTimestamp(any()) }
+    }
+
+    // ---- Classifier ----------------------------------------------------------
+
+    @Test
+    fun classifier_separatesTransientFromArticleFailures() {
+        val transientCodes = setOf(
+            FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.UNAVAILABLE,
+            FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.DEADLINE_EXCEEDED,
+            FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED,
+            FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.ABORTED,
+            FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.CANCELLED,
+            FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.INTERNAL,
+            FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.UNKNOWN,
+            FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.UNAUTHENTICATED,
+        )
+        // OK is not a failure: the SDK refuses to build an exception with it.
+        for (code in FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.values().filter { it != FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.OK }) {
+            val error = FirebaseFirestoreException("x", code)
+            assertEquals("code $code", code in transientCodes, error.isTransientSyncFailure())
+            // A wrapped Firestore failure is classified by its cause.
+            assertEquals("wrapped $code", code in transientCodes, RuntimeException(error).isTransientSyncFailure())
+        }
+        assertTrue(IOException("socket").isTransientSyncFailure())
+        assertTrue(FirebaseNetworkException("no network").isTransientSyncFailure())
+        assertTrue(SyncTimeoutException(120_000).isTransientSyncFailure())
+        assertEquals(false, IllegalArgumentException("bad field").isTransientSyncFailure())
+        assertEquals(false, IllegalStateException("bad state").isTransientSyncFailure())
+    }
+
+    // ---- Repository delegate -------------------------------------------------
+
+    @Test
+    fun repositorySyncFailureCount_isTheDaoFlow() {
+        val dao = mockk<ArticleDao>()
+        val counts = flowOf(2)
+        every { dao.observeSyncFailureCount() } returns counts
+        val repository = ArticleRepositoryImpl(
+            context = context,
+            articleDao = dao,
+            firestoreSyncManager = mockk(),
+            firestoreBackupService = mockk(),
+            coroutineScope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + StandardTestDispatcher()),
+        )
+
+        assertSame(counts, repository.syncFailureCount())
+    }
+
+    private fun realManager(dao: ArticleDao, backup: FirestoreBackupService): FirestoreSyncManager {
+        val user = mockk<FirebaseUser>().also { every { it.uid } returns "u1" }
+        val auth = mockk<FirebaseAuth>().also { every { it.currentUser } returns user }
+        return FirestoreSyncManager(
+            context = context,
+            firestore = mockk<FirebaseFirestore>(relaxed = true),
+            auth = auth,
+            articleDao = dao,
+            firestoreBackupService = backup,
+            scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + StandardTestDispatcher()),
+        )
+    }
+
+    private companion object {
+        val OUTCOMES = listOf(
+            SyncRunResult(pushed = 3, failed = 0) to ListenableWorker.Result.success(),
+            SyncRunResult(pushed = 0, failed = 0, noUser = true) to ListenableWorker.Result.success(),
+            SyncRunResult(pushed = 2, failed = 1) to ListenableWorker.Result.retry(),
+            SyncRunResult(pushed = 0, failed = 1) to ListenableWorker.Result.retry(),
+            SyncRunResult(pushed = 0, failed = 0, interruptedBy = IOException("offline")) to
+                ListenableWorker.Result.retry(),
+        )
+    }

+package com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore
+
+import android.app.Application
+import android.content.Context
+import androidx.work.Configuration
+import androidx.work.ListenableWorker
+import androidx.work.testing.SynchronousExecutor
+import androidx.work.testing.TestListenableWorkerBuilder
+import androidx.work.testing.WorkManagerTestInitHelper
+import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
+import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseUser
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreException
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.AppDatabase
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.Article
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.ArticleDao
+import com.jayteealao.trails.sync.workers.FirestoreSyncWorker
+import com.jayteealao.trails.testing.JvmRoomDatabase
+import io.mockk.coEvery
+import io.mockk.coVerify
+import io.mockk.every
+import io.mockk.mockk
+import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
+import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
+import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.StandardTestDispatcher
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+
+/**
+ * Drives the real sync worker and the real [FirestoreSyncManager] over a real in-memory
+ * database; only the Firestore calls are faked. Proves that one failing article makes the
+ * worker ask for a retry without moving last sync while the others are stamped, that the
+ * same article failing in three runs is skipped by the fourth, which then succeeds and
+ * moves last sync, and that a transient failure stops the run without a strike.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class HonestSyncTest {
+
+    private lateinit var context: Context
+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+    private lateinit var dao: ArticleDao
+    private lateinit var backup: FirestoreBackupService
+    private lateinit var manager: FirestoreSyncManager
+
+    /** The last-sync value the fake user meta holds. */
+    private var remoteLastSync = 1_000L
+    private val lastSyncWrites = mutableListOf<Long>()
+    /** Item ids of every backup call, one list per call. */
+    private val backupCalls = mutableListOf<List<String>>()
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        context = RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication()
+        // The worker's foreground notification reads WorkManager for its cancel intent.
+        WorkManagerTestInitHelper.initializeTestWorkManager(
+            context,
+            Configuration.Builder().setExecutor(SynchronousExecutor()).build()
+        )
+        db = JvmRoomDatabase.create(context)
+        dao = db.articleDao()
+
+        val user = mockk<FirebaseUser>().also { every { it.uid } returns "u1" }
+        val auth = mockk<FirebaseAuth>().also { every { it.currentUser } returns user }
+        backup = mockk()
+        coEvery { backup.getUserMetaSnapshot() } answers {
+            Result.success(FirestoreBackupService.UserMetaSnapshot(isFirstSync = false, lastSyncTimestamp = remoteLastSync))
+        }
+        coEvery { backup.updateLastSyncTimestamp(any()) } answers {
+            val at = firstArg<Long>()
+            lastSyncWrites += at
+            remoteLastSync = at
+            Result.success(Unit)
+        }
+        coEvery { backup.backupArticlesPaginated(any(), any(), any()) } answers {
+            val articles = firstArg<List<Article>>()
+            backupCalls += articles.map { it.itemId }
+            if (articles.any { it.itemId == POISON }) {
+                Result.failure(FirebaseFirestoreException("too big", FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.INVALID_ARGUMENT))
+            } else {
+                Result.success(articles.size)
+            }
+        }
+
+        manager = FirestoreSyncManager(
+            context = context,
+            firestore = mockk<FirebaseFirestore>(relaxed = true),
+            auth = auth,
+            articleDao = dao,
+            firestoreBackupService = backup,
+            scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + StandardTestDispatcher()),
+        )
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+    }
+
+    private fun article(itemId: String) = Article(
+        itemId = itemId,
+        title = "Article $itemId",
+        url = "https://example.com/$itemId",
+        timeAdded = 2_000,
+        timeUpdated = 2_000,
+    )
+
+    private suspend fun seedGoodAndPoison() {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("good1"), article("good2"), article(POISON)))
+    }
+
+    private suspend fun runWorker(): ListenableWorker.Result {
+        val worker = TestListenableWorkerBuilder<FirestoreSyncWorker>(context).build()
+        worker.firestoreSyncManager = manager
+        return worker.doWork()
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun oneFailingArticle_workerRetries_lastSyncStays_othersStamped() = runTest {
+        seedGoodAndPoison()
+
+        val result = runWorker()
+
+        assertEquals(ListenableWorker.Result.retry(), result)
+        assertTrue(lastSyncWrites.isEmpty())
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { backup.updateLastSyncTimestamp(any()) }
+        assertEquals(0L, manager.lastSyncTime.value)
+        assertNotNull(dao.getArticleById("good1")!!.backedUpAt)
+        assertNotNull(dao.getArticleById("good2")!!.backedUpAt)
+        val poison = dao.getArticleById(POISON)!!
+        assertNull(poison.backedUpAt)
+        assertEquals(1, poison.syncFailures)
+        // The failed chunk was retried one article at a time: the poison article went out alone once.
+        assertEquals(1, backupCalls.count { it == listOf(POISON) })
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun sameArticleFailsThreeRuns_fourthRunSkipsItAndSucceeds() = runTest {
+        seedGoodAndPoison()
+
+        for (run in 1..3) {
+            assertEquals(ListenableWorker.Result.retry(), runWorker())
+            assertEquals(run, dao.getArticleById(POISON)!!.syncFailures)
+        }
+        assertTrue(lastSyncWrites.isEmpty())
+
+        backupCalls.clear()
+        val startedNoEarlierThan = System.currentTimeMillis()
+        val fourth = runWorker()
+        val finishedNoLaterThan = System.currentTimeMillis()
+
+        assertEquals(ListenableWorker.Result.success(), fourth)
+        assertFalse(backupCalls.any { POISON in it })
+        assertTrue(backupCalls.any { "good1" in it })
+        assertEquals(1, lastSyncWrites.size)
+        assertTrue(lastSyncWrites.single() in startedNoEarlierThan..finishedNoLaterThan)
+        assertEquals(lastSyncWrites.single(), manager.lastSyncTime.value)
+        assertTrue(manager.lastSyncTime.value > 0)
+        assertEquals(1, dao.observeSyncFailureCount().first())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun transientFailure_stopsRunWithoutStrikeOrStamp() = runTest {
+        // Sixty changed articles make two chunks of the regular chunk size (50).
+        dao.upsertArticles((1..60).map { article("a$it") })
+        coEvery { backup.backupArticlesPaginated(any(), any(), any()) } answers {
+            backupCalls += firstArg<List<Article>>().map { it.itemId }
+            Result.failure(FirebaseFirestoreException("offline", FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.UNAVAILABLE))
+        }
+
+        val result = runWorker()
+
+        assertEquals(ListenableWorker.Result.retry(), result)
+        // The loop stopped after the first chunk and did not retry it article by article.
+        coVerify(exactly = 1) { backup.backupArticlesPaginated(any(), any(), any()) }
+        assertEquals(50, backupCalls.single().size)
+        val rows = (1..60).map { dao.getArticleById("a$it")!! }
+        assertTrue(rows.all { it.syncFailures == 0 && it.backedUpAt == null })
+        assertTrue(lastSyncWrites.isEmpty())
+        assertEquals(0, dao.observeSyncFailureCount().first())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun metaReadFailure_workerRetries_nothingPushed() = runTest {
+        seedGoodAndPoison()
+        coEvery { backup.getUserMetaSnapshot() } returns
+            Result.failure(FirebaseFirestoreException("offline", FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.UNAVAILABLE))
+
+        val result = runWorker()
+
+        assertEquals(ListenableWorker.Result.retry(), result)
+        coVerify(exactly = 0) { backup.backupArticlesPaginated(any(), any(), any()) }
+        assertTrue(lastSyncWrites.isEmpty())
+        assertNull(dao.getArticleById("good1")!!.backedUpAt)
+    }
+
+    private companion object {
+        const val POISON = "poison"
+    }
+}

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database
+
+import android.app.Application
+import com.jayteealao.trails.testing.JvmRoomDatabase
+import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
+import kotlinx.coroutines.channels.Channel
+import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
+import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
+import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeout
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+
+/**
+ * Runs the push-result, skip, failure-count and reset queries against a real in-memory
+ * database on the bundled SQLite engine: a pushed article is stamped and its counter
+ * cleared, a failed one gets a strike, an article at MAX_SYNC_FAILURES strikes drops out
+ * of the push and reconcile queries and is counted, and a reset brings it back into the
+ * next push window.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class SyncFailureQueriesTest {
+
+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+    private lateinit var dao: ArticleDao
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        db = JvmRoomDatabase.create(RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication())
+        dao = db.articleDao()
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+    }
+
+    private fun article(
+        itemId: String,
+        timeUpdated: Long = 2_000,
+        syncFailures: Int = 0,
+        backedUpAt: Long? = null,
+        deletedAt: Long? = null,
+    ) = Article(
+        itemId = itemId,
+        title = "Article $itemId",
+        url = "https://example.com/$itemId",
+        timeAdded = 1_000,
+        timeUpdated = timeUpdated,
+        syncFailures = syncFailures,
+        backedUpAt = backedUpAt,
+        deletedAt = deletedAt,
+    )
+
+    @Test
+    fun recordPushResults_stampsSuccessesAndStrikesFailures() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("a", syncFailures = 2), article("b"), article("c", backedUpAt = 100)))
+
+        dao.recordPushResults(listOf("a", "b"), listOf("c"), 500)
+
+        val a = dao.getArticleById("a")!!
+        val b = dao.getArticleById("b")!!
+        val c = dao.getArticleById("c")!!
+        assertEquals(500L, a.backedUpAt)
+        assertEquals(0, a.syncFailures)
+        assertEquals(500L, b.backedUpAt)
+        assertEquals(0, b.syncFailures)
+        assertEquals(1, c.syncFailures)
+        assertEquals(100L, c.backedUpAt)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun articleAtLimit_isSkippedByPushAndReconcileQueries() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("ok"), article("c", syncFailures = MAX_SYNC_FAILURES)))
+
+        assertEquals(listOf("ok"), dao.getArticlesModifiedSincePaginated(0, 50, 0).map { it.itemId })
+        assertEquals(1, dao.countArticlesModifiedSince(0))
+        assertEquals(listOf("ok"), dao.getArticlesNeverBackedUp(50, 0).map { it.itemId })
+        assertEquals(1, dao.countArticlesNeverBackedUp())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun articleBelowLimit_isStillPushed() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("ok"), article("c", syncFailures = MAX_SYNC_FAILURES - 1)))
+
+        assertEquals(setOf("ok", "c"), dao.getArticlesModifiedSincePaginated(0, 50, 0).map { it.itemId }.toSet())
+        assertEquals(2, dao.countArticlesModifiedSince(0))
+        assertEquals(setOf("ok", "c"), dao.getArticlesNeverBackedUp(50, 0).map { it.itemId }.toSet())
+        assertEquals(2, dao.countArticlesNeverBackedUp())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun failureCount_emitsOnThirdStrikeAndAfterReset() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("c", syncFailures = MAX_SYNC_FAILURES - 1)))
+        assertEquals(0, dao.observeSyncFailureCount().first())
+
+        // Real dispatcher and real-time timeout: Room delivers invalidations on its own threads.
+        withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
+            val emissions = Channel<Int>(Channel.UNLIMITED)
+            // Subscribed before the writes, so later emissions can only come from invalidation.
+            val collector = launch { dao.observeSyncFailureCount().collect { emissions.send(it) } }
+            try {
+                withTimeout(5_000) {
+                    assertEquals(0, emissions.receive())
+                    dao.recordPushResults(emptyList(), listOf("c"), 500)
+                    while (emissions.receive() != 1) Unit
+                    dao.resetSyncFailures(now = 9_000)
+                    while (emissions.receive() != 0) Unit
+                }
+            } finally {
+                collector.cancel()
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun resetSyncFailures_clearsCounterAndRaisesTimeUpdated() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(
+            listOf(
+                article("old", timeUpdated = 2_000, syncFailures = MAX_SYNC_FAILURES),
+                article("newer", timeUpdated = 10_000, syncFailures = MAX_SYNC_FAILURES),
+                article("fine", timeUpdated = 2_000, syncFailures = 1),
+            )
+        )
+
+        val reset = dao.resetSyncFailures(now = 9_000)
+
+        assertEquals(2, reset)
+        val old = dao.getArticleById("old")!!
+        assertEquals(0, old.syncFailures)
+        assertEquals(9_000L, old.timeUpdated)
+        assertEquals(10_000L, dao.getArticleById("newer")!!.timeUpdated)
+        // The row below the limit is not touched.
+        assertEquals(1, dao.getArticleById("fine")!!.syncFailures)
+        assertEquals(2_000L, dao.getArticleById("fine")!!.timeUpdated)
+        // Last sync has moved past the old edit, and the reset brings the row back.
+        assertTrue(dao.getArticlesModifiedSincePaginated(5_000, 50, 0).any { it.itemId == "old" })
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun upsertNewArticle_reSaveKeepsBackupStampAndCounter() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("x", backedUpAt = 700, syncFailures = 2)))
+
+        val id = dao.upsertNewArticle(
+            Article(itemId = "fresh-id", title = "Re-saved", url = "https://example.com/x", timeAdded = 3_000)
+        )
+
+        assertEquals("x", id)
+        val row = dao.getArticleById("x")!!
+        assertEquals(700L, row.backedUpAt)
+        assertEquals(2, row.syncFailures)
+        assertEquals(3_000L, row.timeUpdated)
+        assertNull(dao.getArticleById("fresh-id"))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun failureCount_includesDeletedRows() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("gone", syncFailures = MAX_SYNC_FAILURES, deletedAt = 4_000)))
+
+        assertEquals(1, dao.observeSyncFailureCount().first())
+    }
+}

commit 04139016bbe6c1f3c79c11195429d37d2096a64e
Author: jayteealao <jayteealao@gmail.com>
Date:   Fri Oct 23 00:12:08 2026 +0100

    wip(android): report per-article push results and move last sync only on a clean run
    
    Pushes now record which articles reached the server, stamp those as backed
    up, and give an article a strike only for a failure of its own; transient
    failures stop the run without a strike. Last sync advances only when the
    whole run succeeds. Sync requests require a connected network and back off
    exponentially.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

     }
 
     /**
-     * Sync local changes to Firestore
-     * Pushes articles modified since last sync, or all articles on first sync
-     * Uses chunked processing to avoid OutOfMemoryError with large datasets
+     * Push one chunk of changed articles and record what happened to each.
+     *
+     * The chunk goes out through one [FirestoreBackupService.backupArticlesPaginated] call.
+     * On success every article in it is stamped. A transient failure (see
+     * [isTransientSyncFailure], or no signed-in user) stops the run with nothing recorded.
+     * Any other failure retries the chunk one article at a time, so a single bad article
+     * gets the strike and the others are stamped. Stamps and strikes are written in one
+     * transaction ([ArticleDao.recordPushResults]).
+     */
+    private suspend fun pushChunk(
+        chunk: List<Article>,
+        tagsById: Map<String, List<ArticleTags>>,
+        onProgress: (current: Int, total: Int) -> Unit = { _, _ -> },
+    ): SyncRunResult {
+        if (chunk.isEmpty()) return SyncRunResult(0, 0)
+        val succeeded = mutableListOf<String>()
+        val failed = mutableListOf<String>()
+        var interruptedBy: Throwable? = null
+
+        val chunkError = backupWithTimeout(chunk, tagsById, onProgress).exceptionOrNull()
+        when {
+            chunkError == null -> chunk.mapTo(succeeded) { it.itemId }
+            isTransient(chunkError) -> {
+                Timber.w(chunkError, "Push stopped by a transient failure")
+                interruptedBy = chunkError
+            }
+            else -> {
+                Timber.w(chunkError, "Chunk push failed; retrying its ${chunk.size} articles one by one")
+                for (article in chunk) {
+                    val tags = tagsById[article.itemId]?.let { mapOf(article.itemId to it) } ?: emptyMap()
+                    val error = backupWithTimeout(listOf(article), tags).exceptionOrNull()
+                    when {
+                        error == null -> succeeded += article.itemId
+                        isTransient(error) -> {
+                            Timber.w(error, "Push stopped by a transient failure")
+                            interruptedBy = error
+                            break
+                        }
+                        else -> {
+                            Timber.e(error, "Push failed for article ${article.itemId}")
+                            failed += article.itemId
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (succeeded.isNotEmpty() || failed.isNotEmpty()) {
+            articleDao.recordPushResults(succeeded, failed, System.currentTimeMillis())
+        }
+        return SyncRunResult(succeeded.size, failed.size, interruptedBy)
+    }
+
+    /** A failure that says nothing about the article: retry the run later, no strike. */
+    private fun isTransient(error: Throwable): Boolean =
+        auth.currentUser == null || error.isTransientSyncFailure()
+
+    /**
+     * One backup call bounded by [PUSH_TIMEOUT_MS]; a call that runs out of time fails
+     * with [SyncTimeoutException].
+     */
+    private suspend fun backupWithTimeout(
+        articles: List<Article>,
+        tagsById: Map<String, List<ArticleTags>>,
+        onProgress: (current: Int, total: Int) -> Unit = { _, _ -> },
+    ): Result<Int> {
+        val result = withTimeoutOrNull(PUSH_TIMEOUT_MS) {
+            firestoreBackupService.backupArticlesPaginated(articles, tagsById, onProgress)
+        } ?: return Result.failure(SyncTimeoutException(PUSH_TIMEOUT_MS))
+        // The backup service catches every exception, the timeout's cancellation included.
+        // When this coroutine is still active, that cancellation was our timeout.
+        if (result.exceptionOrNull() is CancellationException) {
+            currentCoroutineContext().ensureActive()
+            return Result.failure(SyncTimeoutException(PUSH_TIMEOUT_MS))
+        }
+        return result
+    }

+    /** Set [syncStatus] for a finished push run, keeping the existing wording. */
+    private fun reportPushStatus(result: SyncRunResult, isFirstSync: Boolean) {
+        val interruptedBy = result.interruptedBy
+        if (interruptedBy != null && result.pushed == 0 && result.failed == 0) {
+            // Interrupted before anything was pushed: report it as a thrown run did.
+            val message = interruptedBy.message ?: "Unknown error"
+            _lastError.value = message
+            _syncStatus.value = SyncStatus.Error(message, interruptedBy as? Exception)
+            return
+        }
+        val message = if (result.failed > 0 || interruptedBy != null) {
+            "Synced ${result.pushed} articles, ${result.failed} failed"
+        } else if (isFirstSync) {
+            "Initial backup complete: ${result.pushed} articles"
+        } else {
+            "Synced ${result.pushed} articles"
+        }
+        if (interruptedBy != null) _lastError.value = interruptedBy.message ?: "Sync failed"
+        _syncStatus.value = SyncStatus.Success(message)
     }

diff --git a/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/services/firestore/SyncRunResult.kt b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/services/firestore/SyncRunResult.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..10a5e06
--- /dev/null
+++ b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/services/firestore/SyncRunResult.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore
+
+import com.google.firebase.FirebaseNetworkException
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreException
+import java.io.IOException
+
+/** What one sync run did, as the workers and callers see it. */
+enum class SyncRunStatus {
+    /** Nothing failed. Articles skipped after too many failed runs are not attempted. */
+    SUCCESS,
+
+    /** Some articles were pushed, and some failed or the run stopped early. */
+    PARTIAL_FAILURE,
+
+    /** Nothing was pushed and something failed. */
+    FAILURE,
+
+    /** No signed-in user: nothing to push. */
+    NO_USER,
+}
+
+/**
+ * The outcome of one push or full-sync run.
+ *
+ * @param pushed articles written to Firestore in this run
+ * @param failed articles whose push failed for a reason of their own (each got a strike)
+ * @param interruptedBy the transient failure that stopped the run early, if any (no strike)
+ * @param noUser true when there was no signed-in user
+ */
+data class SyncRunResult(
+    val pushed: Int,
+    val failed: Int,
+    val interruptedBy: Throwable? = null,
+    val noUser: Boolean = false,
+) {
+    val status: SyncRunStatus
+        get() = when {
+            noUser -> SyncRunStatus.NO_USER
+            failed == 0 && interruptedBy == null -> SyncRunStatus.SUCCESS
+            pushed > 0 -> SyncRunStatus.PARTIAL_FAILURE
+            else -> SyncRunStatus.FAILURE
+        }
+
+    operator fun plus(other: SyncRunResult): SyncRunResult = SyncRunResult(
+        pushed = pushed + other.pushed,
+        failed = failed + other.failed,
+        interruptedBy = interruptedBy ?: other.interruptedBy,
+        noUser = noUser || other.noUser,
+    )
+}
+
+/** A backup call that did not finish within [ms] milliseconds (for example offline). */
+class SyncTimeoutException(ms: Long) : Exception("Backup did not complete within $ms ms")
+
+private val TRANSIENT_FIRESTORE_CODES = setOf(
+    FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.UNAVAILABLE,
+    FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.DEADLINE_EXCEEDED,
+    FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED,
+    FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.ABORTED,
+    FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.CANCELLED,
+    FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.INTERNAL,
+    FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.UNKNOWN,
+    FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.UNAUTHENTICATED,
+)
+
+/**
+ * True when the failure says nothing about the article itself (network, timeout, quota,
+ * server trouble, sign-in), so the run is retried later without a strike. Any other
+ * failure (for example an oversized document or a rejected write) is the article's own.
+ * Looks at the throwable and its direct cause.
+ */
+internal fun Throwable.isTransientSyncFailure(): Boolean =
+    isTransientItself() || (cause?.isTransientItself() == true)
+
+private fun Throwable.isTransientItself(): Boolean = when (this) {
+    is FirebaseFirestoreException -> code in TRANSIENT_FIRESTORE_CODES
+    is FirebaseNetworkException -> true
+    is IOException -> true
+    is SyncTimeoutException -> true
+    else -> false
+}

+        // Add the failed-push counter. Every existing row starts at 0.
+        // Non-destructive: no data is deleted or altered.

+    }

+    @Test
+    fun allTags_dropsTagWhenItsArticleIsDeleted() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("live-3", "Solo hikes")))
+        dao.insertArticleTags(listOf(tag("live-3", "solo")))
+
+        // Real dispatcher and real-time timeout: Room delivers invalidations on its own threads.
+        withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
+            val emissions = Channel<List<String>>(Channel.UNLIMITED)
+            // Subscribed before the delete, so a later emission can only come from invalidation.
+            val collector = launch { dao.getAllTags().collect { emissions.send(it) } }
+            try {
+                withTimeout(5_000) {
+                    assertEquals(listOf("solo"), emissions.receive())
+                    dao.updateDeleted("live-3", DELETED_AT)
+                    while ("solo" in emissions.receive()) Unit
+                }
+            } finally {
+                collector.cancel()
+            }
+        }
+    }

+package com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database
+
+import android.app.Application
+import com.jayteealao.trails.network.ArticleTags
+import com.jayteealao.trails.testing.JvmRoomDatabase
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+
+/**
+ * Runs the search, tag-list and articles-by-id queries against a real in-memory database
+ * on the bundled SQLite engine, seeded with live and deleted articles, and asserts the
+ * result sets: a deleted article never comes back, and a tag that only deleted (or
+ * missing) articles carry never appears in the tag list.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class DeletedFiltersQueryTest {
+
+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+    private lateinit var dao: ArticleDao
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        db = JvmRoomDatabase.create(RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication())
+        dao = db.articleDao()
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+    }
+
+    private fun article(
+        itemId: String,
+        title: String,
+        text: String? = null,
+        deletedAt: Long? = null,
+        resolvedId: String? = null,
+    ) = Article(
+        itemId = itemId,
+        resolvedId = resolvedId,
+        title = title,
+        url = "https://example.com/$itemId",
+        text = text,
+        timeAdded = 1_600_000_000_000,
+        timeUpdated = 1_600_000_000_000,
+        deletedAt = deletedAt,
+    )
+
+    private fun tag(itemId: String, tag: String) = ArticleTags(itemId = itemId, tag = tag, sortId = null, type = null)
+
+    private companion object {
+        const val DELETED_AT = 1_700_000_000_000
+    }
+}

index 0000000..d653fef
--- /dev/null
+++ b/android/tools/db-upgrade-check/reinstall-proxy.sh
@@ -0,0 +1,85 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# Stands in for "uninstall, reinstall, restore" while keeping the signed-in session.
+#
+#   reinstall-proxy.sh <emulator-serial> <out-dir>          wipe the local library
+#   reinstall-proxy.sh <emulator-serial> --after <out-dir>  record it after the restore
+#
+# Wipe mode: stops the app, records every deleted article in the local database
+# (<out-dir>/before-wipe.deleted.txt), deletes the database files and relaunches the
+# app. The sign-in session in shared_prefs is kept, so the next sync restores the
+# library from the backup through the same path a fresh install takes.
+#
+# After mode (run once the restore has finished): records the deleted articles again
+# (<out-dir>/after-restore.deleted.txt) and the count of live articles
+# (<out-dir>/after-restore.live-count.txt).
+#
+# Needs sqlite3 on PATH. Only emulators are accepted: the script deletes app data.
+set -euo pipefail
+export MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 # keep device paths intact under Git Bash on Windows
+
+usage() { echo "usage: $0 <emulator-serial> [--after] <out-dir>" >&2; exit 2; }
+[ $# -ge 2 ] || usage
+serial=$1
+mode=wipe
+if [ "$2" = "--after" ]; then
+  [ $# -ge 3 ] || usage
+  mode=after
+  out=$3
+else
+  out=$2
+fi
+case "$serial" in
+  emulator-*) ;;
+  *) echo "refusing $serial: only an emulator serial (emulator-NNNN) is accepted" >&2; exit 2 ;;
+esac
+command -v sqlite3 >/dev/null || { echo "sqlite3 is not on PATH" >&2; exit 2; }
+
+pkg=com.jayteealao.trails
+mkdir -p "$out"
+# Working copies live under <out-dir>: a native sqlite3 on Windows cannot open /tmp paths.
+work="$out/work"
+mkdir -p "$work"
+trap 'rm -rf "$work"' EXIT
+
+dev() { adb -s "$serial" "$@"; }
+
+pull_db() { # -> $work/Pocket, with the write-ahead log folded in
+  rm -f "$work"/Pocket*
+  for f in Pocket Pocket-wal Pocket-shm; do
+    if dev shell run-as "$pkg" ls "databases/$f" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
+      dev exec-out run-as "$pkg" cat "databases/$f" >"$work/$f"
+    fi
+  done
+  [ -s "$work/Pocket" ] || { echo "no database file on the device" >&2; exit 1; }
+  sqlite3 "$work/Pocket" "PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE);" >/dev/null
+}
+
+deleted_rows() {
+  sqlite3 "$work/Pocket" "SELECT itemId, title, deleted_at FROM article WHERE deleted_at IS NOT NULL ORDER BY itemId;"
+}
+
+dev shell am force-stop "$pkg"
+pull_db
+
+if [ "$mode" = "after" ]; then
+  deleted_rows >"$out/after-restore.deleted.txt"
+  sqlite3 "$work/Pocket" "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM article WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;" >"$out/after-restore.live-count.txt"
+  echo "deleted after the restore:"
+  cat "$out/after-restore.deleted.txt"
+  echo "live articles: $(cat "$out/after-restore.live-count.txt")"
+  exit 0
+fi
+
+deleted_rows >"$out/before-wipe.deleted.txt"
+echo "deleted before the wipe:"
+cat "$out/before-wipe.deleted.txt"
+
+dev shell run-as "$pkg" rm -f databases/Pocket databases/Pocket-wal databases/Pocket-shm
+if dev shell run-as "$pkg" ls databases/Pocket >/dev/null 2>&1; then
+  echo "the database file is still on the device" >&2
+  exit 1
+fi
+echo "local library wiped; the sign-in session is kept"
+
+dev shell am start -W -n "$pkg/.MainActivity" >/dev/null
+echo "app relaunched: run the sync from Settings, then: $0 $serial --after $out"

+package com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore
+
+import android.app.Application
+import android.content.Context
+import androidx.paging.PagingSource
+import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.AppDatabase
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.Article
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.ArticleDao
+import com.jayteealao.trails.testing.JvmRoomDatabase
+import io.mockk.coEvery
+import io.mockk.mockk
+import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
+import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.StandardTestDispatcher
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+import org.junit.runner.RunWith
+import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
+import org.robolectric.RuntimeEnvironment
+import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
+
+/**
+ * A deletion survives the round trip through the real database: the deleted row is
+ * selected for the push, and after a reinstall (a fresh, empty database) a restored
+ * tombstone lands as deleted and stays out of the library list.
+ */
+@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
+// Plain Application so Robolectric does not boot the Hilt app, whose onCreate starts Firebase.
+@Config(sdk = [33], application = Application::class)
+class DeletionRoundTripTest {
+
+    private lateinit var db: AppDatabase
+    private lateinit var dao: ArticleDao
+    private lateinit var manager: FirestoreSyncManager
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        db = JvmRoomDatabase.create(RuntimeEnvironment.getApplication())
+        dao = db.articleDao()
+        val backupService = mockk<FirestoreBackupService>()
+        coEvery { backupService.batchRestoreArticleTags(any()) } returns emptyMap()
+        manager = FirestoreSyncManager(
+            context = mockk<Context>(relaxed = true),
+            firestore = mockk<FirebaseFirestore>(relaxed = true),
+            auth = mockk<FirebaseAuth>(relaxed = true),
+            articleDao = dao,
+            firestoreBackupService = backupService,
+            scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + StandardTestDispatcher()),
+        )
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        db.close()
+    }
+
+    private fun article(id: String, title: String, timeUpdated: Long = 10L, deletedAt: Long? = null) =
+        Article(
+            itemId = id,
+            title = title,
+            url = "https://example.com/$id",
+            timeAdded = timeUpdated,
+            timeUpdated = timeUpdated,
+            deletedAt = deletedAt,
+        )
+
+    private suspend fun libraryIds(): List<String> {
+        val result = dao.getArticlesWithTags().load(
+            PagingSource.LoadParams.Refresh(key = null, loadSize = 50, placeholdersEnabled = false),
+        )
+        return (result as PagingSource.LoadResult.Page).data.map { it.itemId }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun `a deleted row is selected for the push`() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("a1", "Roses"), article("a2", "Apples"), article("a3", "Pears")))
+
+        dao.updateDeleted("a2", 500L)
+
+        assertEquals(3, dao.countArticlesModifiedSince(0L))
+        val pushed = dao.getArticlesModifiedSincePaginated(0L, 50, 0).associateBy { it.itemId }
+        assertEquals(setOf("a1", "a2", "a3"), pushed.keys)
+        assertEquals(500L, pushed.getValue("a2").deletedAt)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun `after a reinstall a restored tombstone stays deleted and out of the library`() = runTest {
+        val live = article("live", "Kept article", timeUpdated = 100L)
+        val tombstone = article("gone", "Deleted article", timeUpdated = 200L, deletedAt = 200L)
+
+        manager.applyRemoteArticles(listOf(live, tombstone))
+
+        assertNotNull(dao.getArticleById("gone")?.deletedAt)
+        assertEquals(1, dao.countAllArticles())
+        assertEquals(listOf("live"), libraryIds())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun `a newer remote tombstone deletes the local live row`() = runTest {
+        dao.upsertArticles(listOf(article("x", "Shared article", timeUpdated = 100L)))
+
+        manager.applyRemoteArticles(listOf(article("x", "Shared article", timeUpdated = 200L, deletedAt = 200L)))
+
+        assertEquals(200L, dao.getArticleById("x")?.deletedAt)
+        assertTrue(libraryIds().isEmpty())
+    }
+}

diff --git a/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/services/firestore/FirestoreBackupServiceDeletionTest.kt b/android/app/src/test/java/com/jayteealao/trails/services/firestore/FirestoreBackupServiceDeletionTest.kt

+package com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore
+
+import com.google.android.gms.tasks.Tasks
+import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
+import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseUser
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.AggregateQuery
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.AggregateQuerySnapshot
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.AggregateSource
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.CollectionReference
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.DocumentReference
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.DocumentSnapshot
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FieldValue
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.Query
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.QuerySnapshot
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.SetOptions
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.WriteBatch
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.util.CustomClassMapper
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.Article
+import io.mockk.clearAllMocks
+import io.mockk.every
+import io.mockk.mockk
+import io.mockk.slot
+import io.mockk.verify
+import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
+import org.junit.After
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertSame
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Before
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/**
+ * Deletions on the wire and on restore: a deleted article is backed up under
+ * `deletedAt` only, and a restore reads `deletedAt` or the old `deleted_at` key and
+ * rewrites old docs to `deletedAt` before the page reaches the local database.
+ */
+class FirestoreBackupServiceDeletionTest {
+
+    private lateinit var firestore: FirebaseFirestore
+    private lateinit var batch: WriteBatch
+    private lateinit var articlesCollection: CollectionReference
+    private lateinit var articleDoc: DocumentReference
+    private lateinit var service: FirestoreBackupService
+
+    /** Calls in the order they happened: heal commits and onPage deliveries. */
+    private val calls = mutableListOf<String>()
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        firestore = mockk()
+        val auth = mockk<FirebaseAuth>()
+        val user = mockk<FirebaseUser>()
+        every { user.uid } returns "u1"
+        every { auth.currentUser } returns user
+
+        val usersCollection = mockk<CollectionReference>()
+        val userDoc = mockk<DocumentReference>()
+        val markersCollection = mockk<CollectionReference>()
+        articlesCollection = mockk()
+        articleDoc = mockk(relaxed = true)
+        every { firestore.collection("users") } returns usersCollection
+        every { usersCollection.document("u1") } returns userDoc
+        every { userDoc.collection("articleMarkers") } returns markersCollection
+        every { userDoc.collection("articles") } returns articlesCollection
+        every { markersCollection.document(any()) } returns mockk(relaxed = true)
+        every { articlesCollection.document(any()) } returns articleDoc
+
+        batch = mockk(relaxed = true)
+        every { firestore.batch() } returns batch
+        every { batch.commit() } answers {
+            calls += "commit"
+            Tasks.forResult(null)
+        }
+
+        service = FirestoreBackupService(firestore, auth)
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        clearAllMocks()
+    }
+
+    /** The field map Firestore receives for a written value: a map as is, a bean converted. */
+    private fun fields(value: Any): Map<*, *> =
+        value as? Map<*, *> ?: CustomClassMapper.convertToPlainJavaTypes(value) as Map<*, *>
+
+    private fun doc(
+        id: String,
+        article: Article,
+        deletedAt: Long?,
+        legacy: Long?,
+        legacyPresent: Boolean,
+    ): Pair<DocumentSnapshot, DocumentReference> {
+        val ref = mockk<DocumentReference>(name = "ref-$id")
+        val snapshot = mockk<DocumentSnapshot>()
+        every { snapshot.toObject(Article::class.java) } returns article
+        every { snapshot.getLong("deletedAt") } returns deletedAt
+        every { snapshot.getLong("deleted_at") } returns legacy
+        every { snapshot.contains("deleted_at") } returns legacyPresent
+        every { snapshot.reference } returns ref
+        every { snapshot.id } returns id
+        return snapshot to ref
+    }
+
+    private fun stubOnePage(vararg docs: DocumentSnapshot) {
+        val aggQuery = mockk<AggregateQuery>()
+        val aggSnapshot = mockk<AggregateQuerySnapshot>()
+        every { articlesCollection.count() } returns aggQuery
+        every { aggQuery.get(AggregateSource.SERVER) } returns Tasks.forResult(aggSnapshot)
+        every { aggSnapshot.count } returns docs.size.toLong()
+

     }
 
+    /**
+     * Map a restored article doc through [RemoteDeletion]: the article with its deletion
+     * read from `deletedAt` or the legacy `deleted_at`, and the heal write fields (null
+     * when the doc needs none).
+     */
+    private fun DocumentSnapshot.toRestored(): Pair<Article, Map<String, Any>?>? {
+        val article = toObject(Article::class.java) ?: return null
+        val resolution = RemoteDeletion.resolve(
+            deletedAt = getLong(RemoteDeletion.FIELD),
+            legacyDeletedAt = getLong(RemoteDeletion.LEGACY_FIELD),
+            legacyPresent = contains(RemoteDeletion.LEGACY_FIELD),
+            timeUpdated = article.timeUpdated,
+        )
+        return article.withRemoteDeletion(resolution, id) to RemoteDeletion.healFields(resolution.heal)
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Rewrite legacy `deleted_at` docs to `deletedAt` in one batch. Best effort: a
+     * failure is logged and the next restore heals again.
+     */
+    private suspend fun commitDeletionHeals(heals: List<Pair<DocumentReference, Map<String, Any>>>) {
+        if (heals.isEmpty()) return
+        try {
+            val batch = firestore.batch()
+            heals.forEach { (ref, fields) -> batch.set(ref, fields, SetOptions.merge()) }
+            batch.commit().await()
+            Timber.d("Healed ${heals.size} legacy deletion keys")
+        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
+            throw e
+        } catch (e: Exception) {
+            Timber.w(e, "Failed to heal ${heals.size} legacy deletion keys; the next restore retries")
+        }
+    }
+

      * doc was stored without inline text (text == null, meaning the text exceeded

diff --git a/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/services/firestore/RemoteDeletion.kt b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/services/firestore/RemoteDeletion.kt
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..25cff41
--- /dev/null
+++ b/android/app/src/main/java/com/jayteealao/trails/services/firestore/RemoteDeletion.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,87 @@
+package com.jayteealao.trails.services.firestore
+
+import com.google.firebase.firestore.FieldValue
+import com.jayteealao.trails.data.local.database.Article
+
+/**
+ * Decides whether a restored Firestore article doc is deleted, and which write heals it.
+ *
+ * Deletions are stored under one key, [FIELD] (`deletedAt`, the name the bean serializer
+ * writes). Older app versions wrote [LEGACY_FIELD] (`deleted_at`) with a merge instead,
+ * so a legacy doc usually carries `deletedAt: null` next to `deleted_at: <millis>`.
+ * Only a non-null value counts as a deletion.
+ *
+ * Rules:
+ * - `deletedAt` set: deleted at that time; a present legacy key is dropped.
+ * - only `deleted_at` set, at or after `timeUpdated`: deleted at that time, and the
+ *   effective `timeUpdated` becomes `max(timeUpdated, deleted_at)` (the old delete
+ *   bumped only the local row's `timeUpdated`); the doc is rewritten to `deletedAt`.
+ * - only `deleted_at` set but older than `timeUpdated` (the article was saved again
+ *   after the old delete): live; the legacy key is dropped.
+ * - `deleted_at` present with a null value: live; the legacy key is dropped.
+ * - neither key: unchanged, no heal.
+ */
+internal object RemoteDeletion {
+    const val FIELD = "deletedAt"
+    const val LEGACY_FIELD = "deleted_at"
+
+    data class Resolution(val deletedAt: Long?, val timeUpdated: Long, val heal: Heal)
+
+    sealed interface Heal {
+        data object None : Heal
+        data object DropLegacy : Heal
+        data class PromoteLegacy(val deletedAt: Long, val timeUpdated: Long) : Heal
+    }
+
+    fun resolve(
+        deletedAt: Long?,
+        legacyDeletedAt: Long?,
+        legacyPresent: Boolean,
+        timeUpdated: Long,
+    ): Resolution {
+        val hasLegacyKey = legacyPresent || legacyDeletedAt != null
+        return when {
+            deletedAt != null -> Resolution(
+                deletedAt = deletedAt,
+                timeUpdated = timeUpdated,
+                heal = if (hasLegacyKey) Heal.DropLegacy else Heal.None,
+            )
+            legacyDeletedAt != null && legacyDeletedAt >= timeUpdated -> {
+                val effective = maxOf(timeUpdated, legacyDeletedAt)
+                Resolution(
+                    deletedAt = legacyDeletedAt,
+                    timeUpdated = effective,
+                    heal = Heal.PromoteLegacy(legacyDeletedAt, effective),
+                )
+            }
+            hasLegacyKey -> Resolution(
+                deletedAt = null,
+                timeUpdated = timeUpdated,
+                heal = Heal.DropLegacy,
+            )
+            else -> Resolution(deletedAt = null, timeUpdated = timeUpdated, heal = Heal.None)
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** The merge-write fields that heal a doc, or null when the doc needs no write. */
+    fun healFields(heal: Heal): Map<String, Any>? = when (heal) {
+        Heal.None -> null
+        Heal.DropLegacy -> mapOf(LEGACY_FIELD to FieldValue.delete())
+        is Heal.PromoteLegacy -> mapOf(
+            FIELD to heal.deletedAt,
+            "timeUpdated" to heal.timeUpdated,
+            LEGACY_FIELD to FieldValue.delete(),
+        )
+    }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Applies a [RemoteDeletion.Resolution] to a restored article. A doc left by the old
+ * merge-only delete of a never-backed-up article has no `itemId`; it takes the doc id.
+ */
+internal fun Article.withRemoteDeletion(r: RemoteDeletion.Resolution, docId: String): Article =
+    copy(
+        itemId = itemId.ifBlank { docId },
+        deletedAt = r.deletedAt,
+        timeUpdated = r.timeUpdated,
+    )

diff --git a/warg/workers/gcs/src/metadata.test.ts b/warg/workers/gcs/src/metadata.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..8d35fa2
--- /dev/null
+++ b/warg/workers/gcs/src/metadata.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
+import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
+import type { ReadabilityResult } from '@warg/shared';
+import { buildFinalizeMetadata } from './metadata.js';
+
+function result(overrides: Partial<ReadabilityResult> = {}): ReadabilityResult {
+  return {
+    title: 'A title',
+    byline: 'Jane Doe',
+    dir: null,
+    lang: 'en',
+    content: '<p>one two three</p>',
+    textContent: '  one two\n three  ',
+    length: 13,
+    excerpt: 'An excerpt',
+    siteName: 'Example',
+    publishedTime: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z',
+    ...overrides,
+  };
+}
+
+describe('buildFinalizeMetadata', () => {
+  it('passes lead_image through unchanged', () => {
+    const url = 'https://example.com/a.jpg';
+    expect(buildFinalizeMetadata(result({ leadImage: url })).lead_image).toBe(url);
+  });
+
+  it('maps a null leadImage to null', () => {
+    expect(buildFinalizeMetadata(result({ leadImage: null })).lead_image).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('maps a result written before leadImage existed to null', () => {
+    const old = result();
+    expect('leadImage' in old).toBe(false);
+    const metadata = buildFinalizeMetadata(old);
+    expect(metadata).toHaveProperty('lead_image');
+    expect(metadata.lead_image).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('keeps the six existing fields as before', () => {
+    expect(buildFinalizeMetadata(result())).toEqual({
+      byline: 'Jane Doe',
+      excerpt: 'An excerpt',
+      published_time: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z',
+      site_name: 'Example',
+      title: 'A title',
+      word_count: 3,
+      lead_image: null,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('uses the same defaults as before for missing values', () => {
+    expect(
+      buildFinalizeMetadata(
+        result({
+          title: null,
+          byline: null,
+          excerpt: null,
+          siteName: null,
+          publishedTime: null,
+          textContent: null,
+        })
+      )
+    ).toEqual({
+      byline: '',
+      excerpt: '',
+      published_time: null,
+      site_name: null,
+      title: '',
+      word_count: 0,
+      lead_image: null,
+    });
+  });
+});

commit c151e4843c087b6e0ba45eb5e2ce7ee8a4cd5dcd
Author: jayteealao <jayteealao@gmail.com>
Date:   Thu Oct 22 22:08:22 2026 +0100

    wip(warg): map finalize metadata in one function
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

diff --git a/warg/workers/gcs/src/metadata.ts b/warg/workers/gcs/src/metadata.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ffbb340
--- /dev/null
+++ b/warg/workers/gcs/src/metadata.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,21 @@
+import type { ReadabilityResult } from '@warg/shared';
+import type { FinalizeRequest } from './types.js';
+
+/**
+ * Maps a readability.json result to the metadata map that /finalize writes
+ * to Firestore. lead_image passes through unchanged; a result written before
+ * the field existed maps to null.
+ */
+export function buildFinalizeMetadata(
+  readability: ReadabilityResult
+): NonNullable<FinalizeRequest['metadata']> {
+  return {
+    byline: readability.byline ?? '',
+    excerpt: readability.excerpt ?? '',
+    published_time: readability.publishedTime ?? null,
+    site_name: readability.siteName ?? null,
+    title: readability.title ?? '',
+    word_count: readability.textContent?.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length ?? 0,
+    lead_image: typeof readability.leadImage === 'string' ? readability.leadImage : null,
+  };
+}

commit cb1f2926aacc0190c33a4a4b46ca76e1b16c0a36
Author: jayteealao <jayteealao@gmail.com>
Date:   Thu Oct 22 22:01:59 2026 +0100

    test(warg): cover lead image extraction cases
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

diff --git a/warg/workers/readability/src/lead-image.test.ts b/warg/workers/readability/src/lead-image.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e1543de
--- /dev/null
+++ b/warg/workers/readability/src/lead-image.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,95 @@
+import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
+import { parseHTML } from 'linkedom';
+import { Readability } from '@mozilla/readability';
+import { extractLeadImage } from './lead-image.js';
+
+const PAGE = 'https://example.com/post/1';
+
+function page(head: string): string {
+  const body = '<p>' + 'Some article text that Readability can keep. '.repeat(40) + '</p>';
+  return `<!doctype html><html><head><title>T</title>${head}</head><body><article>${body}</article></body></html>`;
+}
+
+function lead(head: string, pageUrl: string | null = PAGE): string | null {
+  return extractLeadImage(parseHTML(page(head)).document, pageUrl ?? undefined);
+}
+
+describe('extractLeadImage', () => {
+  it('returns an absolute og:image unchanged', () => {
+    expect(lead('<meta property="og:image" content="https://img.example.org/a.jpg">')).toBe(
+      'https://img.example.org/a.jpg'
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('resolves a relative og:image against the page URL', () => {
+    expect(lead('<meta property="og:image" content="/img/a.jpg">')).toBe(
+      'https://example.com/img/a.jpg'
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('resolves a protocol-relative og:image', () => {
+    expect(lead('<meta property="og:image" content="//cdn.example.com/a.jpg">')).toBe(
+      'https://cdn.example.com/a.jpg'
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('returns null for an invalid URL', () => {
+    expect(lead('<meta property="og:image" content="http://[bad">')).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('returns null when the page has no og:image', () => {
+    expect(lead('<meta name="description" content="x">')).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('returns null for a relative og:image without a page URL', () => {
+    expect(lead('<meta property="og:image" content="/img/a.jpg">', null)).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('returns null for a data: URL', () => {
+    expect(lead('<meta property="og:image" content="data:image/png;base64,AAAA">')).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('returns null for a URL longer than 2048 characters', () => {
+    const long = 'https://example.com/' + 'a'.repeat(2100);
+    expect(lead(`<meta property="og:image" content="${long}">`)).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('takes the first of several og:image tags', () => {
+    expect(
+      lead(
+        '<meta property="og:image" content="https://example.com/first.jpg">' +
+          '<meta property="og:image" content="https://example.com/second.jpg">'
+      )
+    ).toBe('https://example.com/first.jpg');
+  });
+
+  it('reads og:image from a name attribute', () => {
+    expect(lead('<meta name="og:image" content="https://example.com/n.jpg">')).toBe(
+      'https://example.com/n.jpg'
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('falls back to og:image:url', () => {
+    expect(lead('<meta property="og:image:url" content="https://example.com/u.jpg">')).toBe(
+      'https://example.com/u.jpg'
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('skips a blank og:image content', () => {
+    expect(
+      lead(
+        '<meta property="og:image" content="  ">' +
+          '<meta property="og:image" content="https://example.com/b.jpg">'
+      )
+    ).toBe('https://example.com/b.jpg');
+  });
+
+  it('gives the same value when read before Readability parses the page', () => {
+    const html = page('<meta property="og:image" content="/img/a.jpg">');
+    const { document } = parseHTML(html);
+    const before = extractLeadImage(document, PAGE);
+    new Readability(document).parse();
+    expect(before).toBe(extractLeadImage(parseHTML(html).document, PAGE));
+    expect(before).toBe('https://example.com/img/a.jpg');
+  });
+});

commit 5a43e5301cb93937131344317c24dc2c8f7964ea
Author: jayteealao <jayteealao@gmail.com>
